# HANDOFF — CodexTactics (UE 5.8 port of Operation: Cold Silence)

**Purpose.** Any agent (Claude, Gemini, …) must be able to pick up the port from this file alone.
Keep it current: every commit that adds / changes a system updates §5 (system map), §8 (next steps) and §10 (log).

Last update: 2026-09-29 by Claude, after commit `2ca14b0`.

---

## 1. Read first (in this order)

1. This file.
2. `CLAUDE.md` — project rules (Godot is read-only, no GAS, commit per verified system, ask only on real design forks).
3. `docs/port/PORT_MATRIX.md` — Godot file → UE class table with status and tests; intentional deviations.
4. `docs/port/REFERENCE_PLAYTHROUGH_01.md` — the user's reference video, the order the port follows.
5. `docs/port/TANDEM.md` — coordination log between agents (claim files before editing, one builder at a time).
6. `docs/port/ARCHITECT_SUPERVISOR_DIRECTIVE.md` — Gemini's directive; §4 lists where Godot code wins over it.

Godot source of truth: `C:/Users/Zephyrus15Duo/Documents/Codex/godot-test-01/` (READ ONLY — never edit).
The user's rule: **Godot code values win** (except explicit user decisions listed in PORT_MATRIX "deviations").

## 2. Environment facts and traps (read before touching anything)

| Fact | Consequence |
|---|---|
| Engine `C:/Program Files/Epic Games/UE_5.8`, project `Documents/Unreal Projects/CodexTactics` | Scripts assume these paths. |
| **`Documents/Codex/unreal` is a junction to this repo** | Another agent working "in Codex/unreal" works on the *same files*. Builds / tests collide (DLL locks, overwritten `Saved/Logs`). Claim work in TANDEM and build one at a time. |
| The open **Unreal Editor locks the module DLLs** | Close it before `build.ps1` (graceful: `CloseMainWindow`, wait 45 s; never force-kill a window with a save dialog). The user allowed closing / reopening it. |
| Build log `Saved/Logs/Build.log` is UTF-16-ish | Read it with PowerShell `Get-Content`, not grep. |
| UHT forbids names shadowing `AActor` members (`Role`, `Instigator`, …) | Use `SquadRole`, `InstigatorName`. MSVC C4458 is an error here too. |
| `unreal.log()` in `-run=pythonscript` commandlets does not reach stdout | Editor scripts write their result to `Saved/Logs/*.txt` (see `add_level_objects_to_movement_test.py`). |
| Editing a Blueprint's inherited component via Python CDO | Use `set_editor_property` on the component template; `set_relative_transform` is NOT persisted. |
| Git LFS for `.uasset/.umap` (no `lockable`) | `lockable` made maps read-only; keep it off. |
| The user re-laid `L_MovementTest` in the editor (whole layout rotated 180° and moved to Floor (-800, 1130); PlayerStart (-180, -1490)) — user decision 2026-09-29: keep it | Smokes write points in the original design coordinates (map script, Floor at the origin, yaw 0) and map them with `SmokeUtils::LevelPoint` / `LayoutTransform` (the "Floor" actor's location + yaw); `PlaceSquadAtTestStart` puts the squad at the design origin facing design +X. Never hard-code world coordinates in a smoke. |
| A saved `RecastNavMesh-Default` that is rotated / not tile-aligned stays empty at runtime ("Recreating dtNavMesh instance … not aligned with tile size", then nothing is built) | The map ships without a RecastNavMesh actor; it is created and built at load (RuntimeGeneration=Dynamic). A commandlet that loads and saves the map re-creates an EMPTY one that also stays empty at runtime: editor scripts must destroy `RecastNavMesh` actors before saving (see add_level_objects_to_movement_test.py). If smokes suddenly cannot move the squad ("leader on navmesh=0"), check this first. |
| Unity builds merge .cpp files | Anonymous-namespace names collide across files (`PanelColor`, `Clean`…): prefix file-local helpers (`Menu…`, `Loot…`). Names like `FItemInfo` can also clash with engine types. |
| Default canvas / Slate fonts have no emoji | HUD / menu strip them (`ACodexTacticsHUD::StripUnsupportedGlyphs`); texts stay verbatim Godot with emoji in code. |
| CommonUI applies a default "Menu" input config to any activated screen unless `bEnableDefaultInputConfig=False` (DefaultGame.ini) — it would block the game's keys after the pause menu closed | Keep it False; a screen that wants an input mode implements "Get Desired Input Config" in its WBP. Screens are pooled by their stack: reset per-show state in `NativeOnActivated` / the push's init function, not the constructor. |
| Bash heredocs with long / complex Python sometimes break in this harness | Write the Python to the scratchpad with the file tool and run `python <file>`. |

- **Holding a montage on its last frame:** clear `bEnableAutoBlendOut` on the running instance (`GetActiveInstanceForMontage(Montage)->bEnableAutoBlendOut = false`); the instance copies the montage's flag when it starts, so changing the montage after `PlaySlotAnimationAsDynamicMontage` has no effect.
- **Blend spaces made by script** (`UOperativeAnimGraphLibrary::FillDirectionalBlendSpace`) need `ValidateSampleData` + `ResampleData`; `PostEditChange()` alone leaves them empty and the player outputs the reference pose (T-pose height) with no error.

- **Two agents, one checkout:** set `$env:CODEX_AGENT` (claude / gemini); every build / test / smoke / verify_all takes `Saved/agent.lock` and waits for the other agent (`Scripts/agent_lock.ps1 -Status` shows the holder). Roles and file ownership: `docs/port/TANDEM.md`.
## 3. Build, test, verify

**Unreal MCP (AI access to the open editor):** plugins ModelContextProtocol + toolsets are enabled and the server
auto-starts with the CodexTactics editor at `http://127.0.0.1:8000/mcp` (`.mcp.json` server `unreal`). A Claude
session connects at its start, so open the editor first. Tools: `list_toolsets`, `describe_toolset`, `call_tool`;
project toolset `CodexTacticsEditor.BlueprintGraphToolset` (read any Blueprint / AnimBP graph as text, paste nodes,
compile; pasted changes are never saved automatically). An open CodexTactics editor locks the DLLs: close it before
`build.ps1` / `verify_all.ps1` (another project's editor may stay open). Note: ABP_Operative's AnimGraph holds only the
Output Pose — the pose is blended natively in `UOperativeAnimInstance`.

```
powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File Scripts/verify_all.ps1              # build + all tests + all smokes (smoke_parallel.ps1: min(4, cores/4) at a time, -Parallel N / -Serial; shared-file checks and parallel failures re-run alone; logs Saved/Logs/Smoke-<Name>.log)
powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File Scripts/build.ps1
powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File Scripts/test.ps1 [-Filter CodexTactics.Cold]
powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File Scripts/smoke.ps1 -Command CodexTactics.DeployableSmoke
```

### Verification tiers (2026-10-07; impact map `Scripts/test_map.json`)

`Scripts/test_map.json` maps changed paths (globs) -> unit-test filters + smokes (every smoke of `verify_all` is registered and mapped;
`test.ps1 -CheckMap` fails if a new `Debug/*Smoke*Command.cpp` is not). Add a rule / registry entry whenever you add a smoke or a system.

| Tier | When | Command |
|---|---|---|
| edit | after each change | `build.ps1`, then `test.ps1 -Changed -Quick` (one unit run with the combined filter + the 1-2 key smokes per rule, in parallel) |
| pre-commit | before the commit | `test.ps1 -Changed` (all mapped smokes) + `test.ps1` (all unit tests); `typesafe_triage.py --audit-diff --agent claude` |
| nightly / milestone | editor closed, big merges | `verify_all.ps1` (build + all tests + all smokes in parallel; `-Serial` for the old one-by-one run) |

- `test.ps1 -Changed [-Quick] [-Base <ref>] [-DryRun] [-SkipUnit|-SkipSmokes] [-Jobs N] [-Serial]`: files = `git diff --name-only <Base>` + untracked. `-DryRun` prints the plan only. `-Smart` = `-Changed` (the map decides; `-Jev` adds Jev's include-based unit filter as a hint).
- `smoke_parallel.ps1 -Commands a,b,c|all [-Jobs N] [-Serial]`: each smoke is its own UnrealEditor-Cmd with its own log `Saved/Logs/Smoke-<Name>.log`; the build lock is taken once for the batch. Smokes touching shared files (`SaveLoadSmoke`, `PauseMenuSmoke` -> `Saved/SmokeSaves`; `EventBusSmoke` -> `Saved/SaveGames`) are `exclusive` in the map and run alone after the batch; a parallel failure is re-run alone once (`PASS*` = passed only on the re-run, i.e. suspect flakiness). `knownFail` smokes (now `CombatMoveSmoke`) are shown as KNOWN-FAIL without failing the run. Default jobs = min(4, cores/4).
- Unmapped `Source/` paths fall back to the core smokes (`fallback` in the map); docs / scripts / `.claude` need nothing.
- Measured 2026-10-07 (16 cores, 4 jobs): `verify_all -SkipBuild` = 268 unit tests in 46 s + 91 smokes in 24.4 min wall (sum of checks 81.5 min; ~3.3x); serial smokes average ~54 s each (~82 min for all). Same per-smoke verdicts serial and parallel.
- The host re-checks only the build + one key smoke when the agent already ran the mapped set (`test.ps1 -Changed`) on the same tree.

State at last update (2026-10-06, cover facing / hearing / dialogue cold): **239 automation tests PASS**; older note: 145 automation tests, 56 smokes, all PASS (`verify_all.ps1` → ALL GREEN; it also fails on an engine crash during the tests now).

Uses `BP_APC_BTR82` when it exists, else the C++ class (vendor mesh by soft path) |

| `PatrolAmbushSmoke` | 2026-10-11 (user bug + guard request), map **L_PatrolTest**: a hound (24 m to the side) and a marksman (28 m ahead) without routes, facing away, treated as placed in the map (`MarkLevelEnemy`): both GUARD their post (`IsGuardingPost`); auto = ambush level; forced «button»; 6 s: no drift (< 60 cm), no fight, no damage to the squad; a trap next to the hound -> it searches (marksman out of radius keeps guarding), no fight, then it walks back to its post; a squad gunshot -> both hear it, leave their posts and start the real-time ambush fight once (`PatrolDetection`; no cutscene / preparation / horde intro), both are the wave, the Aggressive squad returns fire, no game over |
| `VisionConeSmoke [Shot]` | 2026-10-11, map **L_PatrolTest**: every operative / enemy has a `VisionCone` on its capsule, hidden by default; `bShowVisionCone` on the leader / a guarding brute shows only theirs; operative cone = weapon range x stance (M16 2500 cm), +-35 deg, 34 fill vertices, at the feet +8 cm, red with the brute in it (Passive squad); brute cone = its perception (1800 cm / +-45 deg), idle colour; `Codex.Debug.VisionCones` 0 hides all, 1 shows all, -1 back; trap -> searching brute: amber, range x1.25; no fight. `Shot` (rendered, `-RenderOffscreen`): all cones on, camera over the squad, `Saved/Screenshots/<platform>/vision_cones.png` |
| `EncounterProbe [seconds] [exit]` | dev probe: once a second `[Probe]` lines with the flow phase / combat mode, encounter mode (ambush level, patrols, placed enemies, ambush starts), every enemy (state, patrol, target, hp, placed) and operative (posture, hp, target) |
| `FinishPrep` | dev: skip preparation, start the wave |

Parity tests live in `Source/CodexTacticsTests/Private/<System>/` named `CodexTactics.<System>.<Case>`; they mirror
Godot numbers (same inputs → same outputs). Pure rules are kept in `*Rules` namespaces / structs so they are testable.

## 4. Conventions

- C++ first, Blueprints for look / tuning only. **Characters are Blueprints the user owns** (mesh, AnimBP, collision):
  C++ exposes EditDefaultsOnly properties + BlueprintImplementableEvents, never hardcodes visuals a BP cannot override.
- Each class header names its Godot reference file / functions. Epic coding standard (A/U/F/E/I, TObjectPtr, IWYU).
- Player-facing texts: **verbatim from Godot** (Russian, emoji included) in `LOCTEXT`.
- Colours from Godot are sRGB: use `FLinearColor::FromSRGBColor(FColor(...))`.
- Units: Godot metres × 100 = cm; Godot `+Y up` → UE `+Z up`.
- Commits: Conventional Commits, body references the Godot file(s), `Co-Authored-By` trailer. Only verified work.
- After each system: update PORT_MATRIX row, TANDEM log, this file (§5, §8, §10).

## 5. System map (UE → Godot)

Module `CodexTactics` (runtime). Folder → class → Godot reference.

**Core**
- `Core/CodexTacticsGameMode` — squad spawn from `BP_Operative` (roster: name, colour, offset, fortitude, role, luck),
  HUD class, deployable spawn classes. Godot `main.gd` bootstrap.
- `Core/CodexTacticsPlayerController` — input (Enhanced Input created at runtime): LMB click (select / object / move;
  double click = sprint), 1–3 select, Z/C/V stance, B solo, Q/E + RMB drag rotate, MMB pan, wheel zoom (BindKey),
  Space tap / hold, R rotate placement, **F set-up from supply**. Placement mode: LMB confirm, RMB cancel, wheel rotate.
- `Core/CodexTacticsGameState`.

**Game flow / combat (partly Gemini)**
- `GameFlow/*` — phases Exploration → Cutscene → Preparation → WaveCombat (RealTime / TacticalPause / TurnBased) →
  WaveCleared / PostCombat / GameOver; time dilation 0.02 in the pause. Godot `main.gd` flags.
  **Sudden horde start (user decision + feedback 2026-10-11, default):** `FGameFlowConfig::bSuddenWaveStart` (+
  `PreparedWaveIndices` from the level JSON per-wave `"sudden_start": false`, `LevelFlowRules::ApplyLevel`) — `TriggerCombatZone` /
  `AdvanceAfterWave` go straight to `WaveCombat / RealTime` with the horde intro armed (`IsAwaitingHordeIntro`; no cutscene, no
  preparation / rest countdown): the wave spawns at once into the running fight. `EnterFreeTacticalPause` (the intro) opens the
  **free pause** (`IsFreePause()`: no charge, no timer, ends like any pause — Space tap / hold); a Space tap before the trigger
  opens it at once; `CancelHordeIntro` keeps real time (smokes, the bot). Console override `Codex.HordeIntro.SuddenStart -1|0|1`.
  A loaded save resumes in the wave's preparation and the next flow tick starts it suddenly. Ambush fights are unchanged (no
  horde intro). **Level enemies always start the fight (user bug 2026-10-11):** `ULevelEncounterSubsystem` records the enemies
  placed in the map at `OnWorldBeginPlay` (`IsLevelEnemy`, `LevelHasPlacedEnemies`; `MarkLevelEnemy` for smokes); "auto" is an
  ambush level when the map holds patrols OR any placed enemy; and on every level, even "button", any hostile contact with a
  placed enemy in exploration — it attacks an operative (`AEnemyCharacter::NotifySquadAttacked` from the blow, spit, pounce and
  the marksman's shot -> `EAmbushTrigger::EnemyAttack`), spots the squad, or is hit — starts the ambush fight
  (`LevelEncounterRules::ShouldStartFightOnContact`). Runtime / wave spawns on a button level do not. **Guard posts (user
  request 2026-10-11):** a placed enemy without a route of its own (spline / legacy marksman points) and without an escort
  leader guards its placement (`AEnemyCharacter::TryStartGuardPost` at BeginPlay / `MarkLevelEnemy`;
  `PatrolRouteRules::ShouldGuardPost`; per actor `bGuardPostWhenUnrouted`, default on): a patrol without a route - stands,
  faces its placement yaw with a look-around sweep (`GuardLookAroundDeg` 45 / `GuardLookPeriodSeconds` 12,
  `GetGuardLookOffset`), walks back after a search / shove (`ShouldWalkBackToPost`, 2 m / 0.8 m), uses the patrol
  perception (sight + suspicion, hearing by gait / gunshot / grenade, hound smell; trap -> search 60 s -> back to the post)
  and `BreakPatrol` -> the normal ambush flow. The marksman guards in `EMarksmanAIState::Patrol` (no more Engage at
  BeginPlay without a route - Sprint 06-D's rule now only for wave / runtime spawns); an escort mirrors its guarding
  leader (stays tethered). Wave / runtime spawns hunt as before. The
  squad gets the old warm / healed start and its post-combat stations at the wave-1 spawn (`UMissionSubsystem`, `UWaveVictorySubsystem`).
- `Combat/HordeIntroRules` + `HordeIntroSubsystem` (2026-10-11, reworked after the user's review) — timeline: (1) spawn, the fight
  runs in real time (`PreTriggerTimeDilation` 1), camera on the squad, normal RTS control (orders work; Esc / Space open the free
  pause at once, a click is a normal click); (2) trigger once the horde is a running mass: `MassMovingFraction` 0.6 of the live
  enemies run at the squad (>= `MassMinSpeed` 250 cm/s, heading within 60 deg) for `MassHoldSeconds` 0.4 (latched) and not before
  `MinDelaySeconds` 2.5 s, `MaxWaitSeconds` 6 s at the latest; (3) the free pause opens, the «HORDE INCOMING / TACTICAL PAUSE - GIVE
  YOUR ORDERS» alert (`UI/HordeAlertWidget`, 3 s + fade, restylable WBP subclass via `AlertWidgetClass`) and a slow-motion flyover
  on real time at `FlyoverTimeDilation` 0.2: wide shot of the running mass (moving anchor 80 % towards it, 20..40 m, seen from the
  squad's side turned 25 deg: `ATacticalCameraPawn::SetCinematicYaw`, 3 s), a close tracking shot (7 m, 2.5 s) of a running
  front-runner per type not shown in this fight (spawn order), the glide back to the leader (1 s, player yaw restored); (4) the
  world settles into the free pause (0.02). Known types only: a 2.5 s wide shot + return. Esc / click skip the flyover (camera
  snaps to the leader, free pause). Camera input locked meanwhile, camera zones step aside, the line of sight shows the whole
  horde (`UTacticalSightSubsystem::SetCinematicReveal`). Hooks for a later HUD effect: `OnHordeIntroStarted(Wave, bFlyover)` /
  `OnHordeIntroFinished(Wave, bSkipped)` (BlueprintAssignable). The bot orders its defences and dismisses the intro (real time).
- `Combat/SpaceInput`, `EncounterQueries`, `HealthComponent` (armour, elements, `ApplyDirectHealthLoss`),
  `WaveSubsystem`, `EnemySpawnPoint` (Gemini). `Characters/EnemyCharacter` + `EnemyAIController` (Gemini, tag `Enemy`).
- `Tactics/GorkyGridManager`, `Gorky17Types` — grid, arc zones, AP BFS, A* (Gemini). Turn-based manager NOT yet.
- `Data/CombatTypes`, `WeaponDataAsset`, `EnemyArchetypeAsset`, `WaveConfigTypes` (Gemini; values hand-typed — see §9).

**Squad / characters**
- `Characters/OperativeCharacter` — orders, stances (capsule per stance, placeholder body until a skeletal mesh),
  sprint rules, shooting (Gemini) with cold misfire / aim penalty, inventory (matches 3, grenades 2/4, turrets /
  barricades / mines with limits 2/4/5), role (Commander / Engineer / MedicSapper) + luck (25/30/35),
  carrying (`SetCarrying`), lift limits (80 % cold, 50 % HP), placement radius 15 m. Godot `player.gd`.
- `Characters/OperativeAnimInstance` — state for AnimBP + native locomotion blend (proxy `Evaluate`) until the user's
  graph exists (`bUseNativeLocomotion`). Godot `locomotion_controller.gd`.
- Ctrl + click targeted shots: `ACodexTacticsPlayerController::IssueTargetedShot` (Godot `main.gd` Ctrl branch:
  enemy → `SetManualPriorityTarget`, barrel / mine / crate / trapped object → `AOperativeCharacter::ShootAtObject`;
  in the tactical pause → `PlanTargetedShot`, run by `USquadSubsystem` on release before the planned moves).
  `Combat/TargetedShotRules` = mine hit chance clamp(max(10, Accuracy − cold·0.25)·stance − m·perMetre, 0, 95),
  stance 0.6/4.0, 1.0/1.8, 1.25/1.0; miss reasons. `Accuracy` per role 90 / 75 / 85 (roster). The priority target is
  used first by `FindBestCombatTarget` while alive, in range and in the line of fire (barricade blocks prone only).
  Godot `player.gd shoot_at_*`, `calculate_mine_shot_hit_chance`, `set_manual_priority_target`, `_find_shoot_target`.
- `Combat/CombatFeedbackSubsystem` + `CombatFeedbackActor` — shot tracers with muzzle flash (operative: weapon
  `TracerColor`, glow / fade by damage type, misses deflected; turret: green), cyan plan markers of the tactical pause
  (planned moves, interaction approach, relocation / deploy targets, targeted shots; cleared on every combat-mode
  change), Ctrl + click target flash (red light + glowing overlay on the target's meshes). Glow material
  `/Game/VFX/Materials/M_CombatFeedback` (unlit additive, `Color` × `Intensity`) from
  `Scripts/Editor/create_feedback_material.py`. Effects run on world time (slow down in the pause, as Godot tweens).
  Godot `_spawn_muzzle_tracer` (player / turret), `_spawn_waypoint_marker`, `_clear_planned_markers`,
  `_highlight_target_feedback`.
- `Characters/SquadSubsystem`, `SquadFormation`, `OperativeMovementRules`, `OperativeAIController`.
- Assets: `/Game/Characters/Operatives/BP_Operative`, `ABP_Operative`, `Explorer/…`, `Animations/…`, `/Game/Weapons/M16`.

**Vision cones (user request 2026-10-11)** — `Combat/VisionConeRules` (pure: sector fill / outline geometry, inside test,
`ResolveVisible`, enemy state + colours, colour lerp; tests `CodexTactics.Combat.VisionCone.*`) + `Combat/VisionConeComponent`
(`UProceduralMeshComponent`; driven by its owner's tick through `UpdateCone`; `ConeMaterial` slot, fallback engine
`/Engine/EngineDebugMaterials/M_SimpleUnlitTranslucent` tinted through its `Color` vector parameter (rgb + a = opacity);
console `Codex.Debug.VisionCones` -1 / 0 / 1). `AOperativeCharacter::VisionCone` (range = weapon range x stance
multiplier, +-35 deg = Godot 70 deg full, green / red when an enemy is in it or a combat / priority target exists - Godot
player.gd `_is_any_enemy_spotted`) and `AEnemyCharacter::VisionCone` (Godot had no enemy cones: range = perception sight
range for a standing operative, x1.25 while searching; half angle = `SightHalfAngleDeg`; colour idle blue / suspicious amber
by the suspicion meter / searching / alert red). Godot: Scenes/movements/player.gd `_setup_vision_cone`,
`_rebuild_vision_cone_mesh`, `_update_vision_cone`.

**Camera** — `Camera/TacticalCameraPawn` (+Rules), `CameraZoneVolume`. Godot `camera.gd`, `camera_zone_trigger.gd`.

**Survival** — `Survival/ColdRules`, `ColdSurvivalComponent`; `Interactables/HeatSourceComponent`. Godot
`player.gd _process_cold_system`, `warm_zone.gd`.
**Mobile & tactical heat (Sprint 15, TANDEM #14, 2026-10-11, UE-only)** — every source warms through `UHeatSourceComponent`
(`UColdSurvivalComponent::IsNearHeatSource`: warming, warm regeneration, weapon thaw; the beasts' existing fire fear of any active
heat source applies too). Pure `Survival/TacticalHeatRules` (`FTacticalHeatConfig` = Gemini's spec values, `FHeatBurnState`, heat
pack / buffer, campfire wind + craft, puddle zones, `ShouldAdvanceRealTime`) read from `Content/Data/Survival/tactical_heat.json`
(`Data/TacticalHeatTuning`, loaded at StartPlay; `CodexTactics.DumpTacticalHeat`). `UTacticalHeatTimerComponent` (burn time / gas) is
advanced only by `Survival/UTacticalHeatSubsystem`: real time when not in the tactical pause (incl. the free horde pause and its
flyover), not turn-based and not under a dialogue / cutscene (`UWorldAIPauseSubsystem`); one turn-based squad round
(`UTurnBasedCombatSubsystem::OnRoundAdvanced`) burns total / rounds. The subsystem also runs the heat pack (`UseHeatPack`: self or a
mate, 1 AP in turn-based), the flare reveal (`IsRevealedByFlare`, used by `UTacticalSightSubsystem`) and the save of loose sources.
- Heat pack: `EPersonalItem::HeatPack`, `AOperativeCharacter::HeatPacksCount` (stack 3), U / drawer line; dragged onto a cold mate
  (`USquadTransferSubsystem::Execute`, `ACodexTacticsHUD::HandleTransferRequest`) it is used on him; `UColdSurvivalComponent::ApplyHeatPack`
  (-35 points, frostbite cleared -> crouch, 45 s buffer: `FColdEnvironment::AccumulationMultiplier` 0.5).
- Campfire: `Interactables/ACampfireActor` (`EDeployableType::Campfire`; count = what matches + `FirewoodCount` (or 10 flamethrower
  fuel) make), F cycle / drawer, kindled by `OnDeployedBy`; ashes can be rekindled from the menu; patrols within 15 m search
  (`AlertPatrols`, every 10 s); wind on high ground (base Z >= 140) -20 % burn time.
- Portable gas stove: `Interactables/APortableStoveActor` (`EDeployableType::PortableStove`, max 1 each, `StoveGasSeconds` travels
  with the item: `AOperativeCharacter::MoveDeployableTo`, `OnDeployedBy` / `OnPickedUpBy`); «Pack up» = the deployable pick-up
  (1.5 s, into the packer's inventory). Silent, no smoke, windproof; not auto-recovered after a wave.
- Signal flare: `Combat/ASignalFlareActor` (base `Survival/ATacticalHeatActor`), `FlaresCount`; Shift + G / the weapon selector's 5th
  line / drawer: `UGrenadeSubsystem::StartFlareAim` (grenade rings and arc, 20 m standing / 15 m crouched), `ThrowFlareAt` (throw clip),
  a click within 2 m = `LightFlareAtFeet`. Refused in turn-based combat.
- Fire puddle: `Combat/AFirePuddleActor` (core 1.2 m: Fire + Burning pulses every 0.5 s; ring 3.5 m: `UHeatSourceComponent::WarmthRate`
  12 %/s, `FColdEnvironment::WarmthRateOverride`), 12-15 s / 1 round; `ABarrelActor::Explode` / `IgniteForTurnBased` leave one.
- Visual slots: placeholder engine shapes + point lights; Niagara slots load `/Game/VFX/Heat/NS_Campfire|NS_PortableStove|NS_SignalFlare|NS_FirePuddle`
  when Gemini creates them (TANDEM request #15); game-mode classes `CampfireClass` / `PortableStoveClass` / `SignalFlareClass` / `FirePuddleClass`.

**Interaction / world objects**
- `Interactables/InteractionSubsystem` — click → approach → `BuildActionMenu` → menu or feed line; confirm /
  relocate / trap / cancel. Mine "approaching defuser" mark. Godot `main.gd _trigger_menu_for_object`,
  `_open_action_menu`, `_on_action_confirmed`, `_on_relocate_confirmed`, `_on_trap_confirmed`.
- `Interactables/ActionMenuTypes` (`FActionMenuSpec`, `FActionMenuRequest`), `UI/ActionMenuWidget` (UMG built in C++,
  restylable via a WBP with the same widget names), owned by `UI/CodexTacticsHUD`.
- `Interactables/InteractableActor` — base: quest objects (menus per quest state), **traps on any object**
  (`bTrapped`, defusal attempt on confirm, `TrapWithGrenade`, `DetonateTrap`, `ApplyBlast`). Godot `interactable.gd`.
- `Interactables/BarrelActor` + `BarrelRules` — match, 35 s fire, fade 7 s, heat 5.5 m, charred, `Explode` (trap / shot).
- `Interactables/RelocationSubsystem` + `RelocationRules` + `RelocationGhostActor` — move objects (ghost, radius,
  push task, pause plans, combat / mine drop) **and set-up from supply** (F, two-click placement, deploy tasks,
  mine mishap). Godot `main.gd` relocation + placement blocks.
- `Interactables/DeployableActor` (+ `DeployableRules`) — pick-up / defuse / relocate menus, 1.1 s dismantle, role
  routing. `BarricadeActor` (200 HP, tripwire on enemy contact 1.8 m). `ProximityMineActor` (trigger 1.6 m, blast
  120 / 3.5 m, arming 3 s for squad mines, hidden level mines spotted at 4.5 m / sapper 6 m).
  Godot `deployables/barricade.gd`, `mine.gd`.
- `Interactables/LootCrateActor` + `LootRules` (`FLootContents`, `ELootItem`, `FLootEntry`) — supply crate: intact →
  `HandleDirectInteraction` opens the lid (1.8 s) and the loot dialog, trapped → defusal menu (2 s crouched work),
  detonation burns the contents, enemy contact 1.8 m, tiers Standard / Maximal, Godot colours. Items go to the
  leader: provisions / matches / M16 reserve / deployables (no limit, as Godot) / `ExtraAmmo` for weapons not ported /
  `BonusItems`. `UInteractionSubsystem::OpenLootDialog / LootItem / LootAll / CloseLootDialog`,
  `UI/LootDialogWidget` (+ `ULootEntryButton`). Godot `loot_crate.gd`, `loot_dialog.gd`, `main.gd` loot handlers.
- `Interactables/TurretActor` — powered by default; fires at the nearest visible enemy (≤ 12 m, 16 dmg / 0.45 s,
  barricade in the line of fire = 60 %, walls block); 120 HP, breaks at 0 (repair engineer 2 s / others 4 s);
  menus broken / unpowered («Закрыть») / trapped / pick-up; powered turret is a heat source (4.5 m, Godot
  heat_sources); `SetAllPowered`. Deploy / pick-up routed to the commander first. Godot `deployables/turret.gd`.
- Generator damage on `AInteractableActor` (Generator type): `TakeGeneratorDamage` (200 HP) → breakdown (heat off,
  turrets unpowered, «[АВАРИЯ]» menu), repair on confirm (engineer 2.5 s / others 5 s) → `RepairGenerator`.
  Starting the generator also powers all turrets. Godot `interactable.gd breakdown_generator / repair_generator`.
- `Quests/QuestChain`, `QuestSubsystem`, `Interactables/GateActor`. Godot `quest_manager.gd`, `gate.gd`.
  **Blast gate tied to the generator (user request 2026-10-11, default):** `Quests/GateProgressRules` (pure) + `AGateActor`. Level JSON
  `gate_open_mode` = `generator_waves` (default) | `instant` (the old Godot gate: the terminal opens it). Paced: the terminal only
  unlocks the servos (`UnlockProgress`; quest line «the leaves barely move», objective «Hold the line until the blast door opens»);
  during the wave fight (WaveCombat, real time / tactical pause in game time; not the turn-based grid) the door creeps by
  `gate_progress_per_second` (0.004 = 0.4 %/s) ONLY while the generator works (quest running and no generator broken); a breakdown
  pauses it (never reverses); each wave may push it to `gate_max_progress_before_final` (0.9) x wave / final wave (3 waves: 30 / 60
  / 90 %); it opens fully only when `gate_full_open_after_wave` (0 = the level's last wave) is cleared (latched `IsFinalWaveCleared`)
  AND the generator works (down at that moment -> it waits for the repair). Before that the door stays VISUALLY CLOSED
  (user decision 2026-10-11: no leaf creep; `MaxCrackDistance` removed) — the collision boxes and the navmesh block stay closed. A
  rotating amber warning beacon (`BeaconPivot` / `BeaconMesh` / `BeaconLight`, editable `BeaconOffset`, `BeaconSpinSpeed`,
  `BeaconMaterial`, colour / intensity / radius) spins with its light on while the servos are unlocked and the generator works
  (`GateProgressRules::ShouldBeaconSpin`), stops (light off) when the generator is down, spins during the final slide and is off
  once open.
  HUD: a line under the fire-posture badge + a world label above the gate («BLAST DOOR 37% — GENERATOR OFFLINE», «… — OPENING
  SLOWLY», «… — GRID OVERLOADED, HOLD THE WAVE», «BLAST DOOR OPENING — GET INSIDE»). HQ feed lines on: unlock («power grid is
  overloaded…»), wave cap reached, generator down, power back, last wave cleared without power, opening. Console:
  `Codex.Gate.OpenMode -1|0|1`, `Codex.Gate.ProgressPerSecond`. «Start Battle» (`CompleteChainForCombat`) still opens the gate at
  once (the squad is placed behind it at `CombatStart`, the waves spawn on the near side). Saved: `world_state.gate_progress`
  {progress, final_wave_cleared} per gate (generator health / broken were already saved with the interactables).

**Turn-based combat** — `Tactics/TurnBasedCombatSubsystem` (starts when the flow enters TurnBased, ends on victory /
defeat / leaving): 14 x 14 grid (1.5 m) around the leader, floor traced under it, static colliders baked as obstacles,
barricades / barrels / mines / turrets registered, everything else frozen (enemy / turret / mine ticks off, wave
spawning paused in `UWaveSubsystem::Tick`). Squad round: 8 AP each — move 1 / diagonal 2 (reachable BFS, a mine on the
path stops the walk and blows), stance 1, turn 90° 1, one attack 3 (fire lane, LoS, hit chance with stance bonus, arc
damage − armour, barrel = 3 x 3 blast + 3 burning rounds with a 5 x 5 fire-fear area), Tab next operative, Enter
end the squad turn. Turret phase (nearest enemy ≤ 45 m, fallback curve, 25 dmg). Enemy phase: nearest operative, path
to an orthogonal neighbour (fire-fear aware), bite for 2 AP after a 1.3 s yellow warning (arc x stance multiplier),
step back. Steps animate 0.52 s (enemy 0.48 s, x1.414 diagonal). Victory → «🏆 ПОБЕДА…», flow → tactical pause.
`UI/TurnBasedHudWidget` (bottom-right panel «ХОД ОТРЯДА» / «ХОД ПРОТИВНИКА», unit, AP / HP, end squad turn, next
operative, stance, turn; barrel push disabled — Godot `gorky17_combat_hud.gd`). `Tactics/TurnGridOverlayActor` (instanced glow tiles: grid, reachable, attack targets, enemy reach, active, warning,
fear). Controller: clicks go to `HandleWorldClick`; 1..3 / Z C V / R / Tab / Enter as above. Grid manager gained
`FindPathToAdjacent` / `FindPathClosestOutsideForbidden`; barrels `IgniteForTurnBased` / `ExtinguishNow`.

**Phase banners** — `UI/PhaseBannersWidget`: pause banner «РЕЖИМ ПРИКАЗОВ | Зарядов… | Время планирования…» (the free
pause of a sudden wave: «CONTACT - free pause, charges N/M kept» + the flyover caption «HOSTILE: <type> | [Esc / click] skip»),
combat banner «ПОДГОТОВКА К БОЮ: NN сек» + «Начать бой» (`FinishPreparation`) / «ВОЛНА N | ВРАГОВ ОСТАЛОСЬ: M»,
pre-combat cutscene card (black screen, countdown; click or Space skips = `FinishCutscene`). The mission subsystem
resets the squad (cold 0, full health, stop) when the cutscene ends (Godot `_end_cutscene_and_start_pause`).

**Action bar** — `UI/ActionBarWidget` (Godot TacticalBar, bottom centre): «ИНВ» (inventory drawer; Sprint 13 retired
«ПЕРЕД» — hand-overs are dragged from the drawer onto a model or a squad slot), weapon slot «M16 [clip/reserve] | [G] Граната» («Перезарядка...»), «ПЕР» (object-pick mode:
`ACodexTacticsPlayerController::ToggleRelocateSelectMode`, next object click → `StartRelocate`; «АКТИВ» while
picking / placing), stance letter С / П / Л (click → `CycleLeaderStance`), «ОБОР» (guard — disabled until ported),
squad slots [1] КОМ [2] ИНЖ [3] МЕД + HP / cold bars, [4] РЕЗ locked. Hidden while the start menu is open. Godot
`main.gd _update_tactical_command_bar`, `_cycle_leader_stance`, `_on_relocate_slot_clicked`.

**Dialogues** — `Data/DialogueSequenceAsset` (lines: speaker, text, delay) imported from the Godot .tres by
`Scripts/Editor/import_dialogues.py` into `/Game/Data/Dialogues/DA_*` (intro, prep, wave rest, victory, Susanin
recruitment) — re-run it when Godot texts change, never hand-edit. `UI/DialogueSubsystem`: `StartDialogue` = bottom
window `UI/DialogueWidget` (speaker card, [N / M], «Пропустить» / «Далее»; Space / Enter / click next, Esc skip, world
orders blocked), `PlayInFeed` = timed lines in the message feed. `UI/DialogueRules` (speaker card, button texts).
Game mode soft refs `DialogueMissionStart / PreparationStarted / WaveRest / Victory`. Mission hooks: start (Game /
Exploration: intro window; headless starts get the radio line instead), first preparation (prep), later preparations
(wave rest), PostCombat (victory, then objective «РУБЕЖ ЗАЧИЩЕН: Исследуйте…» + HQ line). Portraits: Godot emoji have
no UE font glyphs → role tags КОМ / ИНЖ / МЕД / ЖИТ / ?. Godot `bottom_dialogue_dialog.gd`, `main.gd play_dialogue`.

**Frontend (2026-10-08 user decision; architecture modelled on a CommonUI frontend, own implementation)** —
`Source/CodexTactics/*/UI/Frontend/`, plugin **CommonUI** (+ CommonInput; `CommonGameViewportClient` in DefaultEngine.ini,
input data / controller data in DefaultGame.ini, `bEnableDefaultInputConfig=False` so screens never switch the game's
input mode). Pieces: `UCodexPrimaryLayout` (one `UCommonActivatableWidgetStack` per layer tag `Codex.UI.Layer.Game /
GameMenu / Menu / Modal`, z-order 100 above the HUD, never hit-testable itself); `UCodexUISubsystem` (GameInstance:
`GetOrCreateLayout`, `PushScreen(LayerTag, ScreenTag, Init, OnPushed)` — soft classes from the settings, async-loaded
(all preloaded when a layout is created), C++ fallback class when a WBP is missing; `ShowConfirm`, `FindScreen`,
`GetTopScreen`, `ClearLayer`, `HandleBackOnTopScreen`); `UCodexFrontendSettings` (Project Settings > Game > Codex
Frontend: screen tag -> WBP, frontend / new-game level, camera blend time / function / exponent, title anchor id);
`UCodexActivatableScreen` (base: placeholder tree via `ICodexDefaultTree::BuildDefaultTree`, widgets found by name,
`UCodexMenuButton` entries — hover / focus = select (description line), click = activate, entry `Back` = back action);
`UCodexMenuButton` (CommonButtonBase: label, description, EntryId, CameraAnchorId); screens `UCodexTitleScreen`
(any key), `UCodexMainMenuScreen`, `UCodexSaveSlotsScreen` (+ `UCodexSaveSlotEntry`; save / load / delete, overwrite
confirm), `UCodexOptionsScreen` (placeholder), `UCodexCreditsScreen`, `UCodexPauseMenuScreen`, `UCodexConfirmDialog`
(Yes / No or OK, blur + dim); `UCodexFrontendSubsystem` (NEW GAME / CONTINUE / LOAD / QUIT TO MAIN MENU / QUIT GAME);
`CodexSaveBridge` (the only place touching `USaveGameSubsystem`: `CanSaveNow`, `LoadGameWithTravel`, `GetContinueSlot`,
slot list); menu map actors `ACodexMenuCameraAnchor` (CameraActor + AnchorId + BlendTimeOverride),
`ACodexMenuCharacterActor` (skeletal mesh idling), `ACodexFrontendGameMode` / `ACodexFrontendPlayerController`
(title or main menu, `FocusCameraAnchor` = `SetViewTargetWithBlend`). Pure rules `CodexFrontendRules`. Assets:
`Scripts/Editor/create_frontend_assets.py` + editor helper `UFrontendWidgetGenerator`. Retired: `UMainMenuWidget`
(«Start game / Start battle»), `UPauseMenuWidget`, `USaveLoadDialogWidget`, the in-level start menu and `-ForceMainMenu`.

**Mission start** — `Core/MissionSessionSubsystem` (GameInstance: last mode, quick-restart flag, `bFrontendStart`,
the save system's `PendingLoadSlot`). A mission level (game mode `ACodexTacticsGameMode`) starts at once:
`UMissionSubsystem::StartMission(Game)` (Ctrl + X: the last mode again; `StartMission(Combat)` stays as the dev / bot
shortcut: quest chain completed via `UQuestSubsystem::CompleteChainForCombat`, squad healed / warmed at the actor tagged
`CombatStart`, pre-combat cutscene). The intro briefing plays unless a save is pending or the run is headless
(`-ExecCmds` / `-NoMainMenu` / `-CodexBot`) without a frontend start (`MissionRules::ShouldSkipIntro`). The frontend map
has no mission (`IsMissionWorld`). Godot `main.gd _ready`, `_on_start_game_pressed`, `_on_start_combat_pressed`.

**Mission** — `Core/MissionSubsystem` + `Core/MissionRules`: objective text (start «Исследовать КПП…», quest chain
objectives, preparation / wave «ОБОРОНА: Отразить волну N! Врагов: M» / victory texts), mission failed on any
operative death (reason hypothermia at cold ≥ 99 else wounds, HQ radio line, GameOver = time stop), `RestartMission`
(reload level; «Начать заново» and Ctrl + X). Godot `main.gd update_objective`, `_trigger_game_over`,
`_restart_current_test_mode`.

**UI** — `UI/CodexTacticsHUD` (canvas: objective banner «ЦЕЛЬ: …» top left, message feed, squad panel with supply,
labels; owns the action menu, loot dialog and `UI/MissionFailedWidget` «МИССИЯ ПРОВАЛЕНА»). Godot colours go
through `ACodexTacticsHUD::GodotColor` (sRGB → linear) in UMG and canvas.
`UI/GameMessageSubsystem` (feed, logs every line to `LogCodexTactics`).

**Tutorial framework (Demo 01 Sprint 1, C2 / C7 / C8 / C9 / C10, 2026-10-11)** — UE-only (Godot had no tutorial).
- **Command choke point** `Core/PlayerCommands.h`: ~60 logical command ids (`Order.Move`, `Squad.Solo`, `Squad.Regroup`,
  `Stance.Crouch`, `Stance.Squad.Prone`, `Cover.Enter`, `Cover.Shimmy`, `Cover.Corner`, `Object.Relocate`, `Object.LightBarrel`,
  `Item.HeatPack`, `Loot.Open`, `Trap.DisarmMine` / `DisarmObject` / `DisarmTripwire`, `Trap.SetOnObject`, `Deploy.Mine` /
  `Barricade` / `Turret` / `Tripwire`, `Attack.TargetedShot`, `Grenade.Aim`, `Time.Pause` / `Resume` / `TurnBased` / `RealTime`,
  `TB.EndTurn` / `TB.Attack`, `Posture.*`, `System.QuickSave`, camera ...) + a registry (default key label, section, description:
  the F1 Controls screen is generated from it: `PlayerCommands::GetSections()` order, `GetKeyLabel()` honours `SetKeyLabelOverride()` for rebinds, `CodexControlsRules::BuildSections()`; texts live ONLY in `PlayerCommands.cpp`). `PlayerCommands::Report(Context, Id, FPlayerCommandContext)` -> `UCodexEventBus::OnPlayerCommand`.
  Reported (one line each, behaviour unchanged) from `ACodexTacticsPlayerController` handlers (`ControllerCommands::Report`),
  `UInteractionSubsystem` (menu confirm -> light / defuse / use, loot, set trap), `URelocationSubsystem` (relocate / deploy /
  tripwire orders), `ATacticalCameraPawn` (WASD / edge pan start). Gameplay events `GameplayEvents::Raise` ->
  `UCodexEventBus::OnGameplayEvent`: `RelocationDone`, `RelocationRefused`, `BarrelLit`, `LessonDowned`, `TripwireSpotted` /
  `TripwireDisarmed` / `TripwireTriggered` (subject = the operative), cover (`AOperativeCharacter`, all modes: exploration, real
  time, pause execution, turn-based grid): `CoverEntered` (+ `CoverEntered.High` / `.Low`) once he has SETTLED at the slot (run-in
  glide / enter clip blend over, corner snap done, still; not on the order, not for the quiet return after an open shot), `CoverLeft`,
  `CoverShimmy` (a player's side-step; the automatic corner snap is none), `CornerReached` (kind Left / Right). Details for
  Blueprints / tools: `GameplayEvents::RaiseWithContext` -> `UCodexEventBus::OnGameplayEventDetailed(Name, FGameplayEventContext)`
  (Subject / SubjectId = the operative, Target / TargetId = the wall actor, Kind = High / Low / Left / Right, Location = slot);
  the tutorial also maps
  `OnMineSpotted` -> `MineSpotted`, `OnItemUsed` -> `ItemUsed` (+ `.ItemId`), `OnSoldierDowned`, `OnDialogueFinished`, cold tier ->
  `ColdTier` (+ `.Chills` / `.Freezing` / ...), flow -> `CombatStarted` / `CombatEnded`.
- `Tutorial/TutorialRules` (pure: steps JSON parse + validation, matching, line timing, lesson rule, save / telemetry formats),
  `Tutorial/TutorialStateMachine` (pure per-step machine: Idle -> Waiting (delay) -> Queued -> Active -> Completed / Skipped /
  TimedOut; one hint card at a time + 1.5 s breather, <= 3 objectives, one radio / walk-and-talk step; queued steps complete
  silently; once / replay; failure_retry rate-limited 60 s; export / import progress), `Tutorial/TutorialSubsystem`
  (UTickableWorldSubsystem glue: loads the steps file named by the level's demo flags on the first tick, real-time ticking,
  overlay views, markers, speech playback, checkpoint autosave per beat (slot `checkpoint`, only when `CanSaveNow`), lesson rule via
  `UHealthComponent::LethalDamageGuard` (HP floor 1 + `ForceKnockDown` + retry prompt), "Retry from checkpoint" = in-place
  `LoadGame("checkpoint")`, save section `"tutorial"` (progress applied later if the steps load after the save), telemetry
  `Saved/Tutorial/tutorial.jsonl`, ON / OFF `Codex.Tutorial.Enabled` + `Saved/Tutorial/profile.json`; dev console
  `Codex.Tutorial.Skip / Reset / Retry / Step <id>`), `Tutorial/TutorialTriggerVolume`, `Tutorial/TutorialMarkerActor`,
  `UI/TutorialOverlayWidget` (HUD z 14). Texts: `FNarrativeManifest::GetTutorialText` (manifest `"tutorial"` section, else a
  `sequences` entry, else `[EN missing: <id>]`), `UTutorialSubsystem::GetTutorialText` for Blueprints.
- **Demo game-mode flags** `Core/DemoModeSubsystem` + `FDemoModeSettings` (level JSON `"demo"` object, `LevelJsonRules::ParseDemoSettings`):
  Panic off on every operative, vision cones forced on / off on operatives and enemies (also later spawns), tutorial on + steps file,
  demo HUD preset (`ACodexTacticsHUD::bDemoHudPreset`: debug squad panel and operative labels hidden). No `"demo"` object = no change.
- **Command locks / conditions / actions (2026-10-12, Demo 01 A0-A1 redesign):** beats and steps carry `lock` / `unlock`
  (command id patterns; beat: on start, step: when shown or, never shown, when finished) -> `FTutorialStateMachine::IsCommandLocked`
  / `GetLocks` (saved as `"locks"` in the save's tutorial section; older saves rebuild them). The lock choke point for order handlers is
  `UTutorialSubsystem::CheckCommandLocked` / `FilterCommand` (`TutorialRules::ResolveLockedCommand`: a locked `Order.Sprint` degrades
  to `Order.Move`, other locked ids are refused) - used by `ACodexTacticsPlayerController::HandleWorldHit` (double click on the ground /
  on an object); a refusal raises `Tutorial.Locked` + `Tutorial.Locked.<CommandId>` (no error line). Tutorial OFF / no steps = no locks.
  Trigger `condition` (polled each tick) + step `condition` clauses (`squad_cold_max` / `squad_cold_min` / `leader_cold` /
  `squad_heat_packs`, `UTutorialSubsystem::GetConditionStat` via `FTutorialStateMachine::StatProvider`; a due step whose condition
  fails is skipped silently, followers go on), `trigger.requires` (arming steps), step `actions` (`cold_bump`:
  `UColdSurvivalComponent::AddColdShock` = `ColdRules::ApplyColdShock`, run when shown), `focus: coldest_operative` (card names / marks
  him, `{focus}` token, `FTutorialCardView::FocusOperative`), kind `feed` (message-feed line, no slot, completes at once). New event
  `ItemLooted` + `ItemLooted.<Item>` (`GameplayEvents::RaiseItemLooted`; `ALootCrateActor::TakeItem` = click / Take all, and the
  crate / pile drag in `USquadTransferSubsystem`). Level JSON `squad_loadout.heat_packs_count` (`FSquadLoadout::HeatPacksCount`,
  `LoadoutRules::StartingHeatPacks`, applied at spawn by the game mode; -1 = the tactical_heat.json role kit).
- **Rev 4 (2026-10-12, playtest "too hard"):** `demo_01_steps.json` locks sprint on the A0 stretch only; beat A1 `unlock`s it at beacon 1 (card `A1_sprint` first, then select / box); starter heat pack 1 per operative (`heat_packs_count: 1`); `A1_wt_nopack` / `A1_wt_warmed` removed; `A1_wt_resupply` (`WT_A1_RESUPPLY`) + `A1_obj_hut` (id kept) trigger after the heat pack lesson; guard on `VOL_A1_BEACON3`. Beat sheet rev 4.
- **Rev 5 (2026-10-13, the user's new 15-step Demo 01 flow; beat sheet rev 5):** `demo_01_steps.json` beats A0-A4: HQ radio
  only at the start (`A0_radio_hq` locks `Order.Move` while it plays; `ACodexTacticsPlayerController::HandleWorldHit` refuses a
  locked move: `FilterCommand(Order.Move).IsNone()`), the staged camera lesson after it (+ `A0_cam_pan_drag` MMB drag), beat A0 locks
  `Order.Sprint` + `Inventory.*` + `Item.*`; the crack = the one-at-a-time bridge (`A1_solo` done on `BridgeCrossed` x3 distinct);
  regroup -> box -> banter; the heat pack card `unlock`s `Inventory.*` / `Item.*`; the sprint card `unlock`s `Order.Sprint`; hut;
  A2 barrel; A3 approach (stances / vault / low fence `CrawlGapPassed` x3 / cover / `MineSpotted` / `TripwireSpotted` cards); A4
  `View.VisionCones` + `Deploy.Tripwire` cards. **Precredit** (user addition): `FTutorialStateMachine` keeps the command history
  (`GetCommandHistory / GetCommandUse`: uses + hold seconds per command id since the steps loaded, every `HandleCommand` /
  `HandleCommandHold`); when a once-only command hint card becomes due and `TutorialRules::IsPrecreditSatisfied` holds it is
  completed without showing (`FTutorialTransition::bPrecredited`; the runtime posts one feed tick "✓ <title> - already done.",
  telemetry `precredited`, `UTutorialSubsystem::GetPrecreditedCount`); partial uses seed the card's progress (`CountHistoryUses`);
  step `"precredit": false` opts out; event / volume completions and `"once": false` cards never precredit; the history is saved
  (`"history": {id: {count, held_s}}`) and reset with the tutorial. **New lock targets:** `Inventory.Open` (I key /
  `ACodexTacticsHUD::ToggleInventoryDrawer`, reported on opening), `Item.*` (`UseSquadItem`, `HandleTransferRequest` item drags).
  **New commands / events:** `Inventory.Open` (I), `View.VisionCones` (O; `UVisionConeComponent::SetPlayerEnemyCones`, CVar
  `Codex.VisionCones.Enemies`, `VisionConeRules::ResolveVisible(.., bPlayerToggle)` for enemies only, pause menu entry
  `VisionConesButton` / `VisionCones`), events `BridgeEntered / BridgeRefused / BridgeCrossed`, `CrawlGapRefused / CrawlGapPassed`,
  `VisionConesToggled(.On/.Off)`. **Level obstacles (`Level/`):** `ABridgeCrossingVolume` (Span box, Capacity 1 default, 0 =
  unlimited, `bAffectsEnemies`, refusal feed line + BP event `OnBridgeCreak`; occupants / waiting; a refused operative is
  stopped and gets an `AOperativeCharacter::SetTrafficHold` the formation honours in `USquadSubsystem::UpdateFollower`; released
  when the span is free or he walks off; `BridgeRules`), `ACrawlGapActor` (hidden blocking Bar from `ClearanceCm` 90 to `BarTopCm`
  200, `Approach` zone, a `UNavArea_CrawlOnly` modifier over the footprint via `UVehicleNavObstacleComponent`;
  `AOperativeCharacter::RequestMove` passes `UNavFilter_NoCrawl` unless prone; `UNavFilter_NoVault` (enemies) excludes it too; a
  standing / crouched operative walking at the fence is stopped + held + hinted (`CrawlGapRules`), a prone one passes and raises
  `CrawlGapPassed` on the far side), pure rules `Level/LevelObstacleRules`. Tests `CodexTactics.Tutorial.Precredit.*` (2),
  `CodexTactics.Level.*` (2), `Schema.DemoFile` / `Schema.DemoFileA0A1Flow` rewritten for rev 5, `Combat.VisionCone.ToggleAndColours`
  (+ player toggle), `UI.Frontend.*` (new command rows / pause entry); smokes `TutorialSmoke` (rev 5 order, precredit, locks, MMB
  drag, O toggle), `BridgeFenceSmoke` (new: the actors on L_MovementTest).
- Tests `CodexTactics.Tutorial.*` (9 + 8 + 2: `Lock.Rules`, `Lock.Schema`, `Lock.LockUnlock`, `Lock.Saved`, `Action.GustTimeout`,
  `Condition.Trigger`, `Schema.DemoFileA0A1Flow`, `Demo.HeatPackLoadout`, `Precredit.Rules`, `Precredit.StateMachine`), smoke
  `TutorialSmoke` (exclusive; runs the real demo file A0 -> A1), `SaveRoundTripSmoke` covers the "tutorial" save section.

**Vehicles (APC BTR-82, phases P0 + P1, 2026-10-12, UE-only; design `docs/vehicles/APC_00..02`)** — kinematic, nav-driven
(approach B, no Chaos). `Vehicles/VehicleRules` (pure: seats `AssignSeats` Engineer driver / Commander gunner / anyone if absent, 7
seats; `CanBoard` / `CanDisembark` / `CanDrive` (P1: refused in the tactical pause and turn-based); the Y key `ResolveKeyAction`
(all addressed aboard = get out, else board); `RankExitPoints` (away from the nearest known threat, else the camera side, blocked
skipped); drive model `StepYaw` (yaw rate <= v / R, no pivot), `ShouldReverse` (goal behind >= 120 deg within 15 m),
`StepSpeed` / `GetDesiredSpeed` (cruise 6 / rush 10 / reverse 2.5 m/s, 3 / 6 m/s2, braking distance, slow to turn),
`GetLookaheadPoint` (pure pursuit, 1 m carry past a corner > 25 deg: no cutting round wall ends), `GetHullSideFacing` (cover probe),
noise radii idle / cruise / rush 20 / 40 / 55 m, wheel spin, Ackermann steer, `GetMountedEffects`). `Vehicles/VehiclePawn`
(`AVehiclePawn`, Blueprintable APawn: Root (ground pivot), `Hull` box (WorldDynamic, blocks everything but Camera, overlaps,
no nav), `Mesh` (vendor SK + ABP, no collision; a C++-only spawn loads `DefaultMesh` / `DefaultAnimClass` by soft path),
`CabinHeat` (`UHeatSourceComponent`, on while the engine runs = a crew member aboard), `NavObstacle`; tunables `DriveConfig` /
`CrewConfig` / `EngineConfig`, hull centre / extent, entry points (side doors, rear hatch), ground probes; `DriveTo` (path on the
"Vehicle" navmesh by agent name, partial paths drive to the nearest reachable point; K-turn first when facing > 70 deg away),
pawn sweep ahead (brakes for operatives / enemies, gives up after 6 s), ground conform (4 traces: Z, pitch, roll), vendor ABP
variables by name (`WheelSpeed`, `WheelsAngle`, `DoorAngle`) + BlueprintImplementableEvents On Visual Drive / On Doors Changed / On
Engine Changed, engine noise breaks patrols (`AEnemyCharacter::BreakPatrol(Hearing)`) once a second). `Vehicles/VehicleNavigation`
(`UNavArea_NoVehicle` = unwalkable for agent 1 only; `UNavArea_VehicleHull` = unwalkable for agent 0 only;
`UVehicleNavObstacleComponent` = one rotated box modifier over the hull + 40 cm on the infantry navmesh, off while driving, on when
parked). `Vehicles/VehicleSubsystem` (`UVehicleSubsystem`: registry; mounted map + `VehicleQueries::IsMounted / GetVehicle`;
`HandleBoardKey` (Y / Alt + Y, action bar BOARD / EXIT), `OrderBoard` (walk to the nearest door, retries / another door when one is
crowded, 2 s climb, `CancelBoarding` on a new move / cover order), `OrderDisembark` (exit candidates on the infantry navmesh, free of
bodies, ranked, 1 s, then `OrderTakeCover` at the hull side), `HandleDriveClick`, mount = stop, leave cover, hidden, no collision,
movement off, attached at `CrewAttachOffset`; opt-in vehicle navmesh (strips the Vehicle agent from untagged bounds at BeginPlay,
`EnableVehicleNavigation`); save `world_state.vehicles` (name, class, transform, crew + seats), `PrepareForLoad` / `RestoreSave`).
**The IsMounted list** (keep it short): enemy targeting / perception / hearing / nearest / pounce (`EnemyCharacter`, `GetBelief`),
marksman targets, operative cover / facing / shooting tick, `ResolveIncomingHit` (no damage), `RequestMove` (refused), formation
followers (a mounted leader: the ones outside hold), Commander Mode, mines / tripwires, the controller's group moves and the click
pick, turn-based entry (refused while anyone is aboard). Controller: a ground click with the leader aboard drives; a click on the
vehicle = cover at its hull (`FindCoverSlotForHit` / `TryHandleCoverClick` vehicle exception, `CoverTraceRules` no longer ignores
vehicle pawns). Commands `Vehicle.Board` / `Vehicle.Disembark` / `Vehicle.Drive` (F1 section «Vehicles»), events `VehicleBoarded` (Kind
= seat), `VehicleDisembarked`, `VehicleDriveStarted`, `VehicleArrived`, `VehiclePathBlocked`; `ATutorialTriggerVolume` «Activated By:
Vehicle». Tests `CodexTactics.Vehicle.*` (10), smoke `VehicleSmoke`; editor script `Scripts/Editor/setup_apc_vehicle.py`.

**Vehicles P2 + user requests (2026-10-12, branch `agents/apc2`)** — `Vehicles/VehicleCombatRules` (pure: `FVehicleTurretConfig`
(cannon 45 dmg + 1.5 m splash 20, 2-shell bursts 0.5 s / pause 1.2 s (nerfed by the user 2026-10-12 from 70 / 35 / 3), 40 m, 60 shells per map, hit 0.9, armour factor 0.25 = the HE
shell mostly ignores the Brute's hide; MG 18 dmg x 8 rounds/s, 35 m, hit 0.8, overheats in 4 s / cools in 3 s, no suppression
stagger (removed by the user 2026-10-12: the MG only deals damage); traverse 60 deg/s, elevation -4..+60, dead zone 4 m from the hull side, Defensive radius 20 m),
`ChooseTarget` (Ctrl+click priority in any posture; Passive holds; Defensive only once provoked and within 20 m; Aggressive any;
a Brute within 20 m first, else the nearest; never in the dead zone / out of sight / range), `ChooseWeapon` (cannon for a Brute /
marksman / group of 3 / priority while shells last, MG for the rest; an overheated MG means small targets wait), `StepHeat`,
`GetCannonDamage`, `GetShellsForMap`, `GetHudLine`; `FVehicleCrushConfig` + `EvaluateContact` (operative /
friendly -> Brake; small enemy -> Crush >= 1.5 m/s else Push aside; Brute -> KnockDown >= 4 m/s unless re-knock immune, else Block;
a downed Brute -> Block (option `bCrushDownedRammed`); flying drone -> None), `GetSpeedAfterImpacts` (x0.9 per crush, x0.25 per ram);
`FVehicleFadeConfig` + `FVehicleFadeTimeline` (0.25 s out / hold >= 0.15 s / 0.25 s in, joins while going black, a request during the
fade-in queues one more cycle, hold <= 1 s) + `ShouldStartGroupFade` (the first at a door waits <= 8 s for the mates who will
reach their doors in that time: one fade for the squad, a straggler gets his own)). `Vehicles/VehicleTurretComponent` (`UVehicleTurretComponent` on `AVehiclePawn::Turret`: gunner = the Gunner seat;
retarget every 0.25 s from LOS traces (ECC_Visibility) from the muzzle socket `gun_jntSocket`; slews the vendor ABP
`TurretHorizontalAngle` / `GunElevation` (signs measured by VehicleCombatSmoke); damage through `UHealthComponent::TakeDamage`
(numbers, armour, downed x1.33 as for any hit; `AttackerSource` = the gunner's name = his kill stats), tracers
(`UCombatFeedbackSubsystem::SpawnTracer`, every other MG round), muzzle flash = the vendor Cascade `PS_MuzzleFire_01` attached to the
socket, noise (`NotifySquadNoise` Explosion / Gunshot, once a second); shells saved in `world_state.vehicles[].shells`; held without a
gunner / in turn-based / in the world AI pause). `AVehiclePawn` additions: `CrushConfig` / `FadeConfig`, `HandleContactsAhead`
(replaces the P1 "brake for every pawn" sweep: a brake zone as long as the braking distance + 1 m, a contact zone = the last metre of
the hull + 60 cm; `CrushEnemy` = body thrown beside the hull + `ApplyDirectHealthLoss` with the driver's name (normal death chain:
corpse, kill stats, XP, waves) + "CRUSHED" text + `VehicleRunOver`; `RamEnemy` = ram damage 40 + `UKnockdownComponent::
ForceKnockDownScaled(.., 0.65, 1.5)` (new: the fall and get-up clips at an absolute 0.65 rate, downed x1.5; `KnockdownRules::
ScaleTimeline`) + 80 cm shove), the wheels from the MEASURED ground speed (displacement per tick): the vendor ABP turns the wheels by
`WheelSpeed x WheelSpeedOffset` where `WheelSpeedOffset` is its own clock (seconds) — any speed change multiplied the whole elapsed
time (the whirl / slip the user saw); C++ now pins that clock to 1 s each tick and writes `WheelSpeed = wheel angle / clock`
(`VehicleRules::GetAnimWheelSpeedForAngle`), the wheel angle integrating ground speed / 60 cm; the acceleration ramps from 30 % to full
over 0.8 s when pulling away (`GetLaunchAccelerationFactor`); BlueprintImplementableEvents On Turret Fired / On Ran Over (sound / FX
polish). `UVehicleSubsystem`: boarding / getting out through the fade (`ShouldFade`: the vehicle's fade on, real time, not paused;
the ones at a door wait for the group, then mount on black; exits all go on black once the hull stands; camera `SnapFocusOnTarget`

(`WeaponTuning::ApplyVehicleWeapons`, dumped by `DumpWeaponTuning`, kept by the Wave Editor), level JSON `"vehicle": {"shells": N}`

aboard. Events `VehicleTurretFired` (Kind Cannon per shell / MachineGun per second of fire), `VehicleTurretKill`, `VehicleRunOver`.

## 6. Data / content status

### Tutorial: how the user places volumes / markers and edits steps (Demo 01, 2026-10-11; rev 5 2026-10-13; rev 5b 2026-10-13; rev 5c 2026-10-13; rev 5d 2026-10-14)

**Demo 01 rev 5d (2026-10-14, the game designer's three requests; beat sheet decision block "rev 5d"):** (3) **Corner peek click on the NEAR edge (bug, every level):** an operative hugging a high wall near one end - hovering that end showed the peek ghost, the click did nothing (the far end worked). **Root cause:** `ACodexTacticsPlayerController::TryHandleCoverClick` ran `FindClickedMember` (a click within `SelectRadius` 120 cm of an operative picks him) BEFORE the corner-peek check, and the edge he already stands at lies 30-70 cm from his body: the click selected him (he already was the leader: nothing visible); the hover (`UpdateCornerPeekHover`) has no such rule. **Fix:** the peek check runs first (`CoverFacingRules::CornerPeekClickWins`: a peek edge of his own wall wins, a click on a body - the hit actor is a pawn - still selects); `AOperativeCharacter::OrderCornerPeek` decides with `CoverFacingRules::DecideCornerPeekOrder` (`ECornerPeekOrder`): within `CornerSnapToleranceCm` + 10 of the stand point -> the aim starts in place, else real time / pause shimmy the few cm, turn-based poses in place when his slot is at that corner (`IsAtCorner`, no AP) and refuses farther away; a stand point the shimmy probe cannot find while already at that corner -> in place. Checks: `CodexTactics.Tactics.Cover.CornerPeekNearEdge` (precedence, both ends, standing / crouched, real time / turn-based), `CornerHoldSmoke` near-edge passes (cover 50 cm from each end, click 20 cm in from that end = 58-62 cm from his body: handled as a peek, corner aim in 0.05 s, 0 cm moved; standing, then crouched facing the other way).

**Demo 01 rev 5c (2026-10-13, the designer's review of the auto layout; beat sheet decision block "rev 5c"):** (1) **movement lesson right after the camera lesson:** `A0_move` ("CLICK TO WALK", `TUT_A0_MOVE`, done on `Order.Move`) follows `A0_cam_zoom` at once (the after_step list with the radio is gone), then `A0_sprint` ("DOUBLE-CLICK TO RUN", `TUT_A0_SPRINT`, done on `Order.Sprint`); both precreditable; `A0_obj_beacon` still asks for beacon 1 (the user's marker with Step Id `A0_move` at beacon 1 shows while the walk card is up). `A1_sprint` is a survival tip (no command completion, 10 s). (2) **the bridge teaches nothing**: `A1_wt_bridge` (2 lines) + objective `A1_bridge` (`OBJ_A1_BRIDGE`, `BridgeCrossed` x3 distinct, 240 s; the user's marker at the bridge: Step Id **`A1_bridge`**, was `A1_solo`); `A1_select` / `A1_solo` (Solo ON, `Squad.Solo` command, unlocks Solo / Regroup) moved to the hut, `A1_regroup` + `A1_box` follow the hut loot (after the bridge the squad arrives together); banter / gust / heat pack hang on `A1_bridge`. (3) **Tactical hut entry** (selection + Solo + cover): `VOL_A1_HUT_APPROACH` -> `A1_wt_hut_approach` + `A1_select` -> `A1_solo` -> three cards queued together: `A1_hut_stack_l` (`{"event": "CoverEntered", "in_volume": "VOL_A1_DOOR_L", "distinct_from": [...]}`, unlocks `Cover.*`), `A1_hut_stack_r` (`VOL_A1_DOOR_R`), `A1_hut_enter` (`{"volume": "VOL_A1_HUT", "distinct_from": [...]}`) -> `A1_wt_hut` -> loot -> regroup -> box. **Engine:** `FTutorialCompletion::InVolume` (JSON `complete.in_volume`: the event's subject stands in a tutorial volume with that id - `UTutorialSubsystem::GetTutorialVolumesAt`, filled by `HandleBusEvent` from the subject actor's location) and `DistinctFrom` (`complete.distinct_from`: an operative who counted for those steps never counts here; `FRuntime::Contributors`, runtime only - not saved); `FTutorialStateMachine::HandleVolume(..., SubjectId)`, `HandleVolumePresence` (a volume step that starts running counts the squad members already standing in it, `UTutorialSubsystem::CountVolumePresence`, the volume's Leader filter respected). **What the user places for his own map:** `VOL_A1_HUT_APPROACH` (Leader), `VOL_A1_DOOR_L` / `_R` (Any) on the outer wall left / right of the door (as seen arriving), `VOL_A1_HUT` over the INTERIOR only with **Fire Once OFF**, markers Step Id `A1_hut_stack_l` / `_r` / `_enter` (the auto layout copy has them all, `docs/demo/DEMO01_AUTOLAYOUT.md` §2a). **Cover fix:** `CoverTraceRules::FindGround` no longer starts inside a roof that juts out over the wall (eaves: every cover click under them failed; `CoverHugSmoke` eaves check). New `UCoverQueryLibrary::FindCoverSlotAt` (Blueprint / editor Python) for level checks. (4) **Manual corner peek by click (every level, C++):** an operative in high cover + a click on the END of his own wall (`CoverFacingRules::FindPeekEdge`: the last `FCoverPeekConfig::EdgeHitCm` 75 cm of the face, up to 60 cm past the edge, the end face / top - at most 60 cm in front of the face, 2.5 m behind it; edges from his slot or, for a long wall, from a slot probed next to the click; his wall actor only; high cover only) -> `ACodexTacticsPlayerController::TryHandleCoverClick` -> `AOperativeCharacter::OrderCornerPeek(Side, EdgeAlongCm)`: shimmies to `CoverFacingRules::CornerPeekStandAlong` (the clip side's stand-off), faces that corner and HOLDS the corner aim (`IsManualCornerPeekSpot` -> `WantsCornerHold` true, no "no fight / no threat" or legacy "no targets" break) until another order: a move (`LeaveCover`; the quiet open-shot step-off keeps it), another cover, a player shimmy, the other corner. Event `CornerPeek` (Kind Left / Right) once per order when the pose is reached; command `Cover.CornerPeek` (F1 "Click wall edge", Combat; locked with `Cover.*` until the hut lesson). **Preview:** hovering that edge shows a second ghost in the corner fire stance (`ACoverGhostActor::ShowCornerPeekFor`, cvr_*_fire_idle) at the corner and the crosshair cursor (`UpdateCornerPeekHover`, probe cached while the cursor rests). **Limits:** turn-based only where he already stands at the corner (pose only, no AP; else "move to the corner first"); in the tactical pause it executes like a shimmy (not planned); low cover has no peek (fired over the top anywhere). The automatic hold when engaged and the Ctrl+click corner shot are unchanged. Card `A3_peek` (optional, A3 cover field). Checks: `Schema.DemoFile` (chain incl. `A0_sprint`), `Schema.DemoFileA0A1Flow`, TutorialSmoke stage 61 (walk + run precredited after the radio-time double click), stages 32-35 / 70-72 (bridge objective; hut approach -> select -> B solo -> "go in" by volume presence -> cover in VOL_A1_DOOR_R / _L by two different operatives through the event bus -> loot -> B regroup -> box), `CodexTactics.Tutorial.StateMachine.InVolumeDistinctFrom`, `Lock.ProgressiveDemo` (cover unlocked at the door), layout verify `Saved/Logs/Demo01LayoutVerify.txt` PASS; corner peek: `CodexTactics.Tactics.Cover.CornerPeekEdgeHit`, `CornerHoldSmoke` first phase (exploring, no enemy: hover ghost + crosshair at the end, none at the middle; a click on the end 3 m away -> at the corner, corner aim held 4 s, CornerPeek once, a move order releases it; again in the legacy cover mode where a shimmy order releases it), `UI.Frontend.Controls.Registry`, `ControlsScreenSmoke`.
