the low fence; `VOL_A4_OVERLOOK` (Beat Id `A4`) where the patrols come into view. (7) **The low fence**: your fence mesh (bottom rail ~0.9 m)
+ a NEW **`CrawlGapActor`** (Place Actors -> "Crawl Gap Actor") on the fence line: local X across the fence, `Gap Width Cm` = the fence
length, `Clearance Cm` 90 (a prone capsule is 80 cm), `Bar Top Cm` 200; close the line otherwise (rocks / wall) within ~15 m or the standing
path simply walks round. Standing / crouched bodies are stopped with "Too low - go prone and crawl under."; prone ones crawl through;
enemies never path under. (8) A3 traps: 2 hidden `AProximityMineActor` on the shortcut, 1 `APMCTripwireActor` across the obvious gap. (9) A4:
2 `APatrolRouteActor` loops + `BP_Enemy_Frostbitten` x2 + `BP_Enemy_Hound` x2 (the skirmish logic = Phase 2, beat sheet §1.3; keep
`combat_start: "button"`). **Keys for the tester:** I = inventory drawer (locked until the heat pack lesson), O = enemy vision cones
(also the pause menu "VISION CONES: ON / OFF").

**Draft texts:** `Content/Data/Tutorial/demo_01_texts_draft.json` supplies placeholder texts for ids missing from the narrative manifest (A0, A1 and the A2 heat-pack card since 2026-10-12); replace by the sheet (request #17) and delete entries as the manifest gains them.

**Demo 01 A0-A1 (2026-10-12, rev 4 order) - what the user places in `L_Demo01_A_FrozenLake`:** (1) `VOL_A0_BEACON1` (Beat Id `A1`) at beacon 1
(as before): it now also UNLOCKS sprint (the lock only covers the A0 stretch). (2) The **fisherman's hut** with its interior
`ACameraZoneVolume` (fixed camera, Environment Shelter x0.5), an **`ALootCrateActor`** inside with Contents **Heat Packs 3** and every
other count 0 (Medkits, Canned Food, Bread, Chocolate, Matches, all Ammo, Firewood, Flares, Turrets, Barricades, Mines; bonus ids empty;
not trapped), a **`TutorialTriggerVolume` Volume Id `VOL_A1_HUT`** covering the hut floor (Activated By: Any Operative), optional
`TutorialMarkerActor` on the crate with Step Id `A1_loot_open`. **Rev 4 order: the squad visits the hut AFTER the crack and the heat pack
lesson** (radio line `WT_A1_RESUPPLY`: "the hut is close"), so the hut should be close to the crack / on the way to beacon 3 rather than
far back towards beacon 1. The user's `TutorialMarkerActor` with **Step Id `A1_obj_hut`** (the "resupply" objective; keep it on the hut /
crate) now shows only from that radio line on, not from the beat start. (3) `VOL_A1_BEACON2` at the crack (as before) and, at the solo scout
point ~20 m off the line on the crack edge, a **wind `ACameraZoneVolume`** Cold Multiplier **2.5** (camera switch off), ~12 x 12 m;
optional marker with Step Id `A1_solo` there. No extra solo volume is needed (the gust timer starts when solo is pressed). (4) The
rest as before (`VOL_A1_BEACON3/4`, `VOL_A1_RIDGE` / `_FAR`, `VOL_A2_CAMP` Beat Id `A2`; the guard card now fires on `VOL_A1_BEACON3`).
The level JSON has `squad_loadout.heat_packs_count: 1` (PER OPERATIVE: every operative spawns with exactly 1 heat pack; the heat pack
lesson uses these starter packs, the hut crate restocks).

1. **Turn it on for a level**: in its level JSON (`Content/Data/LevelJson/<level>.json`) add
   `"demo": { "panic": false, "vision_cones": true, "tutorial": true, "tutorial_steps": "demo_01_steps.json", "hud": "demo" }`
   (every key optional; no `"demo"` object = normal game; `intro_dialogue` defaults to false when a `demo` object exists). **Which level JSON a map gets (2026-10-12):** `-LevelJson=` on the command line wins; else `Content/Data/LevelJson/<MapName>.json` if it exists (map name without the PIE prefix, e.g. `L_Demo01_A_FrozenLake.json`; `LevelJsonRules::ResolveLevelJsonFile`, used by `ACodexTacticsGameMode::LoadLevelConfig` and therefore by `PeekLevelConfig` / the mission intro flag); else the game mode BP's `LevelJsonFile` (default `level_01_outpost.json`, so L_MovementTest / L_PatrolTest are unchanged). A new map gets its own level by saving a JSON with the map's name (Wave Editor: level_id = map name). `L_Demo01_A_FrozenLake.json` is a DRAFT (3 sudden waves on lanes NORTH_ICE / EAST_GULLY / WEST_SHORE, `combat_start: button`, horde off, loadout mines 2 / barricades 1 / turret 1 / matches 3; the `matches` key is only in the Wave Editor type, C++ does not read it yet; `breach_wave` / `susanin_wave` keys do not exist: the breach is the map's dynamic spawn point, Susanin the SusaninSpawn point).
2. **Volumes**: Place Actors -> `TutorialTriggerVolume` (class `ATutorialTriggerVolume`, amber box; resize the `Box` component).
   Details -> **CodexTactics|Tutorial**: `Step Id` (step to start), `Beat Id` (beat to start: checkpoint + lesson rule),
   `Volume Id` (the name steps use in `"trigger": {"type": "volume", "volume": ...}` / `"complete": {"volume": ...}`; empty =
   Step Id), `Required Beat` (fires only from that beat on), `Activated By` (Only The Leader / Any Operative), `Fire Once`.
   Without steps / with the tutorial off it does nothing and is not used up.
3. **Markers**: Place Actors -> `TutorialMarkerActor` (`ATutorialMarkerActor`: ground ring + bobbing beacon, visible in the editor,
   in game only while active). `Step Id` (shown while that step is on screen) or `Beat Id` (shown during that beat); `Ring Radius Cm`,
   `Show Beacon`, `Beacon Height Cm`, `Screen Edge Pointer` (overlay arrow when off-screen), `Color` (amber), `Marker Material`
   (Gemini's material slot, needs a vector parameter `Color`; request #18).
4. **Steps**: `Content/Data/Tutorial/<file>.json` (staged as UFS). Root `{ "id", "version", "start_beat", "beats": [...], "steps": [...] }`.
   Beat: `{ "id", "lesson": bool (lethal hits only down), "checkpoint": bool (autosave slot "checkpoint" at its start), "title_id" }`.
   Step: `{ "id", "beat", "kind": hint_card | objective | radio | walk_talk, "text_id", "keys": [command ids or literal keys],
   "trigger": { "type": manual | beat_start | volume | event | after_step | first_use | failure_retry, "volume", "event", "step",
   "command", "count", "delay_s" }, "complete": { "commands": [...], "count", "hold_s", "event", "distinct_operatives", "volume", "timeout_s" }, "once", "skippable",
   "blocking" (always false), "lines": [ { "speaker", "text_id", "duration_s" } ] }`. No trigger = `beat_start`.
   **2026-10-12 additions (Demo 01 A0-A1 redesign):** kind `feed` (its line(s) go to the message feed at once, no display slot,
   completes immediately: short reactions while a card / speech is up). Beat and step `"lock": [...]` / `"unlock": [...]` (command id
   patterns, `"*"` = all; beat: at its start; step: when shown, or when it finishes without showing; unlock first, then lock): a
   locked `Order.Sprint` walks, the try raises `Tutorial.Locked` / `Tutorial.Locked.Order.Sprint` (trigger a hint with
   `{"type": "event", "event": "Tutorial.Locked.Order.Sprint"}`); saved with the progress; tutorial OFF = no locks. Trigger type
   `condition` + `"trigger.requires": [step ids]` (the trigger counts only after those steps were triggered; works for every trigger
   type) + step `"condition": {"stat": ..., "at_least" | "below": N}` (or an array = AND; stats `squad_cold_max`, `squad_cold_min`,
   `leader_cold`, `squad_heat_packs`): for a `condition` trigger it fires the step; on any step it is re-checked when the step is due
   (after `delay_s`) and a failed check skips it silently (its `after_step` followers still fire). Step `"action": {"type":
   "cold_bump", "amount": 20, "min_level": 42}` (or `"actions": [...]`): run when the step is SHOWN (a walk-and-talk waits for the
   speech slot). Step `"focus": "coldest_operative"`: the card / feed text may use `{focus}` (his name), he gets a floating
   "COLDEST" line, `FTutorialCardView::FocusOperative / FocusName` for the WBP. Unknown stat / action / focus, a `condition`
   trigger without a condition and `requires` naming an unknown step are parse ERRORS. Events for loot: `Loot.Open` (command, crate
   window opened), `ItemLooted` / `ItemLooted.<Item>` (`HeatPack`, `Medkit`, ... - taken into an operative's kit); heat pack used:
   `ItemUsed.HEAT_PACK`. Patterns:
   exact, `"Prefix.*"`, `"*"`. **`"precredit"` (2026-10-13, default true):** a once-only hint card completed by COMMANDS whose completion
   the player's command history already satisfies when the card becomes due (uses / hold time since the steps loaded, recorded even while
   a radio plays or a card is not up) is completed at once without showing ("✓ <title> - already done." in the feed, telemetry
   `precredited`); fewer uses than needed seed the card's progress (one Q press before the Q/E card = 1 of 2). `"precredit": false` for
   lessons that must happen at that moment (regroup, the sprint card, loot open, the tripwire). Event / volume completions and `"once":
   false` cards never precredit. The history is saved in the tutorial section (`"history"`) and forgotten by RESET TUTORIAL. Lock targets
   today: every player command family (rev 5b progressive unlock, see the block at the top); `Order.Sprint` degrades to a walk,
   everything else is refused by its handler; each refusal raises `Tutorial.Locked.<Id>` once per try.
   `"distinct_operatives": true` (2026-10-12): each operative's event counts once, so `count` = how many
   different operatives must do it ("put each operative into cover": `{"event": "CoverEntered", "count": 3, "distinct_operatives": true}`;
   "enter cover 4 times": `{"event": "CoverEntered", "count": 4}`). **`hold_s` (2026-10-12):** accumulated seconds a CONTINUOUS command was
   active while the card is SHOWN (never during the card breather, never while merely queued), in addition to `count` uses:
   `{"commands": ["Camera.Pan.Keys", "Camera.Pan.Edge"], "hold_s": 1.0}`. Continuous inputs report ONE use when the action starts and
   then `PlayerCommands::ReportHold(id, dt)` ticks (`FPlayerCommandContext::HeldSeconds` > 0, same bus; ticks are never uses and never
   fire `first_use`); `FTutorialStateMachine::HandleCommandHold`, rule `TutorialRules::IsProgressMet`. A wildcard is a plain prefix match
   so it spans segments (`Camera.*`, `Camera.Pan.*`). **Camera ids (2026-10-12, were Camera.Pan / .Rotate):** `Camera.Pan.Keys` (WASD, hold ticks),
   `Camera.Pan.Edge` (cursor at the screen edge, hold ticks), `Camera.Pan.Drag` (MMB drag, hold ticks), `Camera.Rotate.Keys` (Q / E, one per
   press), `Camera.Rotate.Mouse` (RMB drag, hold ticks), `Camera.Zoom` (one per wheel notch); key hints in the registry = the F1 Controls
   screen rows (6 camera rows now). **Beat A0 is STAGED:** `A0_cam_pan` (WASD / edge, hold 1 s) -> `A0_cam_rotate_keys` (Q/E x2) ->
   `A0_cam_rotate_mouse` (RMB drag, hold 0.5 s) -> `A0_cam_zoom` (wheel x2) -> `A0_move`; no timeout (skippable); texts
   `TUT_A0_CAM_PAN / _ROTATE_KEYS / _ROTATE_MOUSE / _ZOOM` (draft file). The card counter is per beat ("n / 5" in A0, move included). Sample: `demo_01_steps.json` beat A6 (`A6_*`, volume `VOL_A6_FIELD`). A hint card without completion gets `timeout_s` 12; radio / walk-and-talk steps without completion
   end when their lines have played. Body text may contain `{key:Squad.Solo}` (-> `[B]`). Unknown keys / commands are warned in the
   log; the unit test `CodexTactics.Tutorial.Schema.DemoFile` fails on any warning of `demo_01_steps.json`.
5. **Texts** live in the narrative sheet (Tutorial tab, request #17) -> `narrative_manifest.json` `"tutorial": { "<id>":
   { "title_en", "text_en", "radio_en", "speaker_en" } }`; a walk-and-talk `text_id` may also name a `sequences` entry. Until then
   the overlay shows `[EN missing: <id>]`.
6. **Overlay look**: a WBP child of `UTutorialOverlayWidget` set as the HUD's `TutorialOverlayWidgetClass`, with widgets named
   `TutorialHintCard` (Border), `TutorialCardTitle`, `TutorialCardBody`, `TutorialCardKeys`, `TutorialCardProgress`,
   `TutorialCardDone` (TextBlocks), `TutorialSkipButton`, `TutorialTracker` (VerticalBox) + `TutorialObjective1..3`,
   `TutorialRadioStrip` (Border), `TutorialRadioSpeaker`, `TutorialRadioLine`, `TutorialRadioHint` (TextBlock, rev 5b),
   `TutorialRadioSkipButton` (Button, rev 5b; make it non-focusable), `TutorialRetryPanel`, `TutorialRetryText`,
   `TutorialRetryButton`, `TutorialContinueButton`. Keep panels Self-Hit-Test-Invisible (only the buttons take clicks).
7. **Testers' log**: `Saved/Tutorial/tutorial.jsonl` (one JSON per line: session, map, event shown / completed /
   completed_silently / skipped / timed_out / beat_started / lesson_downed / retry / enabled / disabled, step, beat, active_s, game_s).
- **Per-member Blueprint: how the user sets BP_Operative_FemaleSoldier (user request 2026-10-11).** Each roster entry
  (`ACodexTacticsGameMode::SquadRoster`, `FSquadMemberSpawn`) has `MemberBlueprint` (soft class). Set and loadable -> that
  member spawns from it and the roster's `BodyMesh` is NOT applied (the Blueprint owns mesh, materials, attachments such as
  the backpack); empty / missing -> `OperativeBlueprint` (BP_Operative) + `BodyMesh` as before (one log line «member Blueprint
  ... not found»). Role, fortitude, luck, accuracy, colour, `ExtraWeapons` (her sniper rifle), the balance config and the
  arsenal still come from the entry; save / load restores members by name / role onto the spawned squad, so a member keeps
  its class. Default: the Medic-Sapper -> `/Game/Characters/Operatives/BP_Operative_FemaleSoldier` (C++ default, no asset
  edit needed). Steps: (1) Content Browser `Content/Characters/Operatives` -> right-click **BP_Operative** -> *Create Child
  Blueprint Class* -> name it exactly **BP_Operative_FemaleSoldier**; (2) open it, select the inherited **Mesh** component ->
  Skeletal Mesh Asset = `SK_Female_soldier` (Anim Class stays ABP_Operative); (3) add the extra parts as Skeletal Mesh
  components attached to Mesh (set *Leader Pose Component* to Mesh in the Construction Script, or use the same skeleton) /
  Static Mesh components on a socket; (4) Compile + Save; PIE: the log shows «Medic-Sapper: member Blueprint
  BP_Operative_FemaleSoldier_C». Another name or another member: the game mode has no Blueprint of its own, so change the
  C++ default in `ACodexTacticsGameMode::ACodexTacticsGameMode` (or ask Claude). Note 2026-10-11: the main folder holds an
  untracked `BP_FemleSoldier` (typo; child of BP_Operative with `SK_Female_Soldier_Backpack`) - it is NOT picked up until it is
  renamed to `BP_Operative_FemaleSoldier`. Checks: `CodexTactics.Core.SquadSpawn.*`, `SniperSmoke` stage 0 (class + body
  for both branches).

- **Tactical heat data (Sprint 15, 2026-10-11).** `Content/Data/Survival/tactical_heat.json` (staged as UFS) holds every value of
  `FTacticalHeatConfig` (snake_case keys, cm / s / cold points / AP): heat pack 35 / 45 s / x0.5 / 1 AP / stack 3; campfire 350 cm /
  75 s / 4 rounds / wind x0.8 from Z 140 / 1 match + 1 firewood or 10 fuel / noise 15 m every 10 s / 3 AP; stove 220 cm / 180 s gas /
  15 s per round / 2 AP / pack-up 1.5 s / 1 each; flare 180 cm / 40 s / 2 rounds / throw 20 m / at feet within 2 m / reveal 10 m /
  3 carried; puddle core 120 / ring 350 cm / 12 %/s / 12-15 s / 1 round / 10 dps (pulse 0.5 s) + Burning 3 s x 5 / barrel puddle on;
  new sources' heat tolerance 0 (old sources keep Godot's 50 cm). `starting_loadout` per role (commander 1 pack + 1 firewood + 1 flare,
  engineer 1 pack + 1 firewood + the stove (full canister), medic-sapper 2 packs + 1 flare). Loot: `FLootContents` HeatPacks 1 / Firewood 1 /
  Flares 0 per crate by default (editable per crate). Save keys (squad): `heat_packs_count`, `firewood_count`, `flares_count`,
  `stoves_count`, `stove_gas_seconds`, `heat_buffer_left`; objects: `heat_burning` / `heat_burnt_out` / `heat_time_left` /
  `heat_round_seconds`; world: `heat_sources` (flares, puddles).
- **Vision cones - how to switch them on (user request 2026-10-11).** Off by default (as in Godot's shipped config).
  (1) All operatives: open `/Game/Characters/Operatives/BP_Operative` -> Class Defaults -> Details, category
  **CodexTactics | Vision Cone** -> tick **Show Vision Cone** (also: `Vision Cone Range From Weapon` on = the cone shows the
  weapon's reach x stance; `Vision Cone Range Cm` 1400 without a weapon; `Vision Cone Half Angle Deg` 35). The squad is
  spawned from BP_Operative, so the class default is the switch for the squad. (2) All enemies of one type: open
  `/Game/Characters/Enemies/<Type>/BP_Enemy_<Type>` (Hound, Brute, Frostbitten, Cutter, Marksman, ...) -> Class Defaults ->
  **CodexTactics | Vision Cone** -> **Show Vision Cone** (`Show Vision Cone When Engaged`: keep the red cone while it hunts).
  (3) One enemy placed in a level: select it in the viewport / Outliner -> Details -> **CodexTactics | Vision Cone** ->
  **Show Vision Cone** (per instance). (4) Look: select the **VisionCone** component (Components panel of the BP) ->
  **Vision Cone** category: `Cone Material` (empty = engine translucent fallback), `Fill Alpha` 0.22, `Outline Alpha` 0.85,
  `Segments` 32, `Height Above Feet Cm` 8, `Outline Width Cm` 8. (5) Console in PIE / game (`~`): `Codex.Debug.VisionCones 1`
  every cone on, `0` every cone off, `-1` (default) each actor's own setting. Nothing was saved into the BPs by the port.

- **M16 range 25 m (user decision 2026-10-11; was Godot rifle_m16.tres 14 m).** `Content/Data/Weapons/weapons_tuning.json` "m16"
  (master copy, applied at StartPlay; `Scripts/Editor/apply_weapon_tuning.py` [`CODEX_WEAPONS=m16`] writes it onto
  `DA_Weapon_m16` so the Details match): `attack_range_m` 14 -> 25 (prone x1.35 = 33.75 m, crouched x1.15 = 28.75 m, high
  ground x its multiplier), `max_range_cells` 5 -> 8 (turn-based; the sniper keeps 30 m / 10 cells), `base_hit_chances` per
  1.5 m cell (real time reads it by round(m / 1.5), clamped to the last entry) 0.95 0.85 0.75 0.65 0.5 -> 0.95 0.85 0.75 0.65
  0.5 0.5 0.5 0.5 0.5 0.47 0.44 0.41 0.38 0.35 0.32 0.29 0.26 (no change inside the old 14 m; 14-25 m falls to 0.26),
  `distance_damage_multipliers` 17 x 1. Everything else derives from the weapon: auto fire / fire postures
  (`EvaluateShotLine`), blind fire, autonomy target reach (x1.3), the bot's rifle range, the turn-based attack pattern and
  overlay, the operative vision cone. Not changed: `SquadAutonomy::ThreatRangeCm` 25 m, `CoverDecisionRules::BlindFireMaxRangeCm`
  25 m, the turret 12 m. Gameplay note: the marksman's 20-35 m band (`enemy_weapons.marksman_rifle.preferred_*`) is now partly
  inside the squad's reach (standing 25 m, prone 33.75 m) - tune it in the Wave Editor if he becomes too easy.

- **Sudden horde start data (2026-10-11).** Level JSON per wave `"sudden_start"` (bool, default true when missing; Wave Editor
  card button «ВНЕЗАПНАЯ АТАКА» / «С ПОДГОТОВКОЙ»): false keeps the old start of that wave. `prep_phase_duration` /
  `wave_rest_duration` stay parsed but only matter for such waves. No level sets it yet, so every horde wave starts suddenly.
  Tuning is console-only for now (`Codex.HordeIntro.*`): SuddenStart -1, Enabled -1, PreTriggerTimeDilation 1, MassMovingFraction
  0.6, MassMinSpeed 250, MassHoldSeconds 0.4, MinDelaySeconds 2.5, MaxWaitSeconds 6, FlyoverTimeDilation 0.2, WideShotSeconds 3,
  RepeatWideShotSeconds 2.5, TypeShotSeconds 2.5, ShotBlendSeconds 0.7, ReturnBlendSeconds 1, TypeShotDistance 700,
  WideMinDistance 2000, WideMaxDistance 4000, WideFocusBias 0.8, ViewSideDegrees 25, AlertSeconds 3. Each wave logs a
  `[HordeIntro] N s: ... moving, mean ... cm/s, ... % running at the squad` trace for tuning.

- **Narrative pipeline (2026-10-08; all in-game text is English, Russian returns with a later localization pass).**
  Writer workflow in 3 steps: (1) fill the Google Sheet (columns `SequenceID, LineIndex, Speaker_EN, Text_EN, DelayAfter, ...`;
  sequence ids = `DA_Dialogue*` asset names); (2) `python Scripts/Narrative/sync_narrative.py` (writes
  `Content/Data/Narrative/narrative_manifest.json`; `--local` rebuilds from `dialogues.csv`/`glossary.csv`); (3) restart the game / PIE.
  No re-import and no editor needed: `FNarrativeManifest` (`Data/NarrativeManifest.*`) reads the JSON at runtime (staged via
  `DirectoriesToAlwaysStageAsUFS`), `UDialogueSubsystem::ResolveSequence` swaps the lines of a `DA_Dialogue*` asset for the manifest
  lines (speaker_en / text_en / delay; the asset only supplies the recruitment flag). A missing sequence / empty `text_en` shows
  `[EN missing: <seq>#<n>]` and logs a warning; Cyrillic asset text is never shown. Level-embedded notes (`ANarrativeElementActor`)
  read sequence `<ActorName>` the same way. Open list: `docs/port/narrative_missing_en.md`. Glossary names:
  `FNarrativeManifest::GetGlossaryName(key)`. Tests: `CodexTactics.Dialogue.Manifest.*`; smokes `DialogueSmoke`, `NarrativeSmoke`.
  `Scripts/Editor/apply_english_display_names.py` (headless) copies English names from LevelJson / weapons_tuning.json onto the
  `DA_Level_*` / `DA_Weapon_*` assets (LevelJson + weapons_tuning.json were translated; DA assets still hold Russian names until it is run
  with the editor closed).

- Operatives (user art, 2026-09-30): BP_Operative uses the user's `/Game/Post_Apo_Survivor` mesh (UE4 mannequin rig) and
  the `/Game/RifleAnims` pack. `Scripts/Editor/setup_operative_rifle_animation.py` makes the two UE4_Mannequin_Skeleton
  assets compatible, builds ABP_Operative's graph with `UOperativeAnimGraphLibrary` (editor module: stand / crouch / aim
  blend spaces by Direction + speed axis, prone = crouch set for now, cached locomotion, upper-body Layered blend from
  spine_01 with the pack's "Fire" montage slot; fire montages and the reload clip stretched to the reload time are
  played by `UOperativeAnimInstance`) and sets `AOperativeCharacter::SquadOutfits` (hoodie + pants: base / _Inst_2nd /
  _Inst_3rd for commander / engineer / medic-sapper). Re-running keeps hand edits: the graph is built only while empty (`CountAnimGraphNodes`; CODEX_REBUILD_ANIM_GRAPHS=1 forces it), clips / outfits only while unset.
  The old Explorer clips were deleted by the user; the M16 offset on hand_r is still the Explorer one.
- Enemies (user art, 2026-09-30): `Scripts/Editor/setup_enemy_animation.py` builds `/Game/Characters/Enemies/<Type>/`
  ABP_Enemy_* (graph: idle / walk / run sequence players + full-body slot; clips on the class defaults) and BP_Enemy_*
  for HOUND = Combat_Dog, BRUTE = Mutant_monster_1, FROSTBITTEN = mutant_monster_2, CUTTER = Biochemical_Monster_2 (ue4
  set, the only one with a death clip) — the mapping follows the clip names in Godot resources/enemies/anims/*.tres.
  `UEnemyAnimInstance` plays attacks / hits / the pounce / the held death; `ACodexTacticsGameMode::EnemyClasses` spawns
  them. The Blueprints own their look (user decision 2026-09-30): capsule, mesh offset / rotation / scale are authored in
  BP_Enemy_* (the script wrote the Godot per-type values once — hound / cutter 35/60 cm, frostbitten 40/90, brute 60/120,
  model scale 0.8 / 0.85 / 1.0 / 1.4 — and tags the CDO `LookAuthored`); `AEnemyCharacter::ApplyArchetypeDefaults` only
  sizes the C++ placeholder body. Stats stay Godot balance data. Re-running the script keeps every hand edit. With a death
  clip the body stays `DeathDecayDelay` 5 s (Godot death_decay_delay), otherwise 2 s.
- Placeholder meshes (engine shapes) for barrels, barricades, mines, quest objects; the user will replace them.
