  hands / pose), see §8.

### APC: how the user places it / makes L_VehicleTest / nav for vehicles (P0 + P1, 2026-10-12)

1. **The Blueprint (once, editor CLOSED):** `UnrealEditor-Cmd.exe "<repo>/CodexTactics.uproject" -run=pythonscript
   -script="<repo>/Scripts/Editor/setup_apc_vehicle.py" -unattended -nullrhi` creates `/Game/Vehicles/BP_APC_BTR82` (child of
   `AVehiclePawn`, Mesh = `SK_East_APC_BTR82`, Anim Class = `ABP_East_APC_BTR82`; the vendor folder is untouched; re-running keeps
   your edits). Or by hand: Content Browser -> Blueprint Class -> parent **VehiclePawn**, name it `BP_APC_BTR82` in `Content/Vehicles`,
   select **Mesh** -> Skeletal Mesh Asset `SK_East_APC_BTR82`, Anim Class `ABP_East_APC_BTR82`. Without the BP a dropped
   `VehiclePawn` (C++) shows the same vendor model by soft path. Polish in the BP: the events **On Visual Drive** (speed, steer),
   **On Doors Changed**, **On Engine Changed** (sound, dust FX); the ABP variables C++ writes are `WheelSpeed` (deg/s), `WheelsAngle`
   (deg), `DoorAngle` (0 / `Door Open Angle` 80) - rename them in **Visual** if the ABP changes.
2. **Tunables (Details, CodexTactics | Vehicle):** Drive Config (cruise / rush / reverse speed, acceleration, braking, **Min Turn Radius
   Cm** 700, look-ahead, reverse distance 15 m), Crew Config (passenger seats 5, board range 30 m, board 2 s / get out 1 s, exit
   offset), Engine Config (noise radii 20 / 40 / 55 m, cabin heat radius 3.5 m), **Hull Centre / Hull Half Extent** (the collision box,
   shown light blue), **Entry Points** (doors, pivot space), Crew Attach Offset, Ground Probes.
3. **Make `L_VehicleTest`:** a new level (File -> New Level -> Basic, or duplicate L_PatrolTest), a big flat floor (60 x 60 m or a
   landscape), **a NavMeshBoundsVolume** over the drivable area with the actor **Tag `VehicleNav`** (Details -> Actor -> Tags) - only
   tagged volumes (or a volume with only **Vehicle** ticked under Navigation -> Supported Agents) build the vehicle navmesh, every
   other volume keeps only the infantry one (no cost on maps without vehicles). A PlayerStart; drop **BP_APC_BTR82** 10-20 m from it,
   wheels on the ground (it snaps to the floor at BeginPlay). Walls / wrecks / gates as obstacles (>= 5 m wide passages: the vehicle
   agent's radius is 2 m). **Blocked spots** (thin ice, narrow alleys, steps): a **NavModifierVolume** with Area Class
   **NavArea_NoVehicle** - only the vehicle avoids it; make it ~2 m bigger than the spot (areas are not eroded: the hull centre stays
   out, half the hull may overhang). Press **P** in the editor to see the navmeshes (the vehicle one is drawn in orange).
4. **Play:** select an operative, **Y** = he boards (walks to the nearest door, ~2 s), **Alt+Y** = the whole squad; seats fill
   automatically (Engineer drives, Commander guns, the rest ride; anyone if absent). With the leader aboard **LMB on the ground** =
   drive there (double click = full speed; a short goal behind = reverse; the camera follows). **Y** again = the selected get out,
   **Alt+Y** = everyone (they exit on the side away from known enemies and take cover at the hull). Clicking the APC itself is still
   "take cover at its hull". The action bar shows **BOARD [Y]** near a vehicle and **EXIT [Y]** aboard. Limits (kept by the user
   2026-10-12): no driving / boarding in the tactical pause or turn-based (feed line), turn-based entry refused while anyone is
   aboard, no damage yet (P4), engine noise only breaks patrols.
   **P2 (2026-10-12):** boarding / getting out is a quick fade to black (no climbing animation; one fade for the group). With a
   second operative aboard (gunner seat) the **turret** fires on its own by the gunner's fire posture (`,` `.` `/`), **Ctrl+click**
   an enemy = its target; the HUD shows `TURRET AGG 30mm 57/60 MG heat 40%`. Nothing within 4 m of the hull can be shot (drive off or
   get out). Driving through small enemies (>= 1.5 m/s) **runs them over**; a Brute is knocked down at >= 4 m/s and stops the APC;
   operatives are never run over.
   **P2 tunables:** Details -> **Turret** component -> Config (cannon / MG / mount numbers; `weapons_tuning.json`
   "vehicle_weapons.apc_turret" overrides at play — edit the JSON or the Wave Editor file, it is the master copy at runtime; shells
   per map also by level JSON `"vehicle": {"shells": N}`); **Crush Config** (crushable / rammed archetypes, crush 1.5 m/s, knockdown
   4 m/s, ram damage, slowdowns, the Brute's fall play rate 0.65 / downed x1.5, run over a downed Brute) - JSON "apc_ram" overrides;
   **Fade Config** (on / off, 0.25 / 0.15 / 0.25 s, max hold 1 s, group wait 8 s). Polish events in BP_APC_BTR82: **On
   Turret Fired** (weapon, muzzle: the cannon / MG sound), **On Ran Over** (victim, location: crunch + blood / snow FX).
   **P4 hull (2026-10-12):** the APC has **1200 HP**; the HUD shows `HULL 840/1200` while the leader is aboard and a label over a
   damaged / disabled hull. Hounds / frostbitten / cutters scratch it (x0.1), a Brute's blow and blasts (barrels, grenades, traps)
   hurt in full, spitter acid x0.5, the marksman x0.25, a **mine or tripwire under the wheels = 250**, a fast ram on a Brute 30.
   Enemies go for the APC when it is manned and the closest threat; the crew inside is never hit. At 0 HP it is **disabled**: engine
   off, no driving, the turret silent, the damaged model + smoke, the crew jumps out (fade) on the safe side into hull cover.
   **Y** near it (the action bar shows **REPAIR [Y]**) sends the **Engineer** (select him, or Alt+Y): he walks to the hull and
   works ~5 s -> running again at 50 %. **Ctrl+click a barrel / mine / trapped object** while aboard with a gunner = one 30 mm shell
   (it blows up like an operative's shot; not within 4 m of the hull, not beyond 40 m, needs a line). Tunables: Details ->
   **Hull Config** (`weapons_tuning.json` `vehicle_weapons.apc_hull` overrides at play); the **Damaged Mesh** / **Damaged Smoke**
   components (empty = the vendor damaged mesh / wreck smoke; move them in the BP if the wreck sits off); BP events **On Hull
   Damaged** (sparks / clang) and **On Disabled Changed**.
5. **Nav agents (DefaultEngine.ini, for any map):** `SupportedAgents` = `Default` (35 / 144: the engine's former implicit agent,
   infantry unchanged) + `Vehicle` (200 / 300, step 40, `bCanFly=True` so no ground pawn ever picks it). Keep the order (the nav areas
   address the agents by index 0 / 1). Opening an old map shows no change except the tag rule above.

### How to place a PMC tripwire (Demo 01 G3, 2026-10-12)

A hostile (PMC) tripwire is a level trap: hidden until an operative spots it, set off only by operatives, disarmable by anyone.
1. **Place it:** Place Actors -> search **PMC Tripwire** (class `APMCTripwireActor`, a child of `ATripwireActor` with Faction PMC,
   Armed At Level Start and Hidden Until Spotted on). Drop it at the doorway / gap; rotate it so the wire runs across the passage
   (the wire is drawn in the editor between the two handles).
2. **Anchors:** select the actor -> two diamond handles **AnchorA** / **AnchorB** in the viewport (actor space, `MakeEditWidget`);
   drag them to the two posts / door jambs (span 1-5 m, a longer / shorter one is laid anyway with a log warning). At BeginPlay each
   anchor is projected down onto the WorldStatic ground below it and the wire hangs 30 cm above (prone crawls under).
   `Anchor A/B Bracket` = a bracket on an object instead of a peg (look only).
3. **Details -> CodexTactics | Tripwire** (all per instance, defaults from `TripwireRules`): `Faction` (Squad / PMC), `Armed At Level
   Start`, `Hidden Until Spotted`; **Charge**: `Blast Damage` 140, `Blast Radius Cm` 450, `Squad Damage Share` 0.75 (operatives take
   105 at the centre, falloff 0.5), `Grenades Recovered` 2 (given to the disarmer); **PMC**: `Spot Range Cm` 450 + `Sapper Spot Bonus
   Cm` 150 (the mine rule), `Disarm Seconds Sapper / Engineer / Other` 3 / 4 / 5, `Turn Based Disarm AP` 2.
4. **In game:** hidden = not drawn, not clickable, no path cost; an operative within the spot range of the wire (any stance; outside the
   wave fight, as mines) reveals it: the squad stops, «TRIPWIRE SPOTTED!», feed line, `TripwireSpotted`; then it is red with a «PMC
   Tripwire» label and the squad's paths go round it. Click it -> menu «Disarm (N s)»: ANY operative crouches over it for 3 / 4 / 5 s
   (sapper / engineer / others) and rolls the mine formula `DeployableRules::CalculateDefusal` crouched (+10): role base 60 / 45 / 35
   + luck / 2 - cold (-15 / -30 / -50 above 25 / 50 / 75 %) - 25 per failed attempt; warm defaults: Medic-Sapper 87.5 %, Engineer 70 %,
   Commander 57.5 % (frozen 80 %: 37.5 / 20 / 7.5 %). The mine failure rule: a dangerous attempt (< 45 %) first only warns, a miss
   slips (try again) or detonates (40 % / 80 % risk); a detonation pulls the pin (0.25 s, blast). Success: removed, +2 grenades,
   `TripwireDisarmed`. Enemies walk through it; a standing / crouched operative stepping on it trips it (`TripwireTriggered`).
   **Turn-based:** a click on the spotted wire within 2.2 m of the active operative = one attempt for 2 AP (resolved at once,
   `HandleTripwireClick`); the wire keeps ticking on the grid, so a grid walk across it trips it.
5. **Saved:** faction, spotted, anchors, arming, failed attempts / warning (a disarmed / blown wire stays gone; a hidden wire loads hidden).
6. The squad's own wire (drawer, two clicks, Medic-Sapper only, 1.5 s arming, friendly fire) is unchanged.

### ABP: left-hand IK (the user adds the node; C++ side done 2026-10-07)

Why: the M4 cover pack's clips hold their own M4 differently in the right hand — on our rifle (`m16_01`, rigid on
`hand_r`, offset tuned for RifleAnims) their left hand ends 38-42 cm off the handguard (RifleAnims: ~7 cm). The rifle
mesh has the socket `LeftHandGrip` (weapon-mesh space loc (-5.8, 4.4, 54.5), rot P 58.7 / Y -85.2 / R -170.8 = the
AS_Rifle_Aim left hand; `Scripts/Editor/add_weapon_left_hand_socket.py`). `UOperativeAnimInstance` exposes
`LeftHandIKOffset` (the grip in **hand_r bone space**, ≈ (-26.9, 9.1, -1.1)), `LeftHandIKRotation`, `LeftHandIKAlpha`
(smoothstep 0↔1 over `LeftHandIKBlendSeconds` 0.15 s; 0 with no grip socket / melee weapon, weapon hidden, reloading,
grenade throw and hit reaction windows, vaulting, dead, prone — crawl clips put the hand on the ground, prone aim
already holds the grip at 8-9 cm), `bLeftHandIKGripValid`, toggle `bLeftHandIK`. Graph in `ABP_Operative` (and Rifle2 if used):

1. AnimGraph: after the `FullBody` slot node (and after `UpperBody`, whichever is last), before Output Pose (after an
   Inertialization node, if one is added later): a **Two Bone IK** node.
2. IK Bone: `hand_l` (the chain hand_l → lowerarm_l → upperarm_l follows).
3. Effector: Location Space = **Bone Space**, Effector Target = **hand_r**; expose `Effector Location` as a pin ← the
   anim variable `LeftHandIKOffset`.
4. Joint Target: Location Space = **Parent Bone Space**, Joint Target = **lowerarm_l**, location (0, 0, 0) (the elbow
   stays where the clip has it); if the elbow flips, (0, -30, -30) in `upperarm_l` space.
5. Allow Stretching off, Take Rotation from Effector Space off, Maintain Effector Rel Rot off.
6. Alpha Input Type = Float Value, `Alpha` pin ← `LeftHandIKAlpha`.
7. Optional (hand rotation): a **Transform (Modify) Bone** on `hand_l` after it: Rotation Mode = Replace, Rotation Space =
   Bone Space (relative to hand_r is not offered: use World Space with a computed rotation later if needed), Alpha ←
   `LeftHandIKAlpha`; start without it.
**2026-10-07 PIE feedback, diagnosed:** (A) the user's node reads `LeftHandIKAlpha` but its **Effector Location pin is NOT
bound to `LeftHandIKOffset`** (the asset references no such variable) — with the default (0,0,0) in hand_r bone space it
pulls the left hand onto the RIGHT hand (grip / magazine). CoverCrouchSmoke measures it: final hand_l -> target 21.6 cm
with alpha 1 vs 36.8 cm with alpha 0 ("the node moves the hand but not onto the target"). Bind the pin. (A2) the user's
socket on the handguard (loc (2.45, 0.04, 73.3) in mesh space) is 72-74 cm from `upperarm_l` on the pack's fire stances,
the Two Bone IK chain reaches 57 cm: the C++ slides the target back along the barrel into 95 % of reach
(`LeftHandIKReachFraction`, `LeftHandIKMaxSlideCm` 40, `LeftHandIKSlideCm`; ~29 cm = the receiver). To reach the handguard
itself use a **FABRIK** from `clavicle_l` instead (reach 74 cm, the socket 68.5 cm away) and set the ABP class default
`LeftHandIKChainRoot = clavicle_l`. (B) the straight arm in the cover idle came from the unbound pin (target = hand_r):
with the pin bound (user, 14:06) the IK is ON in every pose where the right hand holds the rifle (cover idle /
look-around / shimmy / enter / stance switches included); off only for reload, grenade, hit reaction, vault, death,
hidden weapon, prone and clips listed in `LeftHandIKFreeClips` (none needed: no pack clip puts the left hand on the
wall). A target still beyond reach after the slide fades the alpha over `LeftHandIKFadeCm` 15 cm (`ReachFade`,
`LeftHandIKExcessCm`). Predicted (read-only commandlet, the user's socket): with the Two Bone IK chain (57 cm) every
cover pose is reachable after a 26-37 cm slide standing / 6-21 cm crouched — the standing hand lands on the receiver,
behind the handguard; with a FABRIK from clavicle_l (73 cm) the slide is <= 12 cm standing and 0-1 cm in the fire
stances: **switch to the FABRIK for a hand on the handguard**. `CodexTactics.LeftHandIKSmoke` measures every pose with
the real ABP (final hand -> target, arm extension, hand on the wall).
Debug: `Codex.Debug.LeftHandIK 1` draws red = socket, green = IK target, yellow = hand_l, blue = the chain root.
FABRIK settings (alternative to Two Bone IK): Tip Bone `hand_l`, Root Bone `clavicle_l`, Effector Transform Space = Bone
Space, Effector Target = `hand_r`, Effector Transform pin <- Make Transform (Location = `LeftHandIKOffset`), Effector
Rotation Source = Keep Local Rotation, Precision 1, Max Iterations 10, Alpha <- `LeftHandIKAlpha`.
Note: the IK puts the WRIST (hand_l origin) on the socket; the palm lies ~8 cm further along the hand — place the socket
where the wrist should be (a little behind / below the palm's spot on the handguard).
The `ik_hand_*` bones do not help: in the M4 clips `ik_hand_l` equals `hand_l` (they follow the pack's own hand).

### ABP: aim offset (the user adds the node; C++ side done 2026-10-07)

Why: a hound running at the operative is LOWER than his eyes, but every clip aims level. The tracer already flies from the
muzzle to the target's capsule centre (`ShootAtTarget` / `ShootAtObject`: `End = Target->GetActorLocation()`), so the shot's
geometry was right; only the pose was level. The user's asset `/Game/RifleAnims/Animations/AimOffsets/AO_Idle/AO1D_Rifle_Idle`
(read-only commandlet 2026-10-07): class `AimOffsetBlendSpace1D`, skeleton `UE4_Mannequin_Skeleton` (the operative's), ONE axis
**"Pitch" -90..90** (grid 4, snap on), 3 samples `AS_Rifle_Idle_FwdDown` (-90), `FwdCenter` (0), `FwdUp` (+90) = one-frame
clips, **additive `AAT_RotationOffsetMeshSpace`**, ref pose = frame 0 of `AS_Rifle_Idle` (also the asset's preview base pose).
So: + = up, - = down; only mesh-space rotation offsets, applied on top of whatever pose comes in. Interpolation is linear
between 0 and +-90 only: 18 deg = 20 % of the FwdDown pose - if the visible lean is too weak, move the samples in the AO asset
(e.g. to +-45 and clamp `AimPitchClampDegrees` to 45), not the C++.

C++ (`UOperativeAnimInstance::UpdateAimOffset`, pure rules `AimOffsetRules`): `AimPitch` (deg, + up / - down; eased by
`AimPitchInterpSpeed` 10), `AimPitchTarget` (raw), `bHasAimTarget`, `AimOffsetAlpha` (0..1 over `AimOffsetBlendSeconds` 0.2 s),
`bAimOffsetActive`, `AimPitchClampDegrees` (90 = the AO range), `bAimOffset`, `bAimOffsetInCover`. The pitch is measured from
`AOperativeCharacter::GetMuzzleLocation()` (the stance's muzzle height: stable, it does not move with the pose the AO bends) to
`AOperativeCharacter::GetAimTargetPoint()` = the target's capsule centre (the tracer's end, a blind shot's silhouette): the
target of a shot fired within `AimTargetHoldSeconds` (1 s: covers turn-based shots and targeted shots at barrels / mines), else
`CurrentCombatTarget` (manual priority, Commander Mode target or the closest visible enemy; all of them end there). No target:
pitch eases to 0. Alpha is 0 while reloading, in the grenade-throw / hit-reaction windows, vaulting, sprinting, prone, dead,
with a melee weapon or a hidden weapon (and in cover when `bAimOffsetInCover` is off).

Graph in `ABP_Operative` (and Rifle2 if used):
1. AnimGraph: after the last slot node (`FullBody`, then `UpperBody`) and BEFORE the left-hand Two Bone IK node (which stays
   last): the AO is additive mesh-space, so it must see the final pose incl. the cover clips that play on FullBody, and the
   left-hand IK then re-pins `hand_l` onto the handguard of the bent rifle. Order: ... -> FullBody -> UpperBody ->
   **Aim Offset Player** -> Two Bone IK (hand_l) -> Output Pose.
2. Add the node: right-click -> *Aim Offset Player* (or drag `AO1D_Rifle_Idle` from the content browser into the graph);
   Aim Offset = `AO1D_Rifle_Idle`. Connect the incoming pose to its **Base Pose** input.
3. Expose the pins (Details -> tick the pin boxes or Pin Options): the axis pin **X** (the Pitch axis; for a 1D asset the node
   offers X only) <- variable `AimPitch`; **Alpha** (Alpha Input Type = Float Value) <- `AimOffsetAlpha`. Nothing else
   (Play Rate 1, Use Blend Space Settings off is fine).
4. Test in PIE: a hound running at him from the open - the barrel dips as it comes close; a target on higher ground lifts it.
   Test both, then cover: the M4 cover fire poses get the offset on top (corner shot at a lower enemy). If a cover clip twists
   the spine oddly, untick `bAimOffsetInCover` (ABP class defaults) - alpha fades to 0 in cover.
5. If the legs / pelvis shift (the AO samples rotate all of the spine chain from pelvis): use **Layered blend per bone**
   instead - Base = the pose, Blend Pose 0 = Aim Offset Player on the same pose, Mesh Space Rotation Blend ON, branch filter
   `spine_01`, Blend Depth 0, Alpha <- `AimOffsetAlpha`; X <- `AimPitch` on the AO node (its own alpha 1).
Caveats: the AO needs the node's base pose to be the same skeleton (UE4_Mannequin_Skeleton - it is); on a pose that is not the
standing idle (crouch, cover) the offset is the same rotation, so check the crouched fire stance too; the node does nothing
in the editor preview until the PIE variables feed it. Checks: `CodexTactics.Anim.AimOffset.PitchToTarget / AlphaRules`,
`CodexTactics.AimOffsetSmoke` (hound low 6 m / high 3 m, hold, sprint fade).

**ABP: 2D aim offset AO_Rifle_Aim (yaw + pitch; user request 2026-10-07; C++ done, the user swaps the node).** Asset
`/Game/RifleAnims/Animations/AimOffsets/AO_Aim/NotUsed_AimOffset2D/AO_Rifle_Aim` (read-only commandlet 2026-10-07):
`AimOffsetBlendSpace`, axis 0 **"Yaw" -180..180**, axis 1 **"Pitch" -90..90**, 15 samples `AS_Rifle_IdleAim_{Fwd,Left,Right,
BackL,BackR}{Center,Up,Down}` - **RightCenter at Yaw +90, LeftCenter at -90** (checked on the sample poses: in RightCenter the
rifle line hand_r -> hand_l turns 90 deg to the character's right), so **+AimYaw = to his right** (UE yaw, clockwise from above),
same sign as the C++. Additive mesh space on `AS_Rifle_Aim`, skeleton UE4_Mannequin_Skeleton (the operative's).
C++: `UOperativeAnimInstance::AimYaw` (deg, + right, eased by `AimYawInterpSpeed` 12, clamped to +-`AimYawClampDegrees` 60;
`AimYawTarget` raw) = the angle from the CURRENT POSE's aim direction to the target: in the corner hold the corner fire
stance's lean-out direction (`CoverFacingRules::CornerAimDirection`, `AOperativeCharacter::CornerAimOutwardDeg` = 100 deg past
the along-wall line, measured on the real barrel of `cvr_std_fire_idle_L`: 100-104 deg), else the barrel's yaw (actor yaw +
`BarrelYawOffset` - the twist already applied). 0 in the plain cover idle and without a target. Alpha = `AimOffsetAlpha` (same
rules as the pitch). **`bAimOffsetYaw`** (ABP Class Defaults, default OFF): tick it once the 2D node is in - only then does the
gameplay count on the upper-body twist (the shot is allowed once the rest of the angle is within `AimConeDeg` 30 and the corner
hold claims targets within 60 + 30 - 5 deg of its aim); with the 1D node keep it off (the shot waits for the pose itself).
**Turn-based (user 2026-10-08):** the AO does not work well there, so `bAimOffsetInTurnBased` (ABP class defaults, default OFF) fades `AimOffsetAlpha` to 0 over `AimOffsetBlendSeconds` and zeroes the AimYaw / AimPitch targets while `UTurnBasedCombatSubsystem::IsActive()`; real time / tactical pause keep it and it eases back in. `bAimOffsetTurnBasedOff` (read-only) shows the state; `GetAimTwistLimitDeg` reports 0 then, so the shot cone and `FaceAimAt` rely on the body turn, not the twist (`GetAppliedAimYaw` is x alpha = 0).
Steps in `ABP_Operative`:
1. Select the existing **Aim Offset Player** node (AO1D_Rifle_Idle) -> Details -> Aim Offset: **AO_Rifle_Aim** (or delete it
   and drag AO_Rifle_Aim in at the same place). Keep it where it is: after `FullBody` -> `UpperBody`, **before the left-hand
   FABRIK / Two Bone IK** (which stays last, so `hand_l` re-pins on the twisted rifle). Base Pose <- the incoming pose.
2. Pins: **X (Yaw) <- `AimYaw`**, **Y (Pitch) <- `AimPitch`**, **Alpha <- `AimOffsetAlpha`** (Alpha Input Type = Float Value).
3. Class Defaults: tick **bAimOffsetYaw**; leave `AimYawClampDegrees` 60 (lower it if the twist looks too strong).
4. PIE: a hound running at his side while he holds a corner - the upper body turns to it up to 60 deg, beyond that he steps
   off the wall and turns his whole body; tracers always leave along the barrel (`CodexTactics.CornerHoldSmoke` logs the angle
   between barrel and target for every shot). If the legs twist, use the Layered-blend-per-bone variant of step 5 above.
Keeping the 1D node: nothing to do (`bAimOffsetYaw` off, `AimYaw` is simply unused). Checks:
`CodexTactics.Anim.AimOffset.YawToTarget`.

**ABP: grenade throw (user clips 2026-10-07).** The user's `/Game/Animations_Grenade/Grenade_01root_root_{Walk,RunAnd,Crouch,Prone}...Throw..._UE` clips (UE5 `SK_Mannequin` of the M4 pack, already compatible with the operative skeleton; in place, no root motion, no notifies; 2.04 / 2.96 / 2.54 / 2.75 s) play on `UpperBodySlot` = "Fire" (the graph's Layered blend per bone from spine_01, so the legs keep the locomotion; the full-body slot is not used). `Scripts/Editor/setup_operative_grenade_animation.py` assigns them to `GrenadeThrow{Walk,Run,Crouch,Prone}Animation` of ABP_Operative and ABP_Operative_Rifle2 (only while empty; CODEX_RESET_GRENADE_CLIPS=1 overwrites). Release = the throwing hand's (hand_r) peak speed, measured per frame: walk 1.28 s, run 1.73, crouch 0.89, prone 1.14 -> `GrenadeThrow*ReleaseSeconds` (anim instance class defaults, <= 0 = 70 % of the clip); the anim instance hands it to `AOperativeCharacter::GrenadeReleaseSeconds`, `GrenadeSubsystem::ThrowAt` passes `GrenadeRules::ReleaseDelay(duration, release)` to the grenade (hidden in the hand until then). Blend 0.15 in / 0.2 out (`GrenadeThrowBlendIn/OutSeconds`). The left-hand IK and aim offset are blocked for the whole clip (`LeftHandIKBlockSeconds`). Clip choice: prone / crouch by stance, else run when sprinting or > 300 cm/s, else walk. Check: `CodexTactics.GrenadeAnimSmoke`. Eyeball in PIE: the prone clip is a lying-down throw layered over the crawl from spine_01 (reload uses FullBody when prone).

**Knockdown values (user decisions 2026-10-11; `FKnockdownConfig` defaults + `Codex.Knockdown.*` CVars, no DataAsset).** Chance `KnockdownChance` 0.5 (`Codex.Knockdown.Chance`) for everyone: a qualifying heavy hit (>= 40 HP, Brute only on a crit) knocks down only when the roll (`FKnockdownHit::ChanceRoll`, rolled in `UKnockdownComponent::TryKnockDown`) is below it; a blast within 2.5 m, a pounce (hound run-up pounce, cutter leap landing) and the Frost Brute's heavy blow (user answer 2026-10-11: guaranteed, like the pounce; its qualify rules unchanged) always knock down. Durations: fall = clip (0.87 / 0.93 s), `DownedSeconds` 0.6, `GetUpSeconds` 0.8 at up to `GetUpMaxPlayRate` 2.1x (Revive_Back 0.8 s, Revive_Front 1.19 s) -> ~2.27 s on the ground (was ~3.35 s). Downed damage: one `DownedDamageMultiplier` 1.33 (`Codex.Knockdown.DownedDamage`) for any blow — shots, melee, blasts / traps — real time (`AOperativeCharacter::TakeHit` incl. its bypass path, `UHealthComponent::TakeDamage`) and turn-based (`ApplySquadHit` -> `TakeHit`, rounded); `EKnockdownBlow`, `DownedRanged/MeleeDamageMultiplier` and their CVars are gone. Turn-based: never a free stand-up; on his turn a lying operative may stay down (x1.33, no move / shot / stance / facing / barrel / relocate / deploy; items, unit switch and ending the turn work) or order «GET UP (2 AP) [Z]» (`UTurnBasedCombatSubsystem::GetUpActiveUnit`; the panel entry replaces the stance button only while he lies — falling / getting up the stance entry stays, disabled, so no empty GET UP button flashes); with < 2 AP it is refused with a feed line and he stays down. Enemies keep spending their whole turn on the get-up (`GetUpForTurn`). Smokes needing a guaranteed knockdown set `Codex.Knockdown.Chance 1` and restore it.

**ABP: knockdown (Sprint 14, 2026-10-08).** Nothing to add to the graph: `UKnockdownComponent` plays `/Game/Animations_KnockDown/AM_Knocked_*` (held), `AM_Revive_*` and `AM_Death_*` on the existing `FullBody` slot and stops `UpperBody`; `UOperativeAnimInstance::bKnockedDown` is available for a graph state if wanted. The UpperBody slot comes after FullBody in the graph, so the C++ blocks every upper-body clip while knocked down. Eyeball in PIE: Death_Back's start pose differs from Knocked_Back's end (pelvis ~19 cm lower, mirrored sideways; blended 0.2 s).

**ABP: hit reactions + death (2026-10-08).** Nothing to add to the graph. `Scripts/Editor/setup_operative_hit_animation.py` (only empty fields; `CODEX_RESET_HIT_CLIPS=1` overwrites) set ABP_Operative / ABP_Operative_Rifle2 Class Defaults: `HitStandAnimation` = `MM_HitReact_Front_Med_01`, `HitCrouchAnimation` = `MM_HitReact_Front_Lgt_02`, `PistolHitAnimation` = `MM_HitReact_Front_Lgt_01`, new `HitBackAnimation` = `MM_HitReact_Back_Med_01` (all `/Game/Characters/Mannequins/Anims/Rifle/HitReact`, UE5 SK_Mannequin, in place, 0.7-0.87 s); `HitProneAnimation` stays `Crawl_Hit_F`. They play on `UpperBodySlot` "Fire" (layered from spine_01: legs keep walking), the prone one on FullBody; tunables `HitReactionMinIntervalSeconds` 0.8, `HitReactionMinDamage` 1, `bHitReactionInCover` false (cover keeps its pose), `HitReactionBlendIn/OutSeconds` 0.08 / 0.2; left-hand IK + aim offset are off for the clip (`LeftHandIKBlockSeconds`). Deaths: `DeathStandAnimations` / `DeathCrouchAnimation` stay EMPTY on purpose — the pack's `MM_Death_*` end half-way down (pelvis 83-88 cm, measured) — so a standing / crouched death falls with the knockdown `AM_Knocked_Back` / `AM_Knocked_Front` (side of the killing blow) held as the corpse pose (`UKnockdownComponent::PlayDeathFall`); downed -> `AM_Death_*`, prone -> `Crawl_Death01`. Assign real lying death clips to the arrays to override.

### ABP: Female Soldier body + sniper rifle set (user request 2026-10-09)

**Body.** The Medic-Sapper (and only she) wears `/Game/Female_Soldier/Mesh/SK_Female_soldier`: `ACodexTacticsGameMode::SquadRoster[2].BodyMesh`
-> `AOperativeCharacter::ApplyBodyMeshOverride` before BeginPlay (BP_Operative untouched; the BP's index-based material overrides are
dropped, `SquadOutfits` / `RoleColorMaterialSlot` find no Post_Apo slot names on her, so she keeps the pack's own materials). Same
`ABP_Operative`. Skeleton facts (`Saved/Logs/inspect_female.json`, read-only commandlet): `Female_Soldier_Skeleton` = UE4 mannequin, 68
bones, the operative's Post_Apo UE4 skeleton minus `Backpack_01..03`, **0 parent differences**; ref pose head 159.6 vs 165.5 cm, hand_r
rotation within 0.5 deg, feet at Z 0 -> the BP's mesh offset (-87.6) fits (SniperSmoke: mesh origin 2.4 cm above the capsule bottom).
So the M16 offset on hand_r, the left-hand FABRIK from clavicle_l, the aim offset, cover, knockdown and death clips run on her by bone
name like on the men. The pack has **no UE5 variant** (only `Separated_Mesh` parts and UE4 ThirdPerson demo clips), the full mesh was
taken. `Scripts/Editor/setup_operative_sniper_animation.py` made `Female_Soldier_Skeleton` compatible with the Post_Apo skeleton and with
everything it lists (RifleAnims, Crawl A / Rifle / Pistol, Characters Manny, M4 Manny 196 = cover / grenade / knockdown, Sniper_Animation
SK_Mannequin), and Post_Apo with her (pack assets, saved in the main checkout through the junction). Runtime playback does not need the
flags (bone-name remapping); they are for the editor (preview / pickers).

**Sniper rifle.** `/Game/Data/Weapons/DA_Weapon_sniper_rifle` (`EWeaponHandling::SniperRifle`, `HandMesh` = the M16 model as a stand-in:
swap the model there, `bOverrideHandMeshTransform` + `HandMeshAttachTransform` for its grip offset, `HandMeshMuzzleOffset` when it has no
"Muzzle" socket); numbers in `weapons_tuning.json` "sniper_rifle" (Wave Editor). Only the Medic-Sapper carries it
(`FSquadMemberSpawn::ExtraWeapons`, `AOperativeCharacter::AddArsenalWeapon`: right after the M16, so X goes M16 -> sniper -> pistol...).
Rules (`Combat/SniperRules`): fires only kneeling (crouched) or prone and standing still; a direct order (priority target, Ctrl + click,
planned / turn-based shot) while moving stops her (`SniperOrderHoldSeconds` 3 s: the formation does not pull her away), standing she
kneels by herself, the shot waits for the kneel clip; the automatic fire of the postures does NOT stop a player's move order (user decision 2026-10-09, confirmed) (no shot
while she walks; once she stands still she kneels and fires); prone stays prone; standing idle with the rifle is allowed. Ctrl + click
objects wait for the kneel (`SniperPendingObject`, `SniperPendingShotSeconds` 6 s). Turn-based: standing the attack costs `StanceAPCost`
+ `AttackAPCost` (`UTurnBasedCombatSubsystem::GetActiveAttackCost`); without the AP the attack mode is not entered and AttackCell is
refused with a feed line; with it she kneels on the grid (stance AP spent) and fires. Commander Mode never stands a sniper up
(`SniperRules::AutonomyStance`). The shot cone uses the body's forward (the sniper clips aim along it), not the stand-in barrel.

**Clips** (`UOperativeAnimInstance` class defaults, `[stand, knee, prone]`, filled by `setup_operative_sniper_animation.py`, only empty
fields, `CODEX_RESET_SNIPER_CLIPS=1` overwrites): `SniperIdle` AS_Stand/Knee/Prone_Aim_Idle; `SniperAimStart` AS_Stand_Aim_Start (relaxed ->
aim, when she comes to rest standing), AS_Knee_Aim_Start (stand -> kneel, pelvis 95 -> 41 cm, 0.97 s), AS_Prone_Aim_Idle_Start (stand ->
prone, 2.63 s); `SniperAimEnd` AS_Stand_Aim_End, AS_Knee_Aim_End, AS_Prone_Aim_Idle_End (rise); `SniperFire` AS_*_Aim_Fire (0.5 s;
stand unused); `SniperBoltCycle` AS_*_Aim_Bullet_Reload (1.73 / 1.73 / 2.63 s, after a shot while rounds remain); `SniperReload`
AS_*_Aim_Magazine_Reload (3.3 / 3.3 / 4.0 s, stretched to the reload time); `SniperHitReact` AS_*_Aim_HitReact. **Nothing was added to
the AnimGraph**: the C++ layer (`OperativeAnimInstanceSniper.cpp`) plays them on the existing `FullBody` slot while the rifle is in her
hands, she stands still (< `SniperStillSpeed` 20 cm/s) out of cover, alive, not knocked down / vaulting / sprinting / carrying; walking
keeps the normal locomotion (no sniper walk clips). Knee <-> prone has no pack clip (the loops cross-blend 0.25 s). `bSniperPose` /
`bSniperWeapon` are exposed for your own graph states; `bUseSniperClips` turns the layer off. Left-hand IK stays on in the prone sniper
pose (off during the bolt); the rifle aim offset fades out in the sniper poses unless `bSniperAimOffsetWired`.
**Aim offsets (optional, your graph):** the script built `/Game/Characters/Operatives/Sniper/AimOffsets/AO_Sniper_Stand|Knee|Prone`
(the pack's Offset_F/L/R/U/D poses copied as additive mesh-space rotation offsets on the F pose; axes Yaw -180..180 / Pitch -90..90 like
AO_Rifle_Aim; the poses are only ~10 deg off centre (prone yaw ~14), so the samples sit at +-10 / +-14 and the offset saturates there).
To use them: Aim Offset Player node -> Details -> expose the **Blend Space** pin and bind it to `ActiveAimOffset` (= the stance's sniper
AO in a sniper pose, else `RifleAimOffset` = AO_Rifle_Aim), then tick **bSniperAimOffsetWired** in Class Defaults.

### Weather idle poses: cold idle, hand warming, sitting at the fire (user request 2026-10-12)

**Pack:** `/Game/90+_Weather_Animations` (untracked pack in the MAIN folder, junction in agents). NEW_version/Cold = 35 clips
(Hot 35 / Storm 24 / OLD_version 52 are for the later weather + day/night work). Sets (`AS_He_*` male, `AS_She_*` female): `Cold_1`
(standing, shivering), `Cold_Fire_1` (standing, hands out to a fire), `Cold_Fire_2` (sitting on the ground at a fire) - measured, loop
pelvis He 92 / 92 / 13 cm, She 96 / 95 / 35 cm. Each set: 1Start, 2Loop, 3Tranzit, 4Loop, 5Tranzit, 6End; exceptions: She Cold_Fire_1/2
name the second loop `5Loop`, **She Cold_1 has no 6End** (the pose blends out). No clip has root motion.
**Retarget:** the pack sits on its own UE5 Manny skeleton (161 bones). Bone-name playback on the UE4 Post_Apo skeleton (like the cover /
sniper clips) was measured wrong for these poses: reference poses differ (clavicle / upper arm ~24 deg, spine_01..03 7-11 deg local rot),
the sitting loop leaned the torso ~40 cm back and lifted the hands to the chest. So `Scripts/Editor/setup_operative_weather_animation.py`
IK-retargets the 35 clips with the M4 pack's `RTG_UE5Manny_UE4Manny` (its "Blend to Source" op switched off IN MEMORY - with it the
knees bent to ~85 % on the taller Post_Apo / Female meshes and her pelvis dropped 13 cm; the shared asset is never saved) onto
`SK_Post_Apo_Survivor_a` -> `/Game/Characters/Operatives/Anims/Weather/Male/AS_He_*` and `SK_Female_soldier` -> `.../Weather/Female/AS_She_*`.
Result per clip in `Saved/Logs/setup_operative_weather_animation.txt` (pack vs retargeted pelvis / head / hands / lowest foot / leg
extension: legs 0.91-1.01 of the mesh vs 0.94-1.02 in the pack, feet on the mesh's reference ground, hands within ~10 cm, scaled to the
taller bodies). No skeleton, no pack asset is modified. Re-run: `CODEX_RESET_WEATHER_CLIPS=1` (re-retargets + rewrites the class defaults),
`CODEX_WEATHER_DRYRUN=1` measures only.
**C++ (no graph change):** `UOperativeAnimInstance` class defaults `ColdIdleMale/Female`, `HandWarmMale/Female`, `SitWarmMale/Female`
(`FWarmIdleClipSet`: Start, LoopA, TransitAB, LoopB, TransitBA, End), `WarmIdleLoopsMin/Max` (2-4 loops before a transit),
`WarmIdleBlendSeconds` 0.3, `WarmIdleInterruptBlendSeconds` 0.2, `WarmIdleStillSpeed` 15, `bHideWeaponInWarmIdle` (the clips use both
hands; the rifle comes back when the pose ends), `bUseWarmIdleClips`. The layer (`OperativeAnimInstanceWarmIdle.cpp`) plays the set on
FullBodySlot; the female set is chosen when the mesh or skeleton name contains "Female" (recruits / Susanin = male). Graph hooks:
`bWarmIdleActive`, `WarmIdlePose`. **Nothing for you to do in the ABP** (the FullBody slot plays it); a later polish could put the
weapon on a back socket instead of hiding it.
**Rules (`Survival/WarmIdleRules`, `UWarmIdleSubsystem`):** exploration (and the post-combat return) only - never in a wave fight /
ambush / tactical pause / turn-based / cutscene / preparation. Gate: alive, not knocked down / frostbitten / in cover / carrying /
vaulting / panicking / raging, no task or use order, not moving, no threat (enemy in his cone / combat target / attacked in the last 5 s),
idle `warm_idle_delay_seconds` 2. Cold idle: no warm zone, standing, cold >= `warm_idle_cold_threshold` 40 (Chills; ends 5 below).
Barrel: everyone idle in its zone walks to a hand-warming place on a ring round it (`barrel_stand_distance` 125 cm, outside the 1.2 m
drum), faces it, `Cold_Fire_1`. Campfire / gas stove (user design): roles by cold - the coldest `warm_min_sitters` 2 (or all if fewer)
plus everyone >= `warm_extra_sitter_cold` 50 sit on the inner ring (campfire 115 / stove 95 cm; capped by the free seats), the rest stand
on the outer ring (175 / 150 cm, between the seats) warming their hands; roles are re-evaluated by the current cold when someone ARRIVES
or the fire is relit; when someone leaves the others keep their roles (only the coldest stander sits down if fewer than 2 remain). Spots:
`warm_spot_spacing` 85 cm apart, spread round the fire (the free slot farthest in angle from the taken ones), projected on the NavMesh and
probed for blocking geometry (blocked / unreachable spots skipped; after 3 failed walks he just stands in the zone), at least the source's
box + a capsule away; walk 160 cm/s; he faces the fire before the pose starts. Orders: any `OrderMoveTo` (player, interaction,
relocation) ends only THAT operative's pose at once (quick 0.2 s blend-out, no busy time); a parked follower keeps his spot while the
leader stays in that fire's warm zone (`USquadSubsystem::UpdateFollower` -> `ShouldFollowerHoldWarmSpot`), the leader leaving it takes
the squad along. Combat start / warm enough / fire out: the End clip (stands up / lowers the arms); threat / move: quick blend-out;
knockdown / death / cover: cut.
**Warming multiplier:** heat-source warming x `exploration_warmth_multiplier` 1.0, x `combat_warmth_multiplier` **0.5** in a wave fight /
ambush (any combat mode; a plain source 8 %/s x fortitude boost -> 4.6 %/s for the commander; preparation / closed-zone warming without a
heat source is not scaled). All values in `Content/Data/Survival/tactical_heat.json` (Unreal master; `FTacticalHeatConfig`).
**Verify:** `CodexTactics.Survival.WarmIdle.*` (8 tests), `WarmIdleSmoke` (cold idle He / She + order cut, barrel hand warming + End clip,
campfire 3 operatives: the two coldest sit on distinct inner seats facing the fire, the warmest stands on the outer ring, no flicker,
x1 warming; an order to one leaves the others seated; stove 2 sit / 1 stands; wave fight: End clips, no poses, x0.5 warming).
`WarmIdleSmoke shot` (rendered offscreen, `-RenderOffscreen -ResX=1280 -ResY=720`) saves stills to `Saved/Screenshots/WeatherAnim/`.

**Squad gathers at the fire (user decision + feedback 2026-10-12):** in EXPLORATION formation (`IsFormationActive`), while the LEADER holds a
warm spot at a barrel / campfire / stove (`AOperativeCharacter::WarmSpotSource`, i.e. he stood idle 2 s in the zone), `USquadSubsystem::UpdateFollower`
(at the repath rate) asks `UWarmIdleSubsystem::GetFollowerPullTarget` and walks the follower to a free place on that source's OUTER (stand) ring
instead of his formation slot (`WarmSpotWalkSpeed`); inside the zone he parks facing the fire, 2 s idle later the normal flow above gives him
his role (the coldest `warm_min_sitters` / >= `warm_extra_sitter_cold` sit on the inner ring, the rest stand on the outer ring right beside
them) and spot. So the formation radius effectively contracts to the fire's rings; campfire, stove and barrel (stand ring) behave the same.
Rule `WarmIdleRules::ShouldPullFollower` (`FWarmPullInput`): `warm_pull_followers` on, exploration (not combat / ambush / pause / turn-based /
cutscene: `IsExplorationPhase` + world AI pause), follower in the formation (solo mode and held followers never reach `UpdateFollower`, guards (T)
are not followers), leader at the fire, follower free (`CanHoldWarmPose`: alive, up, not frostbitten / in cover / carrying / vaulting / busy /
threatened / prone), no explicit order since the pull began (`GetMoveOrderSerial` changed -> that follower is opted out of THAT fire until the leader
leaves its zone), follower <= `warm_pull_max_distance` (1500 cm) from the source at the START (a running pull survives detours). The leader leaving the
warm zone ends the pull (followers follow the formation again; they stand up with the End clip). A fire that went out (barrel burnt out, stove spent) ends the pull at once (BarrelSmoke caught followers parked round a dead barrel). A pull the squad stops asking about lapses
after 3 s; it also ends at once outside exploration. Data: `tactical_heat.json` `warm_pull_followers` (bool), `warm_pull_max_distance`.
**Verify:** rule test `CodexTactics.Survival.WarmIdle.PullFollowers`; `WarmIdleSmoke` scenes 7 (campfire: the leader walks up to it, followers 9 / 11 m away
in formation -> both come in, the two coldest sit, the warmest stands on the outer ring beside them; leader walks away -> they follow again; then the
same at the gas stove; an order to one follower, a guard (T), solo mode keep that one out; wave fight pulls nobody).
**Smoke note (WarmIdleSmoke "squad of three", 2026-10-12):** the Medic-Sapper (max HP 120; Commander 140, Engineer 150 - the same with or without the user's
`BP_Operative_FemaleSoldier`) burned to death ~1 s after sitting at the stove: the barrel stage leaves a fire puddle (core 120 cm, 12-15 s, 10 dmg/s + Burning)
exactly where the later stages put the squad. Not a BP value: the smoke now removes puddles and heals the squad between stages (`ResetBodies`, logs the HP),
and `UHealthComponent::Die` logs `[Health] <actor> died (source '...', max HP ..) at ..` (no more silent deaths).

### Frontend: what the artist edits (2026-10-08)

Everything visual is an asset; the C++ only finds widgets **by name** (keep the names, restyle / move / animate freely;
`CodexTactics.UI.Frontend.DefaultTreeWidgetNames` lists them). Swap any screen for another WBP in Project Settings >
Game > Codex Frontend (`ScreenClasses`). The placeholders were generated by `Scripts/Editor/create_frontend_assets.py`
(re-running keeps existing assets; `CODEX_FRONTEND_REBUILD=1` rewrites the WBP trees — it would undo the artist's layout).

| Visual | Asset (`/Game/UI/Frontend/...`) | Names the code uses |
|---|---|---|
| Layer root (fades between screens: the stacks' Transition settings) | `WBP_PrimaryLayout` | `GameLayer`, `GameMenuLayer`, `MenuLayer`, `ModalLayer` |
| Every menu entry button (look per state: `Styles/BP_FrontendButtonStyle`, texts `Styles/BP_FrontendText_Normal / _Hovered / _Disabled`) | `WBP_MenuButton` | `ButtonLabel` (a CommonTextBlock gets the style's text style) |
| Title «PRESS ANY KEY» | `WBP_TitleScreen` | `TitleText`, `PressAnyKeyText` |
| Main menu (list left, description bottom-left, Back hint bottom-right) | `WBP_MainMenu` | `ContinueButton` … `QuitButton` (EntryId Continue / NewGame / LoadGame / Options / Credits / Quit; label, description and CameraAnchorId editable per button), `DescriptionText`, `BackHintText`, `GameTitleText` |
| Save / load list | `WBP_SaveSlots`, line `WBP_SaveSlotEntry` | `TitleText`, `SlotList`, `EmptyText`, `SlotNameBox`, `SaveButton`, `DeleteButton`, `BackButton`, `StatusText`, `DescriptionText`; entry: `SlotTitleText`, `SlotDateText`, `SlotLevelText`, `SlotPlayTimeText` (+ BP event `OnSlotSet` for thumbnails) |
| Options (placeholder) / Credits | `WBP_Options`, `WBP_Credits` | `TitleText`, `BodyText` / `CreditsText` (lines: `CreditLines` in the class defaults), `BackButton` |
| Pause menu | `WBP_PauseMenu` | `ResumeButton`, `SaveButton`, `LoadButton`, `OptionsButton`, `ControlsButton` / `TutorialButton` / `ResetTutorialButton` (optional: an older WBP without them gets the buttons inserted after `OptionsButton` at run time), `QuitToMenuButton`, `QuitGameButton`, `DescriptionText`, `StatusText` |
| Controls reference (F1 / pause menu CONTROLS) | `WBP_Controls` (`UCodexControlsScreen`; tag `Codex.UI.Screen.Controls`; C++ fallback until the WBP exists) | `TitleText`, `ControlsList` (a ScrollBox: section headers + key / description rows are added by the code from the `PlayerCommands` registry), `BackButton` |
| Confirm dialog (blur + dim) | `WBP_ConfirmDialog` | `BackgroundBlur`, `DimBorder`, `TitleText`, `MessageText`, `YesButton`, `NoButton`, `OkButton` |
| Key icons (keyboard / gamepad) | `Input/BP_ControllerData_KeyboardMouse`, `Input/BP_ControllerData_Gamepad` (InputBrushDataMap) | — |
| Confirm / Back keys | `Input/DT_CodexUIActions` (rows Confirm, Back), `Input/BP_CodexUIInputData` | — |
| Menu scene: floor, sky, light, the idling character, cameras | `/Game/Maps/L_MainMenu` — `MenuCharacter` (`ACodexMenuCharacterActor`: mesh, `IdleAnimation` or the mesh's Anim BP), `Anchor_<Id>` (`ACodexMenuCameraAnchor`: pilot it, FOV / post process on its camera, `AnchorId`, `BlendTimeOverride`) | anchor ids = the buttons' CameraAnchorId (Title, Continue, NewGame, LoadGame, Options, Credits, Quit) |
| Blend time / curve between anchors, levels | Project Settings > Game > Codex Frontend | `CameraBlendTime` 1.2 s, `CameraBlendFunction` EaseInOut, `CameraBlendExp`, `FrontendLevel`, `NewGameLevel` |

Placeholders in phase 1: OPTIONS (text only; the tabbed options screen is a later phase), play time on save slots
("--:--" until the save metadata records it), the plain colour button style, the generated map layout.

### Save system (2026-10-08; audit `docs/port/save_audit.md`)

- `USaveGameSubsystem` (`Core/SaveGameSubsystem.*`) writes JSON slots (`Saved/SaveGames/<slot>.json`), format **version 2**
  (`SaveGameRules::CurrentSaveVersion`; version 1 / Godot saves still load — missing sections leave the world alone).
  Saved: squad (+ bonus items, per-operative fire posture), flow phase / wave / preparation time / ambush, squad posture,
  Commander Mode, quest chain, crates, piles, every interactable by actor name (position, hidden, trap, generator,
  barrel fire, mine, tripwire, barricade / turret health, turret power / broken, note read), gates (+ the paced creep), dialogue triggers,
  patrol routes, living enemies (patrol waypoint / phase / wait / search / escort leader; anyone missing = dead stays
  dead), Susanin (rescue + recruited). Deployables / tripwires / enemies / routes missing from the world are recreated
  from their class path; ones not in the save are removed.
- **Policy (user decision 2026-10-08):** `CanSaveNow(&Reason)` — saving only outside combat (Exploration, Preparation,
  WaveCleared, PostCombat); `QuickSave` / `SaveToSlotWithMessage` refuse in the fight / cutscene / game over with
  *"Saving is disabled during combat"*. `SaveGame` itself is unchecked (tests, autosave). Autosave slot `autosave` on
  `UGameFlowSubsystem::OnBeforeGameFlowChanged` (native, fires before `OnGameFlowChanged`): into WaveCombat = BeforeCombat
  (written as the wave's preparation / the exploration before an ambush, patrols back on duty), WaveCombat -> WaveCleared
  = AfterCombat. Off in headless `-ExecCmds` runs unless a check sets `bAutosaveEnabled`; CVar `Codex.Save.Autosave`.
- **Load:** `LoadGame` applies in place; `LoadGameWithTravel` (frontend CONTINUE / LOAD via `CodexSaveBridge`) stores the
  slot in `UMissionSessionSubsystem::PendingLoadSlot`, reopens `FSaveSlotInfo::MapName` and applies it 0.5 s after begin
  play (Game mode, no intro). `GetContinueSlot`, static `OnSaveApplied()`. Load flow: `SaveGameRules::ResolveLoadFlow`.
- Tests `CodexTactics.Save.Policy.*` (4) + `Core.SaveGame.Rules`; smoke `SaveRoundTripSmoke` (exclusive; snapshot / save /
  mutate / load / diff in place and after travel, policy, autosaves) + `SaveLoadSmoke`.

## 7. Open user decisions / questions

- **Demo 01 rev 5 (2026-10-13) - defaults chosen by the orchestrator, to confirm (beat sheet §12):** (1) the shaky bridge REFUSES a
  second operative at the edge (no fall / damage) - keep, or sway and dump both? (2) the low fence is MANUAL prone (a standing order
  stops with a hint) - keep, or auto-prone through it? (3) moves are refused while the HQ intro plays ("Hold on - HQ is talking.") -
  keep? (4) vision cones on `O`, a session setting (not saved) - OK / save it in the profile? (5) the pre-horde patrol (beat sheet
  §1.3 encounter groups): 2 frozen + 2 hounds, turn-based allowed, Panic off? (6) stances / vault / cover volumes move to the approach
  (`VOL_A3_*`), guard moves to the generator beat - OK? (7) precredit on by default for command cards except regroup / sprint / loot
  open / tripwire - OK?

- **Placed-enemy archetype (2026-10-12, user-approved fix):** an enemy placed in a level (not made by the wave spawner / a save
  load) whose `Archetype` field equals its class default now takes the archetype the game mode's `EnemyClasses` maps to its
  Blueprint (or a parent of it); a value changed on the instance wins; an unmapped class keeps its class default (C++
  `AMarksmanEnemyCharacter` = Marksman). One log line per placed enemy (`[Enemy] placed ... archetype ... from the ...`). To
  confirm: a designer who wants a different type on a BP_Enemy_X sets the field on the instance (as before).

- **APC P4 hull (2026-10-12) - choices made by Claude, to confirm:** (1) numbers: hull 1200 HP; small claws x0.1, Brute x1.0,
  spitter acid x0.5 (the spitter / cryo drone shoot the hull only when nobody is outside), marksman x0.25, blasts x1.0 (the
  blast's enemy damage at the hull side's distance), a mine / tripwire the APC sets off itself = flat 250, a fast Brute ram costs
  the hull 30; (2) enemies go for the APC only while it is **manned and running**, when it is the closest threat (weights: small
  1.0 like an operative, Brute 0.85 vs his 0.7 for operatives) and within 40 m (a horde always knows); an empty / disabled APC is
  ignored; (3) mines: level mines and squad mines with friendly fire; tripwires: hostile (PMC) ones like an operative; (4) the
  crew is ejected at once (also in the tactical pause; a fade in real time) on the side away from known threats, straight into
  hull cover, no damage, no knockdown; (5) repair: **Engineer only** (others refused, `OtherRepairSeconds` 0), no parts, 5 s at
  the hull, crouched, back to 50 %; Y with the Commander selected says "only the Engineer", Alt+Y picks him; refused in the
  pause / turn-based like other vehicle orders; (6) Ctrl+click object shot = **cannon only** (one shell, any posture), barrels /
  mines / trapped objects / trapped crates; an untrapped crate is ignored; (7) no "wheel damage -50 % speed" and no smoke at 25 %
  (design extras, not asked); (8) the damaged model is assumed to share the skeletal mesh's pivot (move `DamagedMesh` in the BP
  if it does not); headless the Cascade wreck smoke does not run (checked when rendering); (9) turn-based blasts do not reach the
  hull yet (the grid has its own blast code; P3).

- **APC P2 (2026-10-12) - choices made by Claude, to confirm:** (1) **run-over decision changed by the user**: small enemies
  (hound, frostbitten, cutter, spitter, marksman - the marksman treated as small as asked) die under the wheels at >= 1.5 m/s
  (credit = the driver); slower they are shoved aside without braking; (2) a **downed Brute is not run over** (the APC stops
  against him; `bCrushDownedRammed` / JSON `crush_downed_brute` switches it); (3) the ram deals 40 to the Brute and **no damage to
  the hull** yet (no hull health until P4; `HullRamDamage` 0 is kept for P4); (4) the Brute's knockdown clips play at an absolute
  0.65x (fall 1.4 s, get-up ~2.5 s) and he stays down 0.9 s (1.5 x 0.6 s); (5) the turret holds fire in **Passive** even when
  attacked (as operatives), Ctrl+click always fires; the cannon is used for a Brute / marksman / a group of 3 / a priority target,
  an overheated MG makes small targets wait (shells are kept for big threats); (6) ~~MG suppression stagger~~ **removed (user
  decision 2026-10-12): the MG just deals damage** (damage / rate / heat unchanged); (7) the turret also fires in exploration (no
  phase gate; a turret hit starts an ambush like any hit); (8) a Ctrl+click on a barrel / object is ignored (enemies only for
  now); (9) no sounds: the project has no weapon sounds yet (BP event On Turret Fired; TANDEM request 20); muzzle flash = the
  vendor Cascade emitter, tracers = the operatives' tracers; (10) enemy AI does not dodge the moving APC (small enemies are run over
  by design); while parked the hull is carved out of the infantry navmesh as before.

- **APC P1 (2026-10-12) - choices made by Claude inside the approved design, to confirm:** (1) the tactical pause refuses vehicle
  orders (board / get out / drive) with a feed line instead of planning them (the task allowed "planned if simple"); (2) turn-based
  entry is refused while anyone is aboard (the grid has no vehicle seats until P3); (3) Alt + Y with the squad partly aboard boards
  the rest ("all aboard"); only with everyone aboard does it empty the vehicle; (4) the vehicle navmesh is opt-in per bounds volume
  (tag `VehicleNav`); (5) the engine runs while anyone is aboard (no 10 s auto-off yet) and then heats a 3.5 m zone round the hull
  centre, which also warms operatives hugging the hull; (6) the path preview ribbon (red where it won't fit) is not built: a drive
  that can't reach the click goes as close as it can, the driver says so and `VehiclePathBlocked` is raised; (7) the vehicle agent's
  slope limit is the infantry's 44 deg (a per-agent 25 deg needs a RecastNavMesh subclass - say if wanted).

- **Real time vs turn-based damage numbers (2026-10-11, table `docs/port/tb_parity_2026-10-11.md`).** The double cuts are fixed;
  still different per mode (all inherited from Godot): crouch defence RT 0.75 vs TB 0.70; barrel on enemies RT 120 x falloff vs
  TB 80; mine RT 120 (squad 90) x falloff vs TB 50; TB blasts keep Godot's luck dodge, RT blasts never dodge. Choose per row:
  keep / real-time value / turn-based value.

- **Blast gate tied to the generator (2026-10-11), to confirm with the user:** (1) speed: 0.4 %/s of wave fight with the generator
  up, capped per wave at 30 / 60 / 90 % (a wave's share fills in ~75 s), full only after wave 3; (2) «Start Battle» keeps opening
  the gate at once because it places the squad behind the gate (`CombatStart`) while the waves spawn near the test start — say if
  «Start Battle» should place the squad in front of the shut gate instead; (3) the Wave Editor has no UI for the `gate_*` keys yet
  (only the TS types / schema).
- **Sprint 15 heat - design gaps decided by Claude (2026-10-11), waiting for the user's confirmation** (values in tactical_heat.json):
  (1) heat pack "-35 %" = 35 points of the 0..100 cold scale (like the food's "25 % cold off"), frostbite cleared -> the operative gets
  up to a crouch; a pack dragged onto a COLD mate is used on him, onto a warm one it is handed over; (2) items: starting loadout (above) +
  every crate 1 heat pack + 1 firewood; flares only per crate / loadout; the stove only in the loadout (no gas canister item yet);
  (3) keys: U = heat pack, Shift + G = flare (and the selector's 5th line), F / drawer = stove and campfire (F keeps the Godot priority of
  turrets / barricades / mines; heat gear when the squad has none, or by cycling F while placing); (4) crafting = the campfire's set-up
  spends 1 match + 1 firewood (else 10 flamethrower fuel; no crafting UI); new item `Firewood`; the "Fuel" of the spec = flamethrower
  fuel; (5) campfire noise = patrols within 15 m start a search (no ambush / fight start); (6) wind = the cold system's elevation rule
  (fire base Z >= 140); (7) the beasts' fear of the flare = the existing fire fear of every active heat source (hounds / cutters /
  frostbitten flee while inside 6 m) instead of a 2 s hesitation — it also applies to the campfire, stove and puddle; (8) not in
  turn-based: throwing / lighting a flare; deploying the stove / campfire costs 2 / 3 AP there; (9) an exploded barrel leaves a puddle
  (its core damages operatives too); (10) no night / darkness system exists: the flare's "night accuracy" part has no hook.

- **Sniper rifle vs move orders (user decision 2026-10-09, confirmed):** the fire posture's automatic fire never interrupts a
  player's move order — the Medic-Sapper does not shoot while walking and fires only once she has stopped (kneeling first);
  direct fire orders (priority target, Ctrl + click, planned / turn-based shot) do stop her (`SniperRules::NextStep`).

- **Enemy AI direction (user decisions 2026-10-04):** bot VETERAN target win rate 40-75 % (the Jev coach's band);
  turn-based ranged enemies (spitter, marksman) shoot at range with line of fire and a hit roll (deliberate departure
  from Godot, where every turn-based enemy only bites); per-archetype morale (fast ones fall back to the pack and come
  again, brutes / frostbitten never fall back, marksmen relocate); real-time enemy tactics first, then turn-based on
  the same shared rules. Enemy *power* knobs (damage, accuracy, ...) stay proposals for the user; enemy *intelligence*
  knobs may be tuned by the coach. Marksman kiting limit (cooldown 10 s, one 3.5 s dash) = user decision 2026-10-04.
- Jev coach finding 2026-10-04 (user's current waves, VETERAN, 8 runs per batch): 0-1 / 8 victories whatever the bot
  knobs; Jev fairness ~1 / 4 every iteration, enemy issue «marksman too accurate / too evasive» — wave 3 (20 hounds,
  brute, 6 cutters, 4 marksmen) is the user's to rebalance; the coach only proposes enemy power changes.
- 2026-10-04 bot balance batch (25 runs, level_01_outpost / stage_01 data): NORMAL 2/10, VETERAN 4/10, CASUAL 1/5 victories; 16 of 19 defeats are FREEZING_FATIGUE, almost all in wave 3 (squad cold at the end 72-97 %), the medic freezes most often. Open for the user: tune the cold (e.g. wave 3 cold_drain_mult in the Wave Editor / cold rates in DA_GameBalanceConfig) or keep it.
- Spawn lanes: resolved 2026-10-04 (Sprint 05-C aliases).

- L_MovementTest re-laid by the user (rotated 180°, moved): kept (user decision 2026-09-29); smokes are layout-relative.
- `Config/DefaultEditor.ini` has local editor changes — never commit it unless asked.
- M16 clip (user decision 2026-09-29): 30 for every operative like the reference video. The current Godot scene sets
  reload_after_shots 10 (commander) / 15 (engineer) — deliberately not ported.
- Level waves (user decision 2026-09-29): like the Godot code — `main.gd _spawn_custom_json_wave` spawns the whole wave
  at once; `spawn_delay_sec`, `initial_delay_sec`, `max_simultaneous_enemies` (described in DATA_CONTRACTS.md) are
  ignored. Wave modifiers apply (hp / damage / speed per enemy, `cold_drain_mult` → operatives' cold outside camera
  zones, kept after the wave like Godot's cold_rate_modifier), `custom_stats.health` × hp_mult.
- L_MovementTest (user, 2026-09-30): PlayerStart moved ~170 m south of the gate; the four EnemySpawn_* points were moved
  next to it on the user's request (same 20-28 m spread, north of the start — the south wall is 9 m behind it). Smokes
  anchor the layout on the checkpoint gate (`SmokeUtils::LayoutTransform`) since the floor was stretched.
- Monster models: the user imports them personally; the enemy BPs / AnimBPs are generated from them by
  setup_enemy_animation.py (user request 2026-09-30). Spitter / cryo drone have no art yet (C++ placeholder body).
- Start menu (user decision 2026-09-29): only «Начать игру» (exploration → combat) and «Начать бой» (preparation);
  Godot's third mode «Начать исследование» is removed from UE (menu button, `EMissionStartMode::Exploration`, texts).
- «Начать бой» position: Godot hard-codes the yard behind the gate; UE uses the `CombatStart` tag (test map: (0, −2150)).
- Turn-based deviations: an operative that survives a mine ends its turn once (Godot schedules end_current_unit_turn
  twice — from the move and from the blast — which skips the next operative; treated as a bug). Squad starts facing the
  enemies' centroid (Godot hard-codes SOUTH = towards the enemies on its map). The attack line names the arc
  (фронт / фланг / тыл; Godot prints the enum number). Trapped-barricade retaliation: Godot _damage_barricade checks `is_mined`, which
  nothing ever sets — dead code, not ported. Enemy idle variations: with the AnimBP.
- Stage loadout (Godot _apply_stage_exploration_resources, `LoadoutRules`): when the cutscene ends, «Начать игру» keeps
  what the squad collected (turrets to the commander ≤ 2, barricades to the engineer ≤ 4, mines to the medic-sapper ≤ 5);
  «Начать бой» turns EXPLORE_AND_COLLECT into STARTING_UNIQUE (at least 1 / 2 / 2; level_01_outpost already says
  STARTING_UNIQUE); EDITOR_PRESET tiers from the level JSON. Godot quirk kept: the specialist gets the squad total
  while the others keep theirs (items double up) — kept as in Godot (user decision 2026-09-30). The --bot-loadout CLI override is not ported.
- Turn-based entry on flat ground only (user decision 2026-09-30; Godot allows platforms): the controller samples the floor
  under the future grid (7 x 7 over 21 m, `CombatQueries::SampleGroundHeights`) and refuses when a living operative's feet
  are more than 50 cm from the median («⚠️ Пошаговый бой можно начать только на ровной поверхности…»). A plateau wider
  than the grid counts as flat. Smokes call `RequestEnterTurnBased` directly and are not affected.
- `FString::ToLower` / `Contains(IgnoreCase)` do not fold Cyrillic: use `FText::ToLower` (see DialogueRules).
- The world is paused while the start menu is open (Godot keeps processing behind its menu) — cosmetic difference.

   assets are `UGameBalanceConfig` — one typed field per Godot export (364, Godot names, Categories = the Godot
   @export_group / @export_subgroup sections, ## comments as tooltips, @export_range as sliders / clamps), generated
   into `Public/Data/GameBalanceConfig.h` by `Scripts/generate_balance_config.py` (plain Python; re-run when Godot adds
   parameters). `UGodotBalanceAsset::GetNumber` / `SetNumber` reach the fields by name via reflection (then `Numbers`
   for keys without a field), so every system reads the tuned values. `import_balance.py` creates missing assets from
   Godot once; afterwards it only writes a Godot-vs-Unreal difference report (`CODEX_BALANCE_REIMPORT=1` overwrites).
   Rule tests use `CodexTacticsTests/Private/GodotBalanceFixture.h` (Godot values), not the tunable assets. Wired so far: turn-based combat
   (`TurnBasedRules::BalanceFromGodot(DA_Balance)` + step durations, loaded at combat start via the game mode's
   `TurnBasedBalance`), operatives (`Characters/OperativeBalance::Apply` = Godot apply_balance_config: max health
   commander 140 / engineer 150 / medic 120, matches, walk / run / crouch speeds, acceleration, sprint / lift / carry /
   wound values, commander prep radius, cold rules — rates, stance multipliers, misfire / freeze / aim; applied on
   the deferred spawn), enemies (`AEnemyCharacter::ApplyBalance` = Godot enemy apply_balance_config: crit chance /
   multiplier per type — hound 0.15, spitter 0.25, brute 0.30, frostbitten 0.10 instead of a flat 0.20 — and
   hound / spitter / brute health, speed, damage, range, cooldown). Deployables need nothing: Godot reads the config
   only when the inspector value is 0, and the UE values equal the Godot export defaults (mine 120 / 3.5 m, turret
   120 HP 16 dmg 0.45 s 12 m, barricade 200 HP). Camera NOT wired on purpose (UE distances were tuned with the user;
   Godot distance semantics differ) — ask before changing.
   Levels — `Scripts/Editor/import_levels.py` → `/Game/Data/Levels/DA_Level_<file>` (level_01_outpost + stage_01..12;
   waves with `is_active: false` dropped like main.gd). The game mode's `LevelConfig` (DA_Level_level_01_outpost = Godot
   main.gd active_level_json_path) goes to `UWaveSubsystem::SetLevelConfig` and, through `LevelFlowRules::ApplyLevel`, to
   the flow config: preparation 60 s, rest 20 s, TotalWaves = 3 (Godot max_waves = active waves; without a level Godot
   uses preparation_phase_duration / max_campaign_waves from game_balance_config.tres). L_MovementTest has four
   `AEnemySpawnPoint`s beyond the gate (NORTH_GATE, WEST_FLANK, EAST_FLANK, FAR_PERIMETER; added by
   add_level_objects_to_movement_test.py, following the Floor transform). Lane matching = Godot _get_enemy_spawn_pos
   (substring either way, else any point). Spawning = Godot _spawn_custom_json_wave (see §7).
   Source trap: Godot has TWO GameBalanceConfig files with different values. The turn-based manager loads
   `resources/balance.tres` first (squad 8 AP, enemy 6 AP — the UE `FTurnBasedBalance` defaults), while camera, enemies,
   turrets, mines, barricades load `resources/game_balance_config.tres` (e.g. tactical_squad_max_ap = 3, enemy 4,
   turret_shot_delay 1.0). Import both and wire each consumer to the file its Godot counterpart loads. Weapons:
   `resources/weapons/*.tres` → `UWeaponDataAsset` (same field names). Levels: `data/configs/levels/*.json`.
