## 9. Known gaps / tech debt

- **Demo 01 rev 5 (2026-10-13):** (1) `VisionConeSmoke` fails on `agents/work` at "operative cone range = weapon range x stance (1400 cm,
  weapon m16)": the leader's cone reads `VisionConeRangeCm` (1400) instead of the M16 range, i.e. `bVisionConeRangeFromWeapon` is off on
  the operative that spawns on L_PatrolTest; nothing of rev 5 touches that path (check the user's BP_Operative defaults / main). (2)
  `UNavigationSystemV1::FindPathToLocationSynchronously(.., UNavFilter_NoCrawl)` returned a FULL path through a crawl-only strip in
  `BridgeFenceSmoke` while the operative's own `RequestMove` with the same filter ends before the fence (the behaviour that matters);
  the smoke logs the query result as info only - investigate the filter class handling of the synchronous query. (3) The bridge keeps
  no memory of a refused operative's destination: a box-selected group ordered across stops at the edge and must be re-ordered
  (formation followers continue on their own); option: re-issue `LastMoveDestination` when admitted. (4) The crawl-only area has
  cost 1: a prone body goes straight under; standing bodies path round when the fence is open at the ends (design: close the line).

- **Sprint 15 heat gaps (2026-10-11):** no flamethrower in the arsenal (puddle hook `AFirePuddleActor::SpawnAt` unused but by the
  barrel); no night / darkness system (the flare's "removes night accuracy penalties" has no hook); no gas canister refill item; the stove
  is not a drag & drop item (it moves through F hand-overs only); flares cannot be thrown in turn-based combat; the bot does not use the
  new items; heat items are not shown on the HUD squad panel (drawer only).
- **Melee chasers stop just outside their bite (found 2026-10-11, pre-existing, NOT changed - gameplay decision):** the chase is
  `MoveToActor(Target, AttackRange * 0.5)` with `bStopOnOverlap` (the reach test adds both capsule radii), so a hound (1.8 m
  range) is "at the goal" at about 1.65-2 m centre to centre, while `EnemyAIRules::CanMelee` measures feet to the target's
  Godot position 1 m higher (3D <= 1.8 m, i.e. <= 1.5 m flat). Unpushed, it stands there; after 3 s `no way to …` skips
  the target for 8 s (EnemyTacticsSmoke: ~10 such retargets in 10 s, and its side / rear check relies on them). With the
  re-path throttle's first version (no per-tick already-at-goal ask) hounds walked on into melee and pounced; the parity
  rule keeps the stand-off. A fix (e.g. acceptance from the flat melee reach, or the reach test without the radii) is a
  gameplay change and re-tunes EnemyTacticsSmoke / the pounce rate: ask the user.
- Enemy spawn points follow Godot exactly: L_MovementTest points carry the Godot lane names («Северные ворота»,
  «Левый фланг (Прорыв)» HOUND only, «Правый фланг» SPITTER only, «Дальний периметр») while the level JSON uses
  NORTH_GATE / WEST_FLANK / EAST_FLANK — in Godot these never match, so laned entries spawn at a random static point
  and only "ANY" entries honour the type filter. Renaming `SpawnLane` on the points (or the JSON lanes) makes lanes
  work; left as in Godot. No Godot level has a dynamic breach point (`bIsDynamic`), so the breach code is dormant
  until a designer places one; `ActiveWaves` / `WarningLeadTime` are exported but unused (like Godot). The breach
  floats «ЧЁРТ ПОДЕРИ» over the leader like Godot.
- Susanin (`URecruitSubsystem`): spawned from the operative Blueprint with `ACodexTacticsGameMode::RecruitSusanin`
  (pink tint via BodyColor — the user's BP decides the real look; Godot swaps the Explorer_Coat material). The spot is a
  TargetPoint tagged `SusaninSpawn` on the west side of the yard (Godot uses a hard-coded (-8.2, 18.0) in its own
  layout). The narrative pause uses the minimum world time dilation, restored with `UGameFlowSubsystem::ApplyTimeDilation`.
  Rage uses the general keys (see Rage below); panic uses the susanin_* stress keys (ordinary susceptibility, user
  decision 2026-10-01). His death does not fail the mission (expendable) and leaves searchable remains (§10).
  The Godot `dialogue_susanin_recruitment.tres` export is unused by Godot's code (it builds both dialogues inline) — same here.
- Waves beyond the level config (or without one) follow Godot's fallback (`FallbackWaveRules` + test,
  `UWaveSubsystem::StartWave`): balance-driven hound / spitter / brute counts from DA_GameBalanceConfig, the whole wave
  at once at the type-filtered spawn points (Gemini's queued built-in waves are gone).
- Level spawn `custom_stats` are all imported (health, damage, speed m/s, attack_range m, attack_cooldown s; `AEnemyCharacter::ApplySpawnEntry`, Godot _spawn_custom_json_wave; no Godot level sets them yet).
- Still hand-typed (Phase 2 continues): camera (user-tuned, see §8.7), enemy visuals / capsules (Gemini). Imported and wired:
  weapons, turn-based balance, operative health / speeds / matches / lift & sprint limits (per role), cold rules, enemy stats and crit chances.
- Floating combat texts (`UFloatingTextSubsystem`, drawn by the HUD over the name plates): wired for operative hits /
  dodge / crit, misses, misfires, frozen / thawed weapon, frostbite, enemy numbers (type prefix, armor «🛡️», immunity,
  burn / bleed ticks), turn-based turret miss, mine armed / spotted / set-up / mishap, barricade cover, repairs, solo
  mode, guard, heal, Susanin, breach, rage, squad stance, sector, cover. Not wired (feature not ported): panic /
  allegiance texts,
  enemy fear of fire, cutter texts, solo frost-crawl. Godot moved the weapon-frozen / frostbite lines
  from the feed to floating texts; UE now does the same (they no longer appear in the message feed).
- **Parity fix:** real-time squad fire follows Godot player.gd (`SquadFireRules`, `AOperativeCharacter::FindShootTarget`):
  range x posture (1 / 1.15 / 1.35) x elevation (1.25), dead zone under platforms, line of fire from the muzzle to the
  enemy (+0.8 m) — walls block, barricades block prone shooters and give crouched ones cover 0.8, another enemy in the
  way becomes the target; target memory with the stance switch delay / ratio from game_balance_config; damage = weapon
  x stance (1 / 1.25 / 1.6) x cover x crit (luck %, x2) x elevation (1.15) x the weapon's distance factor; cryo / fire
  weapons chill / warm the shooter. Before, the nearest enemy in range was shot for flat weapon damage. Not ported:
  panic refusal, reload_after_shots (user decision: 30 rounds).
- Autonomous grenades (`AIGrenadeRules`, `AOperativeCharacter::TryAIGrenadeThrow`; Godot _evaluate_ai_grenade_opportunity):
  in the real-time fight (not raging / reloading, 3 s cooldown) an operative throws at the biggest cluster of 3+ enemies
  in its stance range, 5 m+ away, with no squad member / turret within 5 m of the aim point; the empty-weapon switch
  (Godot _auto_switch_on_empty: M16 with rounds -> pistol -> a grenade at a cluster -> knife) replaces «out of ammo».
- Rage (`URageComponent`, `RageRules`; Godot rage_component.gd): crits per attacker (real-time 25 s memory), chance decays
  with the component's lifetime like Godot's combat_time_elapsed, per-role keys (<role>_rage_*) for the squad, general
  keys for Susanin (Godot re-applies only the general ones to a recruit spawned later). Raging: chaotic target (random
  enemy within 25 m every 0.55 s), fire rate / damage multipliers, +30 luck, no ammo spent, reload paused, OrderMoveTo /
  SetManualPriorityTarget refused. HUD badge «ЯРОСТЬ!»; the fiery aura is an orange 30 cm band at 0.9 m radius under the feet (`URageComponent::UpdateAura`, M_CombatFeedback segments).
  movement_speed_multiplier is exported but unused in Godot — not applied.
- **Parity fix:** enemy attacks on operatives now use Godot player.gd take_damage (dodge luck × 0.4 %, stance defense,
  fortitude cut clamp(f × 1.5 %, 0, 50 %)); before, the enemy armor formula was used (no dodge / fortitude). Grenades /
  traps on the squad use the bypass path (max(1, amount)). A hit on a carrier drops every carried object (`URelocationSubsystem::DropAllForCombat`, alarm line).
- **Parity fix:** turn-based squad shots and turret shots on enemies go through the enemy's own affinity
  (Godot occ.take_damage(final_dmg, KINETIC, 0.0)). **2026-10-11 (audit, user "fix"):** each armour once — a squad shot's
  grid damage already subtracted the grid armour, so it passes `TurnBasedRules::SquadShotArmorPenetration` (1) and the
  health component's % armour no longer cuts it again (Godot did both); the turret / blasts keep the % armour (no grid armour).
  Turn-based barrel / mine blasts (`ApplyBlast`, Godot _detonate_barrel / _detonate_mine): the unit's own hit rules
  (enemy armor; operative `TakeGridBlast` = luck dodge (as Godot) + fortitude + downed x1.33 on the grid-stance amount — the
  real-time stance defence is no longer applied a second time, no cover, no heavy-hit knockdown; `OperativeHitRules`;
  numbers that still differ from real time: `docs/port/tb_parity_2026-10-11.md`), and an enemy dies when its
  health could not take the raw blast (Godot grid hp decides the kill and calls die()). **Deviation:** operatives die by
  real health only — Godot's grid hp would drop a living operative from the fight.
- **Parity fixes (squad control):** operatives are wounded below `wounded_health_threshold_percent` of max health
  (Godot is_wounded; before, `bWounded` was never set, so the wounded speed / no-sprint rules never applied — the speed
  now follows every health change; `UHealthComponent::ApplyDirectHealthLoss` broadcasts OnHealthChanged); a ground click
  during an active wave outside the tactical pause is refused («Перемещение во время боя возможно только в режиме
  тактической паузы»); double-click sprint lines (frozen / wounded / «Бегом к позиции!»). New: Alt + Z / C / V squad
  stance, Shift + click sector facing (no facing indicator arrow; UE has no persistent fixed-facing, the operative just
  turns), auto-cover crouch on arrival next to a barricade in combat with the radio callout. Box selection (Godot
  _perform_box_selection / _set_selected_squad / _get_group_target_positions): LMB acts on release; a drag past 12 px
  draws the cyan box (HUD `DrawSelectionBox`) and selects the living members whose feet / centre / head fall inside
  (`ACodexTacticsPlayerController::SelectInBox`, `USquadSubsystem::SetSelectedGroup`); rings under them (gold leader
  only in a group); ground orders move / plan the whole group in Godot's slots around the click (`OrderGroupMove`,
  `SquadFormation::ComputeGroupTargets`); picking a leader by number drops the group; not in the grid fight (clicks are
  grid orders there). In exploration the formation still pulls followers (as in Godot). The action bar marks
  a squad slot in barricade cover (green frame) or holding (guard / solo; blue) with « ●» (no emoji in the UI font) and
  Godot's tooltips.
- Overhead labels (`FOverheadLabel`, `AEnemyCharacter` / `AInteractableActor::GetOverheadLabel`, HUD `DrawWorldLabels`;
  Godot Label3D): enemies (armor-tier square instead of 🟢🟡🔴, name, statuses as words — ГОРИТ / ЛЁД / ОГЛУШЁН / БРОНЯ-,
  the font has no emoji — HP; hidden for stasis enemies outside a turn-based fight), barricade, turret, generator with
  the Godot texts / colours.
- **Enemy AI (parity pass; Gemini's AEnemyCharacter::Tick replaced):** `EnemyAIRules` + `AEnemyCharacter::Tick` / `FindTarget`
  / `TickSpitter` (Godot enemy_base.gd _physics_process / _process_enemy_behavior / _find_closest_squad_member /
  _find_blocking_barricade / _find_nearest_active_fire_source, enemy_frost_spitter.gd, enemy_frost_brute.gd):
  per-type affinities and base armor (brute kinetic 0.25!, spitter 0.6, hound melee 1.3 …); stagger stops, frost halves
  the speed; hounds / cutters / frostbitten flee burning barrels and active heat sources within 6 m (x1.2 speed,
  «СТРАХ ОГНЯ»); target weights — small enemies: generator 0.4 x, turret 0.5 x, squad 1 x (so hounds rush the generator
  from the start of a wave, like Godot: the generator is always in the "generators" group); large: squad 0.7 x, turrets
  0.85 x (0.6 x after a turret hit) and any turret within 10 m; a barricade / turret within 2.2 m towards the target is
  smashed (brutes double on barricades; an enemy blow sets off a trap); melee needs range and <= 1.2 m height difference;
  spitters pick targets by 100 - distance (+50 elevated), approach / back off around 12 m, shoot only with a line of
  fire (prone behind a barricade hidden, crouched cover 0.65), red tracer. Movement stays on the navmesh (UE AI
  MoveTo) — Godot's ramp routing, wall-slide / stuck avoidance and flocking separation are covered by navigation /
  crowd avoidance. Frostbitten blows shove the operative (+2.5 m/s); hits flash (operative: red light, enemy: red
  overlay; burning ticks do not flash, Godot does). Not ported: the cryo drone (UE maps it to spitter stats), attack
  animation locks (is_attacking; with the AnimBP).
- **Enemy re-path throttle (audit 2026-10-11 #1):** every `AAIController::MoveTo` is a synchronous pathfind in UE 5.8, and
  the real-time enemy brain asks for its move every tick. All per-tick asks go through `AEnemyCharacter::RequestAIMoveToLocation` /
  `RequestAIMoveToActor` -> `AEnemyAIController::RequestMoveTo*`, which issue a MoveTo only when `RepathRules::ShouldRepath`
  (pure, tests `AI.Repath.*`) says so: the pawn within the wanted move's reach (`HasReached`, the test MoveTo runs first) every
  tick as before (an already-at-goal finish, no pathfind - without it a followed chase walked on past the boundary where the
  per-tick ask stopped it, hounds then bit / pounced instead of the old stand-off, and EnemyTacticsSmoke lost its flank
  retargets); a new goal (other actor / location <-> actor / other acceptance) or a stopped / aborted /
  replaced move at once; an actor goal being followed never (the engine's observed path updates it, 0.5 s / 1 m, and the last
  segment walks straight at the actor); a followed location goal when it moved > `Codex.AI.RepathMinGoalDeltaCm` (75) and
  `Codex.AI.RepathInterval` (0.3 s) passed; a reached goal on a goal move or the interval; a failed move every interval.
  `Codex.AI.Repath 0` restores the per-tick behaviour (A/B). The follow state comes from the path following (status, current
  request id, `OnMoveCompleted` result). Turn-based hold and the world AI pause reset the throttle. NOT throttled (own gating):
  `IssuePatrolMove` (patrol route first walk / 1 s stuck, escort tether) - the marksman's override resets the throttle before
  its run; the marksman's other runs (`ExecuteMoveTo`: approach / back-off / flank / retreat) are throttled. Operatives were
  already gated (squad follow `RepathInterval`, autonomy `DecisionInterval`, bot 0.2 s / orders). Counters:
  `AEnemyAIController::GetTotalMoveRequestCount` / `GetTotalPathfindCount` / `GetTotalMoveAskCount` (+ per controller); measure with
  `-ExecCmds="[Codex.AI.Repath 0,]CodexTactics.RepathProbe"` (20 s wave chase, leader walking; not in the smoke registry).
- Cutter (Godot enemy_cutter.gd): own stats (75 HP, 6.2 m/s, 18 damage, 2 m, 1.1 s, crit 0.25 x1.75), the pounce at
  3.5-9 m (windup 0.4 / 1.85 s, ballistic LaunchCharacter flight 1.3 / 1.85 s capped at 9 m, impact damage 28 within
  2.2 m on the squad and barricades, «💥 НАЛЁТ N», recovery 0.75 / 1.85 s, 6 s cooldown) with the numbers imported from
  resources/enemies/anims/cutter.tres (`Scripts/Editor/import_enemy_anim_configs.py` -> /Game/Data/Enemies/DA_EnemyAnim_*);
  Blueprint hooks On Jump Attack Started / Impact for the clips. Godot only jumps when the model has the jump clip —
  UE jumps whenever the config enables it. Shot down mid-leap it keeps falling on the world and crashes («СБИТ В
  ВОЗДУХЕ», «КРАХ»). The airborne kill gives the cutter EXP (16) and counts in the kill statistics (every death goes through HandleDied).
- Frost vignette: drawn with UMG (a UI material drawn by the canvas HUD ignores its opacity). Canvas HUD texts sit under
  it, like Godot's squad HUD under the FrostOverlay. Material traps met on the way (see the script): in a Custom node
  the float3 colour path rendered nothing — keep Custom nodes to scalar math and do colour / opacity with nodes; with
  MaterialEditingLibrary connect a Clamp's first input with the pin name "" (not "Input").
- Silhouette (Godot player.gd + silhouette.gdshader): every operative traces the Visibility channel from the camera to
  its centre (feet + 1 m) 20 times a second; a hit more than 35 cm short puts `M_Silhouette` (colour per role, leader
  cyan) as the overlay material of its visible meshes. Other characters block the ray too (Godot's mask 1 | 2 includes
  bodies on layer 1).
- Event bus (`UCodexEventBus`, Godot event_bus.gd): a hook point for Blueprints / audio / VFX; systems still call each
  other directly (Godot's bus mostly fed the UI). Only the signals Godot actually emits are there; declared-but-never-
  emitted ones, panic / allegiance ones (not ported) and camera_shake_requested (a fallback; the camera is called
  directly) are left out. item_used fires from the inventory drawer only (as in Godot; H / J / K / L do not).
- Turn-based camera choreography (user decision 2026-09-30, Godot main.gd + camera.gd): entering zooms to 14 m; each
  operative's turn / selection glides to it (0.75 s, 16 m); the active operative's own number / a click on it glides
  there (0.85 s) with «🎥 Фокус камеры на бойце»; an enemy's turn glides to it (0.4 s, 11.5 m), its walk (0.35 s,
  11.5 m); after the last enemy the camera glides to the squad centre (0.85 s, 17 m) and waits that long before the
  squad turn. A squad shot (click / Ctrl + click on an enemy, Ctrl + click on a barrel / barricade — Shift until 2026-10-06) plays `AttackCellCinematic`: frame both
  (mid-point, clamp(span x 1.35 + 4 m, 10, 19 m), 0.4 s), shot + shake, the round lands 0.35 s later (`AttackCell`),
  0.65 s to read it, glide back to the shooter at `Config.DistanceCombat` (1.1 s). A turret volley: frame both 0.4 s,
  head turn (snapped, Godot tweens 0.25 s), volley + shake, 0.65 s, glide to the squad centre 0.85 s. Meanwhile
  `IsBusy()` holds every player order. Godot distances are view distances in m; UE uses the same distance in cm. In
  headless runs (no rendering, like Godot's can_tween) the delays are skipped — smokes set
  `bForceCinematicsForTesting` to check them. A turn-based non-leader follow target (enemy) is framed exactly, the
  leader with the deadzone.
- Camera shake (Godot camera.gd): only in turn-based combat, trauma from the squad attack (pistol 0.20 / rifle 0.35)
  and each turret volley (0.28), decay 4 / s, offset = amplitude 0.18 m x trauma² x Perlin noise along the view's right
  / up axes (Godot FastNoiseLite simplex; UE FMath::PerlinNoise1D), values from DA_GameBalanceConfig camera_shake_*.
  The offset is taken off before the follow smoothing so it never drifts the camera.
- Wave victory (`UWaveVictorySubsystem`, `UVictoryPanelWidget`; Godot main.gd): the panel shows while the flow is in
  WaveCleared (time stopped), with Godot's title / subtitle, the kill statistics card and «Запустить следующую волну
  (N/M)» / «Завершить бой и продолжить исследование» (-> `AdvanceAfterWave`) and «Перезапустить уровень (X)». The HUD
  font has no emoji, so the statistics' 🐺 / 🏹 / ❄️ read «гончие / плевуны / громилы». Kills count per Godot
  register_enemy_kill (last attacker; unknown sources go to the leader; the recruit has no entry; only hound / spitter /
  brute per type). Post-combat: enemies removed, the squad teleported to where it stood when the cutscene ended
  (Godot initial_prep_station; before that its position at the post-combat start, Godot: its spawn), orders dropped,
  the commander leads, every bDeployable turret / barricade / mine (abandoned level ones too, as in Godot) goes into
  the supply by Godot's member order and caps with the engineer's feed line. Not ported: deployables_combat_stats
  damage and _record_telemetry (telemetry only); a carried object is not dropped explicitly.
- Progression (Godot player.gd, profile_dialog.gd): kill EXP to every squad member (exp_reward_<type> from
  DA_GameBalanceConfig, cutter 16); level x 250 per level, cap 10; +3 points, full heal, floating text and radio line
  per level; + / - within [start value, cap] (HP +5 up to 200, luck 60, accuracy 100, fortitude 50). Profile on P, on
  the leader's own number key, and on wave clear for the first member with points; Esc closes it. A cleared wave gives
  every member exp_reward_wave_complete (25; `UWaveSubsystem`, Godot _on_wave_cleared); exp_reward_loot_crate is
  config-only in Godot and unused in UE too. Godot names the recruit's profile
  «Полковник Васин» (fallback); UE shows his own name, role «Рекрут / Проводник». The portrait emoji is the role
  initial (no emoji in the font). Level, EXP and points are saved (Godot save_manager.gd).
- Panic is ported and ON (user decision 2026-10-01; Godot ships it disabled), see §10 `UPanicComponent`. The allegiance
  component is only used by Godot tests — not ported.
- Narrative elements (`ANarrativeElementActor`; Godot narrative_element.gd) and dialogue triggers (`ADialogueTriggerVolume`;
  dialogue_trigger.gd): L_MovementTest gets the Godot note (by the generator), signpost (central road), poster (gate wall)
  and the wave-rest trigger (right side, midway) at semantic spots — the Godot scene coordinates don't map onto the UE
  layout. The type emoji marker is a small square (no emoji in the HUD font); in-world text wraps at 48 characters and
  is not occluded by walls (Godot Label3D no_depth_test = false). has_been_read is not saved (Godot neither).
- Space hold (Godot main.gd + tactical_hold_sphere.gd + gorky17_combat_hud charge bar): `AHoldSphereActor` (additive dome
  + growing ring + 15 m boundary ring, eased p x (2 - p)), the squad holds fire while Space is held
  (`AOperativeCharacter::bTacticalCeaseFire`), HUD `DrawSpaceCharge` («ВХОД В ПОШАГОВЫЙ БОЙ (GORKY 17): x.xc / y.yc» /
  «ВОЗВРАТ В ТАКТИЧЕСКУЮ ПАУЗУ»). The dome centre is the leader's feet (Godot ray-casts the floor below the leader).
- Barricade contact damage (Godot barricade.gd contact_*; `ABarricadeActor::ContactType / ContactDamage`): every interval
  enemies within 2.2 m take half of it, a striking enemy takes all of it (not when trapped — the trap goes off), fire
  burns 3 s (tick max(1, dmg / 4)), cryo freezes 2.5 s, energy staggers 1 s, 15 % armour pen, source «Баррикада»; the
  label adds [шипы] / [огонь] / [холод] / [ток]. L_MovementTest's generator barricade (Barricade_Abandoned_West, Godot
  AbandonedBarricadeGenerator) has spikes 5 and vault off, like Godot (BarricadeContactSmoke).
- Pushing / defusal / set-up animations: none (operatives only slow down / crouch).
- **Deliberate deviation (user request 2026-09-30):** enemies that fear fire walk round burning barrels / active heat zones on an arc (35° steps on a circle 1 m outside the zone, `FireDetourWaypoint`) and wait at the edge for a target inside one (the running generator); once panicking they calm down 1 m past the edge. Godot only flees, which made them shake at the edge.
- Turn-based walk: units and pushed objects follow one continuous speed profile over the path (accelerate over the first cell, cruise, decelerate over the last) in Godot's total time; Godot eases each step (first in, last out, middle linear) and snaps the facing per step — UE turns smoothly (~0.15 s).
- Vault (Godot locomotion_controller.gd check_vault_obstacle / start_vault, player.gd try_vault_obstacle; `VaultRules` + test,
  `AOperativeCharacter::TryVault` / `UpdateVault`, `VaultNavigation`): barricades (`bVaultable`, on by default like Godot)
  and level objects tagged `Vault` stop cutting the navmesh and get a `UNavArea_Vault` (cost 3) instead — operatives path
  across and vault (knee-height probe 1.25 m ahead, 25..105 cm, landing 1.35 m behind within ±1 m, arc apex height + 15 cm,
  0.83 s running / 1.09 s walking, collision off, 1.2 s cooldown, the walk resumes); not on the run unless stopped, followers
  only when blocked, never prone or in the grid fight. Enemies use `UNavFilter_NoVault` (they go round / smash, as in Godot).
  No vault clips yet: the anim instance exposes `bIsVaulting` and feeds the arc speed to the locomotion (VaultSmoke).
- Hidden mines are revealed by a distance scan in the mine's Tick (Godot scans from each operative) — same result.
- Weapon switching does not change the weapon mesh (no pistol / shotgun / knife models in the art yet — ASSETS has only
  pistol animations) (the operative Blueprint owns WeaponMesh) and has no holster
  animation. X cycles the arsenal like Godot switch_weapon (grenade -> aim, «🔫 Оружие: …»), R reloads outside placement / the grid fight (Godot KEY_R, «🔄 Перезаряжаю …»).
- Grenades: placeholder sphere mesh, no explosion VFX / sound (Godot has none either) — `AGrenadeActor` Blueprint events
  On Landed / On Detonated and the operative's On Grenade Throw + GrenadeThrowDuration are the hooks.
- Turn-based set-up: the item grows in like Godot (0.2 s, then scale 0.05 -> 1 in 0.45 s, back ease-out; `StartGrowIn`); no assembly animation (Godot play_action_animation "working_device" — no clip yet);
  a squad mine placed in turn-based skips the real-time mishap roll like Godot.
- Turn-based relocation: a hologram of the object follows the hovered cell (Godot _create_relocate_ghost_preview; `SetRelocationHover`, green on a target cell, red elsewhere);
  target cells use the reachable layer (for barricades: cells valid at the current angle). The barricade glides to
  its new place in 0.25 s like Godot (quad ease-out; instant headless).
- Exposed-zone outlines pulse like Godot (alpha 0.65 ± 0.35 at 6 rad/s as glow brightness; the target warning blinks 0.25..0.95 at 9 rad/s, `ATurnGridOverlayActor::Tick`); the additive M_CombatFeedback glow reads pink-white on the
  light floor instead of red — a material for the user to tune (layer colours / intensity in TurnGridOverlayActor.cpp).
- Relocation / placement ghost: `M_GhostHologram` (create_ghost_hologram_material.py; unlit translucent, opacity 0.65, emission x2 like Godot's ghost material).
- Click rules (Godot main.gd plain click, `ACodexTacticsPlayerController::HandleWorldHit`): in a wave a live enemy becomes
  the priority target (direct assignment, no Ctrl); set-up items / movable objects are refused outside the pause; in
  the pause / preparation a set-up item opens its menu at once, in the pause a movable object is picked up for
  relocation at once. A leader alone in a camera zone may move / pick up objects mid-wave (Godot is_zone_solo; `bInCameraZone`).
- Targeted shots: the priority target now gets the barricade cover / blocking of `EvaluateShotLine` and is refused
  while raging (panic refusal: panic not ported). The barrel
  line «💥 Прицельный выстрел…» is posted only when the shot actually fires (Godot posts it even when frozen).
- Save / load differences: a load resumes an active wave from its preparation (Godot only flips flags; neither saves
  enemies); «В главное меню» restarts the level with the start menu (Godot shows the menu over the running scene);
  `AAutosaveTrigger` is not ported — Godot's only trigger (gate_kpp.tscn AutosaveTrigger_Gate) has no collision shape
  and never fires; the start menu has no «Загрузить» (Godot neither). The save file stores the weapon id (Godot:
  current_weapon_idx) and grenades / ExtraAmmo in addition.
- **Deliberate deviation:** the «Патроны 9мм (12 шт.)» hand-over works (Godot shows the button but _transfer_item_to_target
  has no PISTOL_AMMO branch). Ammo handed to a mate without that weapon is kept in ExtraAmmo (Godot drops it).
- **Deliberate deviation (user decision 2026-09-28):** a shot at an untrapped supply crate only posts «💥 Пуля пробила
  ящик снабжения.» — Godot also detonates it (bug: `detonate_trap` always exists on loot_crate.gd).
- Tracers leave `GetWeaponMuzzleLocation` (the weapon's "Muzzle" socket or `MuzzleOffset`); the line of fire keeps
  Godot's stance heights (1.4 / 0.85 / 0.25 m). Light intensity mapping
  (`FeedbackLightPerEnergy` 1500 per Godot light_energy) is a first guess for the user to tune.

| (this, agents/coverhug) | **Fix: wall hugging broken at a wall edge in exploration (user bugs 2026-10-11: "wall hugging doesn't work again" - at the corner of a high wall the feed said "Hugging the wall, holding the corner" but the body stood off the wall in a rifle stance looking elsewhere; "at the first wall he stood strangely at the edge looking in a completely different direction, and ordered along the wall he walks backwards").** Reproduced headless by the new `CodexTactics.CoverHugSmoke` (exploration: no fight, every enemy removed; a 5 m x 3 m wall; run into its middle, then 80 cm from its left edge, shimmy away and back). Three causes: (1) **mesh turned round for good** - the 2026-10-11 bb3345c entry-yaw ease read the enter clip's weight, but `GetCoverEnterBlendWeight` is -1 once the clip is not playing and -1 counted as 0: when the auto-snap shimmy to a near corner cut the enter clip short, the full ~180 deg entry turn stayed on the mesh (measured mesh yaw -179.8 at the corner and after shimmies): the body faced the wall / round, the forward shimmy clip on a reversed body looked like walking backwards, and `GetShotAimResidualDeg` returned 180 (no shot from there). Now `CoverFacingRules::EntryHeldYawFraction` (held = 1 - weight while it plays; full hold only until the clip starts, <= 0.25 s; a clip gone early hands what is left to the timed ease-out `CoverEntryYawBlendSeconds`). (2) **corner hold with nobody around** - the 2026-10-07 corner hold stepped him into `cvr_*_fire_idle` at every exposed edge, also exploring with no threat (pelvis 90 cm off the wall plane, aiming round the corner). Now `AOperativeCharacter::WantsCornerHold` = the hold spot AND (wave combat / a turn-based fight / a fresh known threat, `CoverFacingRules::WantsCornerHold`), tunable `bCornerHoldOnlyWhenEngaged` (default on; off = the old behaviour); outside a fight the hold ends once no threat is known and no shot for `CoverEngageHoldSeconds`. Exploring at an edge he hugs with `cvr_*_look_at_idle` (the 2026-10-06 clip rule); in a fight the corner hold is unchanged (CornerHold / CornerEntry smokes PASS). (3) **clearance push at a corner** - `UpdateCoverWallClearance` measured every bone against the infinite wall plane: the corner stance's limbs past the edge (no wall there) pushed the whole mesh ~30 cm off the wall. Now only bones within the wall's span count (`CoverFacingRules::IsWithinWallSpan`, margin `CoverWallClearanceCm`; a closed side is unbounded); the no-clipping guarantee inside the span is unchanged (CoverReverseSmoke samples with the same rule). Measured after the fix (male / Female Soldier): middle and corner capsule gap 10 cm, mesh push 0, mesh yaw 0, clip `idle` / `look_at_idle`, shimmies both ways `walk_fwd_loop_*`, no bone in the wall. Tests `CodexTactics.Tactics.Cover.Facing.CornerHoldOnlyWhenEngaged / EntryHeldYawNeverSticks / ClearanceOnlyWithinWallSpan`; smokes `CoverHugSmoke`, `CoverHugMedicSmoke` (`legacy` arg = hold everywhere: FAILS at the corner) registered in `test_map.json` (rule cover). Verified: build, `test.ps1 -Changed` 51 / 51 unit tests, 21 smokes (Cover* / Corner* / CoverHug* / CommanderMode / LeftHandIK / Movement / Stance / SquadControl / ...) PASS - CoverSmoke ("... and back behind the corner", a same-frame bLeaning read) and GroupSelectSmoke failed under parallel load and PASS alone (CoverSmoke 2 / 2 alone); CoverReverseSmoke `corner` / `threat` PASS; Jev audit PASS. No asset changes. |

Not a regression of the horde / repath / knockdown commits — the gap existed since the 2026-10-06 ambush start (the committed L_PatrolTest has no enemies; the user placed them). Fix: `ULevelEncounterSubsystem` records the enemies placed in the map (`IsLevelEnemy` / `GetNumLevelEnemies` / `LevelHasPlacedEnemies`, `MarkLevelEnemy` for smokes); `LevelEncounterRules::IsAmbushStart(Mode, bPatrols, bPlacedEnemies)` (auto = ambush with patrols OR placed enemies — L_MovementTest has none, wave maps unchanged) and new `ShouldStartFightOnContact(bAmbushLevel, bContactWithLevelEnemy, Phase, bUnlocked)`: a contact with a placed enemy starts the ambush fight on any level (also an explicit "button" one); new `EAmbushTrigger::EnemyAttack` from `AEnemyCharacter::NotifySquadAttacked` (melee blow before the hit lands, spitter / drone shot, pounce start, `AMarksmanEnemyCharacter::Fire`); feed «We're under attack! Return fire!»; bot outcome `attacked`. After the fix (same map): the marksman's first shot at 4 s starts WaveCombat / RealTime (ambush fight, 2 level enemies adopted), the squad kills the hound within 2 s. Tests `CodexTactics.Combat.LevelEnemyContactStartsCombat` (new) + `Combat.AmbushStartsCombat`; new `PatrolAmbushSmoke` (L_PatrolTest, `smokeOptions.map`), probe `EncounterProbe` (nonSmokeDebugCommands). | (this, agents/work) | **Stance clips root the body: solo-off followers no longer slide while rising (user bug 2026-10-11).** Repro: formation -> Alt+V (squad prone) -> B (solo: followers crouch, the player may lay them down again) -> the leader scouts -> B: the followers were sent to their slots at once (`UpdateFollower` -> `FollowTo` -> `RequestMove`) while the stand-up clip played, so they slid towards the leader rising. Only `OrderMoveTo` knew about stance clips (and only `IsPlayingStanceTransition` + the sprint-from-prone case: the clip starts on the next anim update, so the first frame after `SetStance` was unguarded). **Rule (pure, `OperativeMovementRules::ComputeMoveHold / ShouldDeferMove`, `MoveHoldThreshold` 0.05 s):** a character never translates while a stance change plays: hold = max(settle time armed by `SetStance` = the ABP clip length from `GetStanceChangeDelay`, not in cover; time left of the playing stance clip). `AOperativeCharacter::GetMoveHoldSeconds()`. **Users of the hold:** `OrderMoveTo` (queues the move on `PendingMoveTimer`, replays after the hold - all player/Ctrl+click/regroup/autonomy (`AutonomousMoveTo`)/tactical-pause plan (`ExecutePlannedOrders`)/interaction/relocation orders go through it; the replay re-arms if the hold is still on), `FollowTo` (refuses while held), `USquadSubsystem::UpdateFollower` (a held follower stops if walking and repaths exactly when the hold ends - no 0.2 s lag), turn-based `UTurnBasedCombatSubsystem` grid walk (`Delay = max(prone rise delay, hold)`). Leader movement unchanged (the leader is moved by the player's `OrderMoveTo`, which already waited). **Stance after solo (unchanged by design):** `ExitSoloMode` sets every follower to the LEADER's current stance (as Godot `can_sync_stance` on solo exit), then the formation lets each keep it (user decision 2026-10-01). So prone followers + standing leader: stand up in place (prone->stand clip ~1.85 s), then walk; crouched followers + standing leader: no crouch->stand clip in the ABP (hold 0), they straighten while walking; crawling (prone) leader: followers stay prone and crawl. New `CodexTactics.SoloRejoinSmoke` (`Debug/SoloRejoinSmokeCommand.cpp`, registered + rule operative-movement-squad): 3 variants (prone/standing, crouched/standing, prone/crawling leader) - solo held positions, then after B the stance, the hold (1.85 s), no slide (< 5 cm) while held, the walk starts after the clip and the followers close >= 150 cm to the leader; verified it FAILS with the gate off. Test `CodexTactics.Squad.StanceMoveHold`. | (this) | **Stretched Marksman (user bug 2026-10-11: elongated body walking and lying dead) - diagnosed, fix script ready, NOT applied yet (the main editor was open).** Root cause: `SK_base_mesh_ue5_1_Skeleton` (the UE5 export under the Marksman's `SKM_base_mesh_2_ue5`) has all 161 bones on the FBX-import default translation retargeting mode **Animation**, so every clip's own bone lengths replace the mesh's. The Marksman plays UE4-mannequin clips (monster pack walk / run / idles / attacks, RifleAnims, Crawl) and, since 4c75003, Manny clips (Sniper death): the UE4 clips carry `spine_02` / `spine_03` ~12 cm while this mesh has them at 0 cm (spine 33 cm -> 62 cm), legs / arms come out 8-18 % long (the Sniper death clips shrink the lower arm 24 %). Measured read-only on the 25 clips of `ABP_Enemy_Marksman` (`AnimPoseExtensions` on the mesh): torso x1.56 / legs x1.18 / arms x1.18 of the mesh's own lengths worst case; with the fix modes applied in memory all 25 clips x1.00. Operatives (80 clips incl. Manny knockdown / cover / Sniper) and Brute / Frostbitten / Cutter measured x1.000: not affected (UE4 skeletons already are Skeleton-mode + pelvis / ik Animation). Fix = `Scripts/Editor/fix_marksman_retargeting.py` (skeleton asset only; backs it up to `Saved/Backups/marksman_retarget_<ts>/`): root / ik_* / interaction / center_of_mass Animation, pelvis AnimationScaled, the other 150 bones Skeleton. Run with the editor closed (commandlet) or via Tools > Execute Python Script in the open editor; `CODEX_RETARGET_DRYRUN=1` measures in memory only. Python note: `Skeleton.bone_tree` is read-only as a whole, assign elements by index (`tree[i] = node`). Details in `skeleton_audit.md` section 6. Still open: re-run `MarksmanSmoke` + `EnemyDeathSmoke` and a rendered still after the apply; the same one-off is needed for any other Manny-exact UE5 export skeleton before a character uses it (BM2 ue5, Explorer) |

| (this) | **Cover bugs (user reports 2026-10-11, male operatives and the Female Soldier alike): wrong shimmy clip after reversing direction + body / rifle in the wall.** (1) **Reverse shimmy:** `AOperativeCharacter::OrderShimmyTo` measured the direction from `CoverSlot`, which is still the slot the RUNNING shimmy started from, not from the body; a reversing order whose target lay on the old side of that slot (he ran 3 m left, is ordered 1 m left of the slot = right of him) got `ShimmyDirection` = left: he walked right in the left clip, with an unknown threat also facing left (no turn). Now `CoverTraceRules::AlongWallDistanceFrom(Slot, ActorLocation, Target)`. Test `Tactics.Cover.ShimmyReverseDirection`; new smoke `CoverReverseSmoke` (also `threat`, `corner`, `crouch`, `medic` args: shimmy A, reverse mid-run toward a target still on A's side of the start slot, reverse from standing, ground order to the other side leaves the cover and turns; the clip names `walk_fwd_loop_L/R` / `walk_bwd_loop_L/R` must follow; fails without the fix). A ground click on the other side was already correct. (2) **Wall clearance:** root cause = the 2026-10-09 cover-entry yaw hold: after a turn > 90 deg the old yaw stayed on the mesh until the enter clip weight reached 0.5 and then dropped in ONE frame (pelvis ~1 m off the capsule pivot: a 79 cm pop; `CoverMoveSmoke` had been failing at 28dd0f7) and the held run pose blended with the enter clip swung hands / the rifle through the wall for ~0.5 s (approach along the wall, skimming it; any mesh). Fixes: the held yaw eases out WITH the enter clip weight (`UpdateCoverEntryBlend`), and a generic `UpdateCoverWallClearance`: while in cover the closest skin bone (no ik_ / VB) is kept `CoverWallClearanceCm` 6 cm outside the wall plane by pushing the mesh out along the wall normal (`CoverWallClearanceRiseCmPerSec` 300, ease-out `CoverWallClearanceInterp` 8; measured after the entry offset so the capsule's jump to the slot is not read as a body in the wall). New smokes `CoverWallSmoke` / `CoverWallMedicSmoke` (six approach angles, per-frame bone-to-wall distance; limit: inside the wall < 0.15 s) and the clearance check inside `CoverReverseSmoke`; `CoverMoveSmoke` back-pop check now `max 20 cm in a frame` + 60 cm total (the arc of the swung pose; was 25 total). `PhoneShots coverreverse` (male + Female Soldier, camera relative to the wall normal) -> `cover_reverse_fixed.mp4`. **Not reproduced headless:** the Female Soldier sinking in a steady cover pose (her bones stay >= 0.9 cm out in every approach after the fix; before it, hands / feet crossed the wall during the entry for male and female alike). Tuning note: a known threat keeps the facing fixed (walking back = the `walk_bwd` clip, no turn) by the 2026-10-06 design rule; only an unknown threat turns him with the move. |

(3) **Kneel rise between shots:** sniper one-shots / kneel clips hand over `SniperHandoffMarginSeconds` 0.05 s before their own blend-out (the FullBody slot dipped and the graph's crouch locomotion, pelvis ~57 cm, showed through; rendered pelvis now locked at 44 cm from the first shot through bolts and the magazine reload). SniperSmoke: TB drift after the shot (0.0 deg), run-to-wall body yaw step, cover entry slot covered within 0.1 s, crouch-then-fire in cover, FullBody slot min 0.99 through shot / bolt / reload. | 680ce8d | **Barrel / barricade orders in the fight (bug + user decision 2026-10-08).** User report: real-time fight, tap Space, order an operative to ignite a barrel or to move it, tap Space: nothing happened. Root causes: (1) `URelocationSubsystem::Tick` dropped every carry while `CanRelocateNow()` was false, i.e. in the real-time wave — so the push planned in the pause was dropped the frame after the release («Боевая тревога! Бросаю…»; barricades moved via their menu the same). Fix: `FRelocateTask::bPlannedInPause` (set by `HandlePauseReleased`), `RelocationRules::ShouldDropActiveTask` (a paused carry keeps running in real time; one started outside the pause is still dropped when live combat begins; turn-based drops all), per-task `DropTask`. (2) A planned Ctrl + click object shot was lost when the shooter could not fire at the release (reload after the auto-fire, misfire, frozen weapon): `AOperativeCharacter::ExecutePlannedTargetedShots` now keeps it (returns bool), `USquadSubsystem::RetryPlannedShots` retries it for `TargetedShotRules::PlannedShotRetrySeconds` (8 s; `GetPlannedShotRetry`: real time retry, pause wait, turn-based / wave end give up; feed «⏳ Оружие не готово…» / «❌ Не могу выстрелить…»). (3) **User decision: «поджечь» = with matches.** Before, a barrel could not be lit in a wave at all (Godot: a click in the pause = relocation, in real time = HQ refusal). Now a plain click on a barrel in the wave (real time or pause) opens its action menu at once (like a barricade in the pause): «Разжечь (1 спичка)», plus «Вытолкать» only where moving is allowed (`ABarrelActor::CanPushNow` asks `URelocationSubsystem::CanRelocateNow`; the pause -> the old placement flow). «Разжечь» in the fight is a use order (`UInteractionSubsystem::OrderUse`, `UseOrderRules`): real time — the operative walks up and lights it on arrival (`ExecuteAction`: a trap is defused first), pause — planned with a marker (replaces his planned move / relocation), run on the release; another move order (`OrderGroupMove`, a relocation, or his move goal changing) cancels it, 30 s timeout with a feed line, turn-based / wave end drop it. Outside the wave the menu works as before (walk up, menu on arrival). Turn-based unchanged. (4) Found by the smoke: the worker vaulted over the barricade he came to push and the push stalled; objects being relocated get the tag `VaultNavigation::RelocatingTag` (not vaultable), and a carried barricade's vault `UNavModifierComponent` no longer blocks the worker's own path (push 28 s -> 9 s). New `OperativeCharacter::GetLastMoveDestination`, `URelocationSubsystem::ClearPlannedTask`, `ExecuteRelocate(..., bPlannedInPause)`. Tests: `CodexTactics.Relocation.PausedCarrySurvivesResume`, `CodexTactics.Combat.TargetedShot.PlannedShotRetry`, `CodexTactics.Interactables.UseOrder.DispatchPerMode / .Steps`; new smoke `PauseBarrelOrderSmoke` (registered in `test_map.json`), `ClickRulesSmoke` updated for the barrel menu. No Godot reference for the fight-time barrel menu (deviation). |

Causes: (1) a target behind him sat at +-180 deg, the sign flipped frame to frame and the clamped AimYawTarget jumped +60 / -60; (2) the yaw reference was the barrel measured on the posed weapon (the AO twist in it) minus a lagging correction - the AO fed back into its own input. Fixes: `AimOffsetRules::YawTargetWithinReach` (beyond clamp + `AimYawReachMarginDegrees` 30 the twist eases to 0, hysteresis 15 deg; test `Anim.AimOffset.YawReachAndWrap`), `BarrelYawOffset` measured with the applied twist taken out (an AO-free reference for the facing and AimYaw; the corner stance keeps its fixed `CornerAimDirection`, crch fire_idle_L measures the same hand line as std), no twist while walking a turn-based grid path. After: walking 0 reversals / 27 deg/s, standing after 0, crouched corner 8 reversals / 43 deg/s, max step 5.2 deg. `Scripts/build.ps1` matches the editor by the full .uproject path (builds in the agents worktree while the user's editor has the main folder open). |

| (uncommitted) | **No tuck-back blip on arrival at a wall edge (user PIE after ba59bb4, 2026-10-07).** New `CodexTactics.CornerEntrySmoke`: every entry path (walk / run / crouched / shimmy to the edge / slot 90 + 170 cm in from the edge / stand <-> crouch at the edge; with and without a firing spitter behind the wall) traced per frame - every FullBody montage + weight, the FullBody slot weight, the cover flags; fails on a tuck-back clip (fire_to_idle, plain cover idle, look-around, the idle -> idle stance switch) after the first fire-stance clip, on a 2nd idle -> fire, on a corner-aim break on the way in, on the slot below 0.9 in the fire stance. Causes found: (1) **montage cross-fades dipped the FullBody slot to 0.44** - `PlaySlotAnimationAsDynamicMontage` stops the old montage with the NEW clip's blend-in (and an ending one-shot auto-blends out over its own 0.1 s) while the new one fades in over 0.2 s: e.g. idle_to_fire w0.12 + fire_idle w0.34 = 0.46 - the graph's plain rifle idle (the body ~70 cm back at the wall, turned) showed through at 55 %: the blip; (2) stand <-> crouch at the edge played the pack's idle -> idle switch clip (tuck back) and re-entered via idle_to_fire; (3) entering 90 cm from the edge the hold began and broke in the same instant for the snap shimmy (a later snap = step out / tuck / step out), and a shot left from the wall during the snap. Fixes (`UOperativeAnimInstance`): `PlayCoverMontage` (no engine group stop) + `FadeOutCoverMontages` (old clips fade over 2 x blend-in + 0.05 s, the slot normalises above 1), hand-off from an ending one-shot before its own blend-out (`CoverHandoffMarginSeconds`), shot clip blend-out 0.05, idle_to_fire blend-out 0.2; in the fire stance a stance switch cross-blends the fire_idle clips (`CoverFireStanceSwitchBlendSeconds` 0.35); `AOperativeCharacter`: corner hold and shots wait while `bCoverSnapPending` / the auto-snap shimmy runs; at the one exposed edge of a high wall the facing is locked to it (CornerHoldSmoke showed a turn to the closed side after an open shot). Result: slot min 1.00 on every path, sequences enter -> idle_to_fire -> fire_idle, stand -> crouch fire_idle_L -> crch_fire_idle_L. |
| (uncommitted) | **Sprint 13: drag & drop hand-over from the inventory drawer (Gemini spec, user «выполняй» 2026-10-07).** The «ПЕРЕД» dialog is retired: `UTransferDialogWidget` deleted (nothing else used it; HUD `ToggleTransferDialog` / `GetTransferDialog` gone, action bar button removed, `HudShot transfer` now shows the drawer + the new split dialog). The drawer (`UInventoryDrawerWidget`) is the single access point: new ammo lines (M16, 9 mm always; 12k / fuel / cryo / plasma only when carried, grid reflows; panel auto-height 540 wide), matches / ammo lines enabled while there is stock (click = info), a hint line. LMB on a line with personal stock is taken in `NativeOnPreviewMouseButtonDown` (`DetectDrag`); a release without a drag clicks the line (`NativeOnMouseButtonUp` -> `Activate`), a drag creates `UInventoryDragDropOperation` (`ETransferItem`, sender = leader, available; visual = purple label «Item xN»). Drop targets: a squad slot of the action bar (`UActionBarWidget::NativeOnDrop` -> `HandleTransferDropOnSlot`; the bar swallows any of our drops) and the world (`UInventoryDrawerWidget::NativeOnDragCancelled` -> `HandleWorldDrop`: absolute -> viewport pixel, `GetHitResultAtScreenPosition` ECC_Visibility; a drop back on the drawer is ignored). Both go to `ACodexTacticsHUD::HandleTransferDropOnActor(Sender, Item, Actor, Point)`: real-time order lock respected, recipient = hit operative (or owner) / nearest living mate within 2.2 m of the point, max = min(stock, recipient capacity); `TransferRules::NeedsQuantityDialog` -> `UQuantitySplitDialogWidget` (C++-built: slider + [-] [+] by the step, «ВСЁ», «ПОДТВЕРДИТЬ», «ОТМЕНА»; Esc = cancel, first in `HandleEscape`; starts at the whole stack; shows max and step), else instant. Rules (`TransferRules`): `GetItemQuantityStep` ammo 5 / items 1, `GetMinQuantity` (a remainder below 5 = the whole remainder), `QuantizeQuantity` / `StepQuantity` (the whole stack allowed off the grid), `GetRecipientCapacity` (deployables up to the carry max, others unlimited), `GetMaxTransferQuantity`, `IsWithinTransferRange` / `CanTransferTo` (horizontal <= 200 cm), `DecideRange` + `FTransferRangeContext`, `TransferQuantity` (exact quantity, partial on capacity: `Moved`, `bClampedByCapacity`). `USquadTransferSubsystem`: `ResolveDropRecipient`, `BuildRangeContext`, `RequestTransfer` (in range -> `ExecuteTransferQuantity`; far + free -> move order to 150 cm short of the recipient, hand-over on arrival; far + blocked -> feed «Слишком далеко для передачи (макс. 2 метра)»), `ExecuteTransferQuantity` (feed «Передал(а) +15 Патроны M16 бойцу …», «нет места», «(больше не поместилось)»; no event-bus event — the old transfer had none). **Blocked definition (decision):** a wave fight (`ECodexGamePhase::WaveCombat`, real time or turn-based), turn-based active, a living enemy whose current target is the sender, or the sender dead / raging / no path (the move order not accepted). In range the hand-over is allowed in every mode, also in combat, and costs no AP (as before). Pending approach (timer 0.2 s, world time): hand-over once within 2 m (sender stopped), re-aim when the recipient walks off > 1 m, cancelled (feed «Передача отменена.») when the sender follows another path (another order), stops short, either dies, or after 45 s; a new request replaces it. The legacy click mode (ring cursor, `StartTransferMode` / `HandleClick`) stays in the subsystem but nothing starts it. Tests `CodexTactics.Transfer.QuantityStep.AmmoQuantizedByFive / .AmmoRemainderBelowFive / .MedkitsQuantizedByOne`, `.DistanceCheck.RejectsBeyondTwoMeters` (also the range decisions incl. turn-based / under fire / immobile), `.PartialStackTransferDeductsCorrectly`, `.CapacityClamp` (in `TransferRulesTest.cpp`). `CodexTactics.TransferSmoke` rewritten for the drop flow (handlers called directly). IWYU fixes found by the non-unity compile: `GameFramework/Actor.h` in `SquadTransferSubsystem.h`, AnimSequence / SkeletalMeshComponent in `HudShotCommand.cpp`. **Fixes after the first host run:** a move order given in the same frame as the request was not seen as «another order» (the path baseline was captured only by the first timer tick, and during the 1 s grace the re-aim at the walking recipient overrode the player's order) — the baseline is now captured in `IssueApproach`, re-aim only on a confirmed own path; the smoke read the feed before the drop (C++ argument order) — outcome and feed are read in order and the exact texts asserted. **Additions (user, same sprint): drop to the ground and two-way crates.** Shared container `UItemStashComponent` (`Interactables/ItemStashComponent`: ETransferItem -> units, `Capacity` in units over all items, 0 = unlimited, `Add` / `Take` / `GetFreeSpace` / `SetContents`, `OnChanged`, revision; `ItemStash::ToLootItem / FromLootItem`). `ADroppedItemActor` (pile: small coloured box — ammo yellow, medicine red, food brown, engineering grey, matches orange — overhead label «Патроны M16 x15», ground trace + navmesh projection, visibility-only collision, no nav / pawn blocking; `SpawnOrMerge` merges drops within 60 cm; empty -> destroyed; click = the usual interaction walk-up (planned in the tactical pause), arrival -> `PickUpAll` with capacity clamp, leftover stays; engineering items lie as items, never armed). `ALootCrateActor` now stores its items in a `Stash` (Capacity 500): the authored `FLootContents` counts move in lazily (`SyncContentsIntoStash`, so code that fills `Contents` keeps working; bonus weapon / clothing stay in Contents), `IsLooted()` = nothing inside (derived, `bLooted` removed), a crate with stored items opens again; the loot-window click still takes the whole stack without the carry limit (Godot rule), drag-takes clamp. Generic request `FTransferRequest` (Give / DropToGround / Store / Take; `USquadTransferSubsystem::Request / Execute / GetMaxQuantity / PickUpAll`), same 2 m rule (to a crate: to its box) and walk-over / cancel logic for all four; `TransferRules::RemoveFromOperative / AddToOperative / StoreInStash / TakeFromStash`. Drop routing (`ACodexTacticsHUD::HandleTransferDropOnActor`): crate -> store, pile -> add to it, a mate's model / portrait or within 80 cm of him (was 2.2 m) -> give, anything else -> the ground; `HandleTakeDrop`, `HandleTransferRequest`, `HandleDragReleasedOverWorld` (shared world trace). Loot window: storable lines draggable (take-out onto a model / portrait / the open drawer, split dialog, capacity clamp), an inventory line dropped on the window stores it, the list refreshes on stash changes. Split dialog titles per action (`OpenForRequest`). **Decisions:** a ground drop out of reach while blocked (combat / under fire / cannot walk) goes down at the operative's feet (`DroppedAtFeet`) — getting rid of a load must always work; a store / take out of reach while blocked is refused with «Слишком далеко…». No AP cost for any of it in turn-based (the existing pickups / loot had none). Feed: «Выбросил(а) на землю: …», «Подобрал(а): …», «📦 Положил(а) в ящик: …», «📦 Взял(а) из ящика: …», «В ящике нет места», «не поместилось». **Save / load:** each crate saves `stash` (exact contents; older saves keep the authored loot), world_state `dropped_items` (location + items); load clears the session's piles and respawns the saved ones. Tests (+3): `CodexTactics.Transfer.Stash.CapacityAndTake / .StoreAndTakePartial`, `.DropToGround.RemovesExactQuantity`. TransferSmoke extended: 30 rounds dropped near -> dialog, 15 on the ground (pile label), the engineer picks them up (pile gone), 2 mines -> the nearly full engineer takes 1 (1 stays), save / load restores the pile, a far medkit drop walks then drops, far under fire -> at his feet; crate: store 10 (dialog) -> crate +10, the engineer takes 5 -> crate 5, room for 3 -> 3 stored, then «нет места». Verified: build OK, 268 / 268 tests, `test.ps1 -Smart` OK, smokes TransferSmoke / InventorySmoke / ActionBarSmoke / LootSmoke / SaveLoadSmoke / SquadControlSmoke / EventBusSmoke / RecruitDeathSmoke PASS. Not verified: a real mouse drag in PIE, clicking a pile in turn-based mode (same path as clicking a crate). |

CoverSmoke / CoverCrouchSmoke run the legacy mode (`bCornerHoldAtEdge` off, `bCornerAutoDuck` on). Fix: `FCornerAimSituation` pressure / flank / damage from RANGED enemies only (spitters, cryo drones, marksmen; `RecentRangedDamage`), the wound / fire ducks only while a ranged threat is present (`bRangedThreatPresent`), `DecideCornerAim(..., &Reason)`; while leaned out, a target beyond the facing edge stays a corner-shot target at any depth (`CoverFacingRules::IsBeyondFacingEdge`; no step off the wall per rusher); a melee mutant that reaches his side of the wall gets the open shot (turn and fire), not a duck. Jev re-validated with 7 horde scenarios: 25 / 31 = 81 % (`jev_validate_corner_aim.py`, new option turn_shoot; the remaining disagreement h_melee_horde_wounded at Jev 0.39 kept: the corner does not stop a horde). Trace: `[CornerAim]` log on every decision / reason change (decision, reason, legacy decision + reason, HP, ranged / all damage, pressure with ranged / all aimers, melee within 5 m, laser, grenade distance, open-side enemy distance, seconds without a target, clip); `Codex.Debug.CornerAim 1` shows it over the operative. Test `Tactics.Cover.CornerHoldVsMeleeHorde`; new `CodexTactics.CornerHoldSmoke` (10 hounds round the corner: no duck for safety, >= 8 corner shots in a row; then frostbitten + spitters: ducks only for ranged reasons). Second cause found by the first run: `DuckForSafety: grenade near` at `grenade 0` — the squad's OWN grenade (in the thrower's hand before the release, then flying at the horde) counted; only enemy grenades count now (`AGrenadeActor::GetThrower`: every AGrenadeActor is an operative's today), a landed friendly one only with `bCornerAimDuckForFriendlyGrenades` (default off). CornerHoldSmoke PASS: horde 13 corner shots in a row, 0 ducks for safety (the legacy column shows "DuckForSafety: heavy ranged fire" with 2 melee aimers); mixed group 2 ducks for 2 ranged aimers (press 0.70). Only a duck for safety ends the counted run (a return between rushers does not). |

(2) Wall edges bisected: `CoverTraceRules::RefineEdgeDistance` (4 steps inside the 40 cm bracket, `FCoverTraceConfig::EdgeRefineIterations`), Left/RightEdgeDistanceCm are the real edge (smoke: measured 48.8 vs real 50.0 cm) so the corner snap stands the clip side's stand-off from the TRUE edge (left corner: 29 cm for 28); `OrderShimmyTo` "already there" 30 -> 10 cm so short snaps run. (3) `IsCoverFireReady`: the sustained aim, else the fire-ready corner pose only for a threat behind the wall / round the corner (`IsCornerShotTarget(CoverThreatLocation)`), never while reloading / ducked / within the hold after an aim ended; a threat in front keeps the plain cover pose. | 6de5bd7 | **Fix (user report 2026-10-06): hidden enemies / silhouettes slid in the T-pose.** Root cause: `UTacticalSightSubsystem` hides unseen enemies with `SetActorHiddenInGame`; an unrendered skinned mesh refreshes its bones only with `AlwaysTickPoseAndRefreshBones` (`USkinnedMeshComponent::ShouldUpdateTransform`; ACharacter's default is `AlwaysTickPose`), so a hidden enemy kept a stale pose — the reference (T) pose if it was never on screen — and `AEnemyGhostActor` copied it (a heard silhouette was even rebuilt from it every 0.2 s, hence the sliding T-pose). Fix: `UTacticalSightSubsystem::KeepPoseWhileHidden` — while hidden the enemy's (and attached actors') skinned meshes get `AlwaysTickPoseAndRefreshBones`, the authored option is saved and restored when it is seen again (rendered then; off-screen enemies keep the cheaper option). `AEnemyGhostActor`: a heard silhouette ticks (TG_PostUpdateWork) — follows the enemy and copies its live pose every frame (`SyncFromSource`, the poseable copies are reused instead of rebuilt), an unheard one freezes; a new silhouette re-copies the pose on its first 2 frames (bones valid from the frame after the hide). Test `Combat.Sight.HiddenEnemiesKeepAnimating`; SightSmoke +4 checks (option while hidden / restored, heard silhouette ticks, unheard frozen) |
